# Changelog

All notable changes to **SocialCentral** are documented in this file.
Versioning follows Semantic Versioning (`MAJOR.MINOR.PATCH`). Ongoing development changes stay under `[Unreleased]` without altering the published version number until an explicit public GitHub release is requested (`PATCH`: `0.X.Y` -> `0.X.(Y+1)`).

## [Unreleased]

## [0.2.33] - 2026-11-28
### Added
- **Early Dungeon/Raid Leaver Verification & Dedicated Avoid List (`Providers/Group.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added accurate instance-completion tracking in `Group` across Classic and Forever `16001` dungeons (including multi-wing SM, Dire Maul, Stratholme, LBRS/UBRS, and Forever custom dungeons), Classic raids, and dynamic saved-lockout raids (`ENCOUNTER_END`, `BOSS_KILL`, `LFG_COMPLETION_REWARD`, `UPDATE_INSTANCE_INFO`).
  - Verified voluntary early leavers who drop out of a started dungeon or raid before either the final boss or all required bosses are killed (ignoring kicks via `ERR_UNINVITE_S`, group disbands via `ERR_GROUP_DISBANDED`, disconnects, unconfirmed aborts when leaving or finalizing, and quick ~2.5s rejoins).
  - Added 1-click **Add to Avoid List** prompts on interactive Toast cards and clickable chat links (plus opt-in auto-Avoid mode in Settings), **Early Leavers** quick-flag actions & completion badges in **Dungeon & Raid Log**, non-toggling `Store:FlagPlayerAvoid(...)` note preservation, and an **Avoid List** filter chip + **Add to Avoid** modal inside `Social` (`O`).

### Changed
- **Unified Player & Guild Management Across Avoid, Favorites, Watchlist, Guild, Allies & Enemies (`Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/LootRollFrame.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added direct 1-click `[Edit]` and `[X]` remove/unblock row actions inside **Social -> Avoid List** and **Account Ignores**, plus synchronized removal when unflagging players who are also Account Ignored, an edit-in-place mode with `Remove` + `"Also hide official chat across account"` inside **Add/Edit Avoid**, and a **Recent** player quick-picker inside **Account Ignore** (and expanded **Add/Edit Avoid** recent candidates with nearby players).
  - Added 1-click **Favorite**, **Good Partner (+)**, **Avoid**, and **Watchlist toggle** buttons in Social expanded contact cards, an interactive `+` quick-add menu (Right-Click anywhere on Social `+`; Left-Click Favorite picker on Favorites view; online quick picker on Watchlist `+ Add`), clickable **Remove from Watchlist** toggles on Nearby and Quick Reply, and full **Favorite / Avoid / Account Ignore / Watchlist / Guild Diplomacy** actions across unit context menus (incl. Target/Focus/Enemy Player frames), Target Radar HUD + drawer (`+ Avoid` on Enemies, `Manage Block` on Blocked targets, `- Watchlist` on Watched), Group/Recent Companions (with Favorites & Avoid filters), LFG rows, Guild Roster, Hub Chat right-click, Quick Reply sender menus, and Loot Roll player menus.
  - Enhanced Guild & Diplomacy management with `Favorites` / `Good Partner (+)` / `Avoid List` Guild Roster filters, direct Watchlist/Favorite/Alliance Chat whitelist/Manager toggles on Guild Roster row menus, live search + Alliance shortcut + Ally/Enemy counters on the Diplomacy toolbar, `+ Recent` guild picker & direct `Remove` inside the Diplomacy modal, and `/sc avoid`, `/sc fav`, `/sc diplomacy <guild>` slash commands with updated trilingual guides.
- **External Whisper (`SendTell` / `SendMessage`) Routing While `SC Chat` Is Open (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`):**
  - Fixed Blizzard's "Send Message" / `SendTell` / `SendBNetTell` actions (such as right-clicking group leaders in Blizzard's `LFGBrowseFrame`) silently failing or getting trapped behind the docked `Geral` channel when `SC Chat` is open in Addon Chat mode, by prioritizing external whisper/BNet-tell routing before docked official-channel guards, preserving two-word WoW Forever character names (including accented UTF-8 names), cleanly closing Blizzard's hidden editbox state, and focusing the new conversation tab in `SC Chat` (or the Chat Hub if floating chat is disabled).
- **Whisper Level, Zone & Guild Discovery (`Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added automatic passive Level, Class, Guild, and Zone resolution from cached official and addon LFG listings (`C_LFGList` / `Clan.officialLfgCache`), live target/focus/mouseover unit enrichment, incoming `DPING`/`DPONG` handshakes, and full `WHO_LIST_UPDATE` metadata capture whenever manual `/who` or native Shift+Click queries run.
  - Added a 1-click `Lv.?` header check button on whisper tabs with unknown levels in both `SC Chat` and Hub `Conversations`, added `"Check Level & Zone (/who)"` to player/thread menus, upgraded unfocused chat `Shift+Click` (including native `player:` links inside General/Group/Guild streams) to run silent hardware-event `/who` lookups with clear inline feedback and synced trilingual `[?]` help guides.
  - Propagated `DPING`/`DPONG` peer level/class/zone handshakes and cached LFG/unit observations directly to non-Watchlist stranger whisper headers & quick menus, widened responsive trilingual controls across Battle.net Watchlist Picker, Guild sidebar, LFG Browse Category, Loot Roll Confirm BoP, and Minimap Chooser, added deterministic comparator tie-breakers, fixed `Spotter:TrimRuntimeCaches` namespace routing, connected `FlightTimes` live palette updates, exported `QuickBar:GetSelectedIdsCached`, and eliminated `Social:Scan` CPU spikes (`Compat` shard-label memoization + `2000`-slot parse cache + `O(1)` indexed linked-alt Watchlist resolution).
- **Clean Official Loot Roll Bar Removal & First-Roll Prompt (`UI/LootRollFrame.lua`, `Providers/LootRoll.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Replaced the `SetScale(0.001)` + `SetAlpha(0)` + `GroupLootContainer_Update` suppression hack (which could taint and freeze Blizzard's official `GroupLootFrame` bars on screen) with clean `START_LOOT_ROLL` listener unregistration (`UIParent` and non-protected Blizzard frames out of combat) plus safe `Hide()` cleanup for already-open frames (restoring `START_LOOT_ROLL` cleanly when disabled without resurrecting stale expired roll bars).
  - Streamlined the first-roll activation prompt (`SocialCentralLootRollPrompt`) so players choose once on their first drop between replacing WoW's official roll bar with SocialCentral or keeping Blizzard's official bar, with synced `[?]` guidance across English, Portuguese, and Spanish.
- **Deep Memory Growth Elimination & Runtime Pooling:**
  - Added out-of-combat incremental memory cleanup across zone transitions, post-combat recovery, and quiet idle windows, replacing grow-only cache clears with shrinkable structures across core lookup, unit observation, combat/world logs, radar, LFG, and alt/recipe indexes.
  - Replaced repeated per-message chat filter/deduplication/tracking table and debug-stack allocations with circular ring buffers and shared sentinels, lowered the in-memory text quota cap from `100 MB` to `6 MB` while keeping saved notes and active conversations intact, and dehydrated inactive whisper threads in memory until reopened.
  - Pooled chat stream viewport item/line records, badge cluster layouts, notification preview slots, and combat meter scratch tables, added sliding-window viewport trimming to eliminate full-history rebuild cliffs at 210/480 lines, and detached stream drag-scroll frame listeners whenever idle.

## [0.2.32] - 2026-11-27
### Changed
- **Chat Pipeline Performance & Stability Overhaul (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`):**
  - Replaced linear `O(N)` viewport visible-bounds scans with `O(log N + K)` binary search inside `CreateChatStreamViewport`, added shadow color/offset equality guards to stop pooling FontStrings on unchanged refreshes, and hoisted/cached chat bar RGB tables (`Theme.CHAT_SELF_BAR_COLOR`, `Theme:GetClassBarColor`) to eliminate hundreds of temporary per-line table allocations.
  - Eliminated hidden-window `QuickReply` keybinding/hotkey scans on incoming chat events, deduplicated `GetQuickReplyThreads()` calls between the header bar and tab strip, hoisted the chip pagination allocator helper, dirty-checked header/input-prefix/mute/status-bar/pinned-banner/send-button widgets, and capped incremental append drift at 210/480 items so long Trade/Raid sessions never degrade scroll performance or leak memory.
  - Deduplicated multi-window `OfficialChatShieldFilter` formatting work (URL linking, raid icons, mention highlighting) per `lineID`, hoisted loop-invariant checks out of `GetThreadsList`, cached first-name mention lookups in `AppendMessage`, coalesced `BN_FRIEND_*` event bursts, reused badge/unread scratch tables without double `GetThreadsList` scans in the Conversations Hub, added per-thread live search caching, and hoisted `NotificationToast` read-state predicates out of `CHAT_UPDATED`.
- **Dark Proximity Grouped Chat Feed (`SC Chat` & `Conversations`):** Implemented Discord/Chattynator-style dark proximity grouping directly on the active theme palette without boxes or light backgrounds: consecutive messages from the same author within a continuous 3-minute gap (5-minute max cluster) group tightly (`1px` line gap) with full-width continuation indent (`+7px`), a subtle `2px` vertical left connector bar in muted class/self tone, compact `4px` spacing between speaker turns, dark hover timestamp badge on continuation lines, right-click actions anywhere on a line, and full sticky-header + idle transparency support.
- **Group Loot Roll Choice Bar & Non-Overlapping Tooltips:** Moved the roll choice bar (`Need`, `Greed`, `Disenchant`, `Pass`, inline BoP confirmation, and roll status banner) to the top of the card body above item details and stat comparisons, anchored choice tooltips cleanly outside the roll window so stats and party votes stay visible while hovering, and replaced the broken Unicode collapse triangle next to Hide/Show votes with crisp native arrow textures (updating the Chromie `?` tour steps in EN/PT/ES).

## [0.2.31] - 2026-11-27
- **SC Chat (`Quick Reply`) & Conversations Hub Overhaul (`UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - **Same-Minute Timestamp Deduplication & Vertical Text Alignment:** Hid repeated `[HH:MM]` timestamps on consecutive messages from the same player within the same minute (displaying `[HH:MM]` again only when the minute changes, while preserving exact-time hover tooltips and message actions on `·`) and vertically aligned subsequent grouped lines to start at the exact text column of the speaker's first message across both **SC Chat** and **Conversations**.
  - **Smart Group Session Continuity & Consolidation:** Prevented spurious new group headers after `/reload`, relogs, instance transitions, or rapid member joins/swaps by matching live teammates against the latest group session and visually merging overlapping consecutive sessions in **Party**, **Raid**, and **Instance** tabs.
  - **Clickable Sticky Section Headers:** Added a slim clickable sticky header bar at the top of the message viewport in **SC Chat** and **Conversations** that tracks the active Group or Day/Time block as you scroll, dims its backdrop smoothly during idle transparency while keeping text sharp, displays full class/role roster tooltips on hover and whisper/invite actions on right-click, and jumps cleanly to the start of the block (exposing the tail of the prior section above) on left-click.
  - **In-Chat Search, Scrollbar, Font Size & Composer Polish:** Added inline message search, vertical scrollbar thumb + `v Latest` pill, per-window **Font Size** presets (`Default` / `11` / `13` / `14` / `16`), `+` New Chat shortcut, unread-other-tabs indicator, `General` `/s`/`/y`/channel selector & filter pills, **Show Quick Replies Bar** toggle (Settings & Conversation Options), unified sent-input history, idle foreground contrast handling, and synced `[?]` guides across English, Portuguese, and Spanish.

## [0.2.30] - 2026-11-27
- **Top Flight Countdown Bar (`Providers/FlightTimes.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added a live draggable countdown bar at the top of the screen during Flight Master travel displaying destination, route, remaining arrival time, progress bar, `[?]` guide, `[X]` per-flight dismiss, `/reload` recovery, and safety guards against recording interrupted flights (`Death`/`AcceptProposal`/instance transitions).
- **General Tab Notification Filters (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added a `Show Notifications` filter submenu in the `General` (`ch:general`) right-click menu across Quick Reply and Conversations so players can selectively hide or show Loot, Achievements, World/Bosses/NPCs, System/Notices, Public Channels (`/1`, `/2`...), and Say/Yell/Emotes live without losing non-filtered chat.
- **All-in-One Screenshot Demo Showcase (`/sc demo` & `/sc demo clear`) (`Providers/DemoShowcase.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `SocialCentral.toc`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added `/sc demo` to populate clean in-memory hypothetical showcase data across every window and widget in a single command (Social, Watchlist, Nearby, Target Radar HUD, Guild Hub/Events/Diplomacy/Professions, LFG Browse, Group/Synergy/Dungeon History, Account Alts & World Buffs, Conversations/Quick Reply/Mentions, Minimap Previews & Fusion Badge, Notification Toasts, QuickBar, and Group Loot Rolls + `/roll`), plus `/sc demo clear` (and automatic `PLAYER_LOGOUT` cleanup) to wipe 100% of demo data without altering real saved records.

## [0.2.29] - 2026-11-27
- **Message Button & Reply Focus Consistency Audit (`UI/LootRollFrame.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`):**
  - Fixed broken `Ask about item (/w)` menu actions in both Group Loot Roll quick cards and Roll Results so they properly open conversations pre-filled and ready to type via the player's active chat mode instead of failing silently.
  - Preserved keyboard focus across external Official Whisper / Share Draft buttons while Quick Reply is visible by preventing the button's mouse-up event from cancelling the newly opened chat input box.
  - Aligned **Quick Reply in Chat** (`Shift+Click` and right-click menu on Minimap Message Previews), Focus Summary notifications, New Chat modal starts, and clickable sender replies inside Conversations & Mentions so explicit reply actions open focused and ready to type immediately.
- **Social -> Quick Reply Focus Preservation (`UI/Minimap.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`):**
  - Clicking "Chat in SocialCentral" (`O`) or opening a direct conversation now keeps keyboard focus armed in the message input box immediately on click instead of losing focus on the button's mouse-up event.
- **Chat Window & Quick Reply Memory Optimization (`UI/Minimap.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):**
  - Eliminated continuous background RAM growth while the Quick Reply / Chat window stays open by fixing the broken unfocused focus-border cache guard, memoizing docked official chat input styling/skin colors and header buttons, scoping Quick Reply refreshes strictly to relevant events instead of rebuilding on every nearby/watchlist/social update, caching official channel lookups, pinned-thread order, and portal quick-reply lists, pooling general chat capture batches, replacing repeated `GetThreadsList` scans on the Whispers button with an O(N) zero-allocation unread counter, and hoisting hot-path Blizzard theme color tables.

## [0.2.28] - 2026-11-27
### Major Feature Overhauls & Audits
- **Group Loot Roll & Raid Leader `/roll (1-100)` Overhaul (`Providers/LootRoll.lua`, `UI/LootRollFrame.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/QuickBar.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - **Unified Active Roll Card Redesign:** Rebuilt the active Group Loot Roll card into a single cohesive `380px` obsidian-bronze card with a rarity-colored top accent bar, soft ambient quality wash behind a `44x44` hero icon, crisp `BoP`/`BoE` pill badge, edge-to-edge glowing countdown bar, clean minimalist header icons (Dice Results shortcut, geometric Tabbed/Stacked layout toggle, Padlock, Chromie `[?]`, Close — zero clipped labels), and refined multi-drop item tabs with quality underlines, status dots, and pending count.
  - **2-Column Stat Diff Grid, Usability Matrix & Dual-Slot Swap:** Replaced flat wrapped comparison text with a clean dynamic-height **2-column stat diff grid** (gains sorted first, never duplicating iLvl), color-coded Verdict Pills (`+iLvl`, `Empty`, `Cannot Equip`, `Wrong Armor`, `Unique`, `Can Learn`, `Known`, `Alt Needs`), Classic/Forever class armor/weapon/relic usability detection (including Lv 40 Mail/Plate unlock, Unique-owned checks, smallest bag comparison, and weapon DPS), and a compact `1/2` / `2/2` slot swap button for Rings, Trinkets, and Dual-Wield/Off-Hand slots.
  - **Semantic Action Bar, BoP Safety, `/reload` Recovery & Stacked Multi-Drop Mode:** Styled **Need** (emerald), **Greed** (gold), **Disenchant** (arcane violet), and **Pass** (muted crimson) cleanly across `dark`, `native`, and `blizzard` themes; added inline **Confirm BoP / Cancel** state machine (`CONFIRM_LOOT_ROLL`/`CONFIRM_DISENCHANT_ROLL`) so canceling never locks buttons; added automatic `/reload` active roll recovery (`GetActiveLootRollIDs`); added **Stacked Multi-Drop Cards** mode (up to 4 drops simultaneously); and integrated the collapsible **Live Party Votes** strip (icon tallies, waiting-player detection, tie badges, right-click player whisper menu, and manual **Greed Unusable** / **Pass Remaining** batch footer for 2+ unvoted drops).
  - **Resizable Roll Results, Run Luck Analytics, Boss/Master Loot & Raid Leader `/roll` Console:** Upgraded the Roll Results window with resizing, **Rarity** (`All`/`Uncommon+`/`Rare+`/`Epic+`) & **Sort** (`Newest`/`Rarity`/`Highest Roll`/`Winner`) filters, **Boss** & **Master Loot (`[ML]`)** drop attribution, **Run Luck Analytics** (Win %, Average/Best Roll, per-player loot distribution), right-click drop/player menus, and a full **Raid Leader `/roll (1-100)` Console** with automatic MS (`1–100`) / OS (`1–50`, `1–99`) brackets, fixed multi-way tie highlighting, 1-click **Roll 1–100**, **Open Roll**, automatic **Tie-Breaker** rounds restricted to tied contenders, **Announce Winner**, and `/reload` persistence.
  - **Settings, QuickBar & Minimap Parity:** Added dedicated **Group Loot Roll & /roll** section in **Settings -> General & Tools** (Tabbed/Stacked layout, hover comparison tooltips, active card lock, auto-hide floating dice button with QuickBar), fixed immediate Settings event sync in Minimap menus, added unread badge & Shift+Click active card shortcut on QuickBar's dice icon, and made `/sc roll` open Roll Results directly (`/sc roll preview` for simulated preview).
- **Social & Friends Directory Transformation & Smarter Watchlist (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Replaced the overflowing Battle.net friends dropdown with a dedicated searchable, filterable **Battle.net Friend Picker** modal (`WoW`, `Online`, `Offline`, `All`, instant `+ Follow`/`Following` toggles) and added a batch **Social Folder Manager** modal for organizing friends cleanly into custom folders.
  - Improved **Watchlist** passive status verification to automatically detect stale/offline characters from guild, friends, Battle.net, and group/whisper activity without requiring manual `/who` clicks.
- **Flight Map Travel Duration Tooltips (`Providers/FlightTimes.lua`, `SocialCentral.toc`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added verified Classic/Forever flight travel durations directly inside Blizzard's Taxi/Flight Map tooltips alongside destination name and cost, automatically learning and refining exact flight times as you fly.
- **Official Chat Focus & Send Parity (`UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):**
  - Clicking chat tabs no longer steals keyboard focus automatically (typing activates strictly on `ENTER` or clicking the input field), and sending a message cleanly releases input focus matching Blizzard's official chat behavior.
- **Main Menu, Settings & Alts (`C`) Comprehensive Audit (`UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Providers/Alts.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Audited and refined Settings pages, search index, section jump targets, Main Window tabs, and the Character (`C`) / Alts Dossier, bags/bank, professions, and cross-world views for layout integrity, theme consistency, and trilingual help parity.

### Messaging, Notifications & Privacy Audit
- **Messaging & Notifications Deep Pass 3 (`UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Group.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Fixed **Minimap Message Previews** coalescing so consecutive messages from the same sender refresh the displayed snippet immediately instead of staying frozen on the 1st message text; enabled instant live preview re-rendering when toggling Streamer Mode; fixed `MsgPreview` first-time prompt combat-silence default checkbox, hidden-on-create guard, and `[?]` theme refresh.
  - Restored **Mention Radar**, **Private Room**, **Alliance**, and direct `[SC]` chimes when using **Official WoW Chat Mode**; prevented `OpenPreviewOrLatest` and the Minimap menu's `Open Quick Reply` shortcut from opening Quick Reply in Official Chat Mode; enabled clickable URLs and `{rt1..rt8}` icons on outgoing official whispers (`CHAT_MSG_WHISPER_INFORM`); gated `CHAT_MSG_COMMUNITIES_CHANNEL` under `mentionWatchPublic`.
  - Separated **LFG Watcher** toasts from Guild (`J`) / Guild Dungeon alerts so guildless players receive watched dungeon popups and clicking Bell-Off silences `lfgWatchersEnabled` cleanly (with full visibility & 1-click restoration in **Settings -> Notifications**); restored opted-in per-guildmate login/logout alerts when general Friend Online mode is `Off`; fixed recent stranger whisperers falsely showing as Offline across **Quick Reply** and **Conversations**.
  - Enforced **Focus Mode** silencing on Guild LFG, LFG Watcher, Guild Event, Signup, Scout, and Event Reminder chat prints/badge counters, fixed Scout toast `senderClass` to color the guildmate scout (plus `15s` anti-spam dedup), enforced `>= 3.0s` audio cooldowns on Group Avoid & official Invite/Trade/Duel warning sounds, fixed `closeBtn` hit-rect overlap on Toasts, auto-pruned queued read toasts and stale post-combat World Buff countdowns, cleared preview/guild badges on **Mark All Conversations Read**, and closed every remaining **Streamer Mode** leak (`ROOM` titles, `RMI` invite bodies, muted-thread expiry prints, Official `[SC]` echoes, Filtered Log copy/unblock, Copy Chat, Clear Chat, Mute/Leave menus, Pin/Copy Message, and Battle.net Redirect labels).
  - Polished modal theming & focus across **Quick Replies & Shortcuts**, **Combat/World Log**, **Create/Invite/Delete Room**, **Chat Mode Prompt**, and **Left-Click Chooser** (exempting `_configModal` from Quick Reply outside-click auto-close), added hover tooltips for `addon:SCentral:whisper:` and `delnote:` links, aligned `chatMirrorChannels`/`clanLfgAlertEnabled` settings getters with their defaults, and added deterministic tie-breakers in chat sort comparators.
- **Messaging & Notifications Deep Pass 2 (`UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Closed every remaining **Streamer Mode** leak across Toast pagination button tooltips, Toast & Preview right-click menus and mute buttons, Minimap unread sender tooltips, Quick Reply tab chips/menus/close-confirm dialogs, Room Invite banners, and Manage Conversations confirmations, and added Streamer Mode & Clickable URLs into `QuickReply` and `Conversations` message-feed rebuild signatures so toggling privacy masks visible chats instantaneously.
  - Upgraded **Quick Reply** with a dynamic character counter (`180` vs `255`), full `[Channel]` + `[Keyword]` badges and a 1-click **Keywords** button/right-click menu on the Mentions tab, live `[sent]`/`[ok]`/`[off]` delivery confirmation ticks on outgoing `[SC]` messages, fixed `Compat.AddonIconMarkup(true, 12)` argument order, increased `msgFrame:SetMaxLines(260)` so dividers never evict older lines, dynamic transparency delay in Chromie's tour badge, and proper `[?]` theme preservation (`btnHelp:RefreshTheme()`).
  - Enforced strict audio-visual atomicity and `>= 3.0s` sound cooldowns across Watchlist Online, Friend Online, Clan/Event/LFG/Scout, and Mention Radar alerts; fixed `TriggerTestMention` to preview the true Mention chime without polluting Recent Notifications; kept `MENTION` toasts separate from regular channel coalescing; fixed `navBar`-mode top button overlap on `titleRule` and unblocked countdowns on explicitly visible combat toasts (`AVOID_ALERT` / combat-silencing disabled); unified full 7-option timed **Do Not Disturb** menus on both Toast and Preview mute popups; enriched chat player-name and clickable-URL hover tooltips (`UI/Theme.lua`); removed broken Unicode `★` square glyphs from Settings (`Allow Favorites`), synced stale `[?]` toast/Streamer Mode descriptions, fixed Minimap tooltip `"Classic Gold"` theme detection, and wired missing `SETTINGS_UPDATED`/`CHAT_UPDATED` notifications across Settings & Minimap toggles.
- **Floating Notification Toasts & Providers (`UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Group.lua`):**
  - Fixed a silent argument-offset bug when muting Battle.net-only friends directly from a toast (and immediately pruned queued/active toasts from that muted sender), restored Class Color & Class Crest rendering on Mention Radar and Private Room Invite toasts (`senderClass`), preserved priority `minDuration` timers on Avoid alerts (`12s`) and Event reminders (`16s`), cleaned raw WoW hyperlink codes before emitting toast text, updated toast `[?]` duration hints dynamically, upgraded hover tooltips with class-colored sender headers and full cleaned body text (respecting Streamer Mode), organized right-click menus cleanly with dividers (hiding the non-applicable mute action on Focus Summary), expanded settings/instance auto-pruning across all notification toggles, and enforced audio-visual atomicity + Streamer Mode BattleTag masking on Friend Online/Offline alerts.
- **Minimap Previews, Recent Notifications & Quick Reply (`UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`):**
  - Straightened `MsgPreview`'s header divider cleanly and expanded the preview text clip area (`+32px`) without colliding with top-right buttons, added thread-specific `Silence Notifications` options directly inside the preview right-click menu, fixed Streamer Mode leaks in preview tooltips and Recent Notifications (adding class colors, cleaned links, and coalesced `(xN)` counters), added live theme updates and `skipAutoRestore` on preview mute confirmation popups, widened and synced **Combat / World Log** (`countText` right-bound, single-row World Log filters, themed header controls), added live theme & context refresh + keyboard-capture cleanup + target channel badges + empty-state guidance inside **Quick Reply -> Customize Replies & Shortcuts**, fixed `QuickReply` info-bar line spacing and subtext clipping, added full-preview hover tooltips and Streamer Mode masking in Whispers dropdown & message feeds, fixed UTF-8 truncation on `Quote Reply` (hiding it on read-only Mentions), and made **Settings -> Notifications**'s mute counter, disabled-alerts detector, **Re-enable Alerts**, and **Unmute All Players** accurately cover Do Not Disturb, Friend Online alerts, Minimap Previews, Watchlist mutes, and session Battle.net/channel mutes.
- **Conversations Tab & Multi-Line Copy Modal (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Upgraded the shared **Copy** modal (`OpenCopyModal`) with an automatic scrollable multi-line text area (`SetMaxLetters(0)`) and **Select All Text** button so full chat transcripts copy without truncation or broken newlines, and aligned `Close` cleanly at the bottom-right corner.
  - Added immediate `Hide()` guards after modal creation, dynamic wrapping height on **Delete Conversation**, wider non-clipping layouts & hover highlights on **Mentions & Keywords** and **Filtered Chat Log**, full Streamer Mode masking across New Chat, Filtered Log, thread rows, tooltips, headers, and message lines, grouped right-click and `Actions` menus with dividers (plus `Pin in Quick Reply`), context-aware `charCounter` (`180` vs `255`), UTF-8 safe `Quote Reply`, and per-context Quick Reply removal/reset + shortcut customization from the main **Conversations** tab.

### UI Polish & Social / Group Finder Redesign (`O` & `I`)
- **Social Window (`O` — Watchlist, Social, Nearby) Layout & Readability (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Unified all non-compact rows across **Watchlist**, **Social**, and **Nearby** at `44px` height with crisp cropped `24x24` class avatars and corner status pips, anchored `metaRight` tags cleanly on Line 1 (`TOPRIGHT`) so Line 2 details (Guild, Zone, Notes, Last Seen) extend unobscured across narrow embedded windows, styled collapsible folder headers with tinted banners and right-aligned online/total counters, added hover Whisper/Invite quick buttons and source badges to **Nearby** rows, preserved expanded card highlights across button hovers, suppressed redundant folder repeats on grouped Social sublines, enhanced the inline Social Quick-Inspect card with full note fallback/alts summary and top divider, separated **Social** filter/sort vs profile/add controls and source vs tag chips with hairline dividers, restored the tab-strip hairline divider in Dark/Native embedded `O`, kept `searchBox` at an unclipped `23px` height, tightened compact tab spacing, cropped embedded compact cog/refresh icons, and added semantic highlight colors to `summaryText`.
- **Group Finder (`I` — Browse, Categories, Activities & Filter Drawer) Hierarchy (`UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Providers/LFGFilterEngine.lua`):**
  - Re-architected **Browse** cards into a zero-collision 3-band hierarchy (Line 1: crisp ivory Activity headline left of `20x20` stepped role slots; Line 2: full-width Leader Crown, Name, Level, Class crest, clickable relationship icons & alert tags cleanly below role slots; Line 3: `[LFM]`/mode, Playstyle & full Note clamped cleanly left of member class icons), removed duplicate `"Lv X"` echo on narrow Line 1 titles, cleared stale right-edge recycle anchors on `metaFs`, centered **Bulletin** header rows cleanly inside `74px` cards, fixed trailing separator on Bulletin lockout tooltips, cropped all row action button icons (`Character-Plus`, `Pass`, `Gossip`), prevented recycled card backgrounds from leaking onto section headers, and added theme-aware dark card backings and section header tints.
  - Eliminated narrow-window button overlaps on the bottom action bar across `browse` and `activities` views, raised Role Circles slightly in Create view so checkboxes cleanly clear `"Auto-Select"` (and capped `autoRoleBtn` width so role suggestions never clip), calibrated Category banner titles (`GameFontNormal`) for clean margins inside textured bezels, aligned **Browse** header dropdowns/buttons along a single centerline, expanded the Browse **Activity** dropdown width (`+26px`) inside narrow `338px` frames, added a high-contrast translucent card behind empty-list messages over cliff artwork (including a clear amber notice whenever active listings are hidden by current filters), prevented **Playstyle**, **Listing Mode**, and **Broadcast To** dropdowns from clipping adjacent localized labels in Create view, framed the Whisper Template modal's **Invite Preview** cleanly with overflow-safe presets, added `>` chevrons to Category banners, upgraded the non-leader notice card and Browse amber bar, added alternating zebra/selected tints + `24px` rows and a live `0/90` character counter in Activity listing view, added dynamic wrapping height (`54 -> 64px`) when Tab 1's Mirror Bar shows the redirect instruction so text never truncates, added complete hover tooltips on Side Filter Drawer utility, loot-shield, source, and organization buttons, and reflowed **Guild & Addon** cards to eliminate cramping.
- **Social (`O`), Group & Run Log Refinement (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added an adaptive right-aligned live summary counter inside embedded `O` tab bars (`Watchlist`, `Social`, `Nearby`) whenever horizontal space allows, differentiated **All** (`Achievement_GuildPerk_EverybodysFriend`) and **Online Only** (`UI-Toast-FriendOnlineIcon`) chips from the right-hand **Show Offline** status orb, compacted `socialChipsBar` (`22x22`) so **Legend** remains visible in narrow Blizzard `O` frames, added dark contrast halos (`dotBg`) behind avatar corner status pips, removed inline ASCII `> `/`v ` clutter on **Social** player names, capped `metaRight` width (`108px`) & shortened compact `[Alts]` tags in narrow `O` views so names stay readable, streamlined **Watchlist** & **Social** Line 2 sublines (omitting redundant `"Online"` prefix when zone/guild exist and pairing `"Offline (time)"` concisely), kept the Battle.net friend invite banner readable in narrow `O`, framed **Watchlist**'s input toolbar (`watchBar`) inside a matching surface card with full `+ Add` text in wide layouts, added a 100%-opaque backing on the Watchlist autocomplete dropdown, enriched **Watchlist**/**Social**/**Nearby** hover tooltips with class-colored headlines/levels, live status/source info, and click cues, grouped right-click context menu sections cleanly with dividers, eliminated localized text clipping/overflow across **Social Card**, **My Social Profile**, and **Character Match** modals, standardized `detailBox` action buttons (`24x22`), and added tailored contextual empty-state messages across `Watchlist`, `Social`, and `Nearby`.
  - Upgraded **Current Group** & **Recent Companions** rows (`UI/SocialPanels.lua`) to `44px` height with cropped `24x24` class avatars and corner status pips, separated **Group**'s header summary line into an uncluttered upper band above the view-tab divider (`72px`), pinned **Need/Avoid** and **Dispels/Resurrection** summaries onto right-aligned columns in `compCard` so narrow embedded views or 25-man raids never clip utility counts, added responsive width reflow on **Find Groups** (`OnSizeChanged`) so `searchBox` and `roleRow` controls never overlap on narrower windows, unified **Find Groups** (`ROW_H = 74`, `22px` search box, `16px` refresh icon) with `(N)` active advanced-filter counts moved onto `More Filters »`, and converted **Dungeon & Raid Log**'s `Recording: ON/OFF` control into an explicit auto-fitted status pill matching its pause prompt.

### Performance & Memory Architecture
- **Collectibles & Gathering Highlight 3D World Display Fix (`Providers/GatherHighlight.lua`, `UI/GatherHighlight.lua`):**
  - Restored `SoftTargetIconInteract = "1"` alongside `SoftTargetIconGameObject = "1"` and `SoftTargetNameplateInteract = "1"` (with `SoftTargetIconSize >= 36`, `SoftTargetNameplateSize >= 32`, `outlineModeShowLootEffectWhenDisabled = "1"`, and safe table/scalar `SafeGetCVar` handling) so Blizzard's official overhead herb/ore/chest icon renders reliably over world collectibles without touching quest or enemy outlines.
  - Fixed SocialCentral's over-node pulsing starburst halo, downward arrow, and contrast label pill failing to appear over detected herbs/nodes: removed transient `IsVisible()` frame-0 rejection in `IsPlateAnchorSafe`, enabled `SetIgnoreParentAlpha(true)` / `SetIgnoreParentScale(true)` on `EnsurePlateGlow` so 0-alpha GameObject soft-interact plates never hide the halo, added multi-source `FindSafeSoftInteractPlate` lookup (`"softinteract"`, `NAME_PLATE_UNIT_ADDED` hint unit, and safe `C_NamePlate.GetNamePlates(false)` scan), broke the `unchanged` re-emit deadlock when a plate attaches late, and wired automatic plate attachment inside the active beacon's `0.35s` sanity tick and post-CVar-toggle refresh.
- **Cyclical RAM Leak Fix & Seamless Combat Quick Reply Input (`UI/Minimap.lua`, `Providers/Group.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`):**
  - Eliminated an infinite `UPDATE_BINDINGS` feedback loop in **Quick Reply** (`SyncEnterBinding`, `SyncCtrlWBinding`, `SyncQuickReplyHotkeys`) caused by unconditional `ClearOverrideBindings` / `SetOverrideBindingClick` calls re-triggering `UPDATE_BINDINGS` every frame until Lua memory exceeded `100 MB` and forced a `99%` CPU GC sweep; added strict re-entrancy/self-echo suppression and desired-state signature diffing so keybindings only update when actual state changes.
  - Throttled live `DAMAGE_METER_COMBAT_SESSION_UPDATED` polling (`Providers/Group.lua`) to `2.0s` intervals during combat (preserving immediate encounter-end/finalize reads) and switched `ResolveDamageMeterSourceKey`, `RecordOutboundOrigin` ring slots (`Providers/Chat.lua`), `OnAddonMsgHook` unknown-prefix `debugstack` caching, `ApplyExclusiveChatMode`, `EnforceLater`, and `IsAnyBlizzardGamePanelOpen` to zero-allocation reuse.
  - Pre-docked `_G.ChatFrame1EditBox` cleanly inside Quick Reply's bottom input slot across all tabs (`Grupo`, `Guilda`, `Raide`, `Whisper`, `Geral`) while in SocialCentral Chat Mode, keeping it hidden until activated and seamlessly replacing Quick Reply's composer bar in place during combat (with live `ChatEdit_UpdateHeader` skin sync, combat-safe Z-order clamping, and unified font/offset/channel-color/focus-border parity via `SyncInputStyleToOfficial` & `ResolveOfficialPrefixColor` so out-of-combat and combat typing look identical).
- **Phase 1 — Zero-CPU Idle UI, Native GPU Animations & Dynamic Event Gating (`UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Nearby.lua`, `Providers/Group.lua`, `Providers/LootRoll.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`):**
  - Replaced the continuous Lua `OnUpdate` pulse loops for `[?]` Help buttons and single-tone counter badges with native WoW `AnimationGroup` (`BOUNCE`) animations (**0% Lua CPU** while idle/visible, pausing cleanly in combat).
  - Implemented automatic zero-CPU idle sleep in **Quick Reply** (`QuickReply:WakeOnUpdate()`), detaching `OnUpdate` whenever settled and unfocused in Always-Open mode (waking via hover/click/focus/message/combat or a single fade timer), added an `O(1)` fast-path on outside mouse clicks, and scoped `UNIT_SPELLCAST_SENT` strictly to `"player"` via `RegisterUnitEvent`.
  - Eliminated combat `debugstack()` unwind spikes in the Error Origin Tracker (`Providers/Chat.lua`), added fast-path & 30s probe caching for shared-library prefixes, converted `_worldLogBuffer` to an `O(1)` zero-allocation ring buffer, replaced per-message `OfficialChatShieldFilter` table/closure allocations with a pooled coalesced queue, deferred `ChatFrame1` copy capture to native `GetMessageInfo` on demand, and made Watchlist presence probing (`SyncPresenceProbeTicker`) strictly on-demand (0 timers when empty, disabled, or inside instances).
  - Unregistered Blizzard events and tickers dynamically whenever modules are disabled or idle across `Nearby` (26 events + 8s ticker), `Group` (12 dungeon-run events including `UNIT_FLAGS` registered strictly during active runs), `LootRoll` (dynamic history/chat roll listeners), `Clan` (external chat LFG listeners), and `Social` (active-only UI polling), and softened idle GC stepping to avoid frame hitches on window close.
- **Phase 2 — Startup RAM & Lazy Settings Construction (`Locales/Locale.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`):**
  - Pruned **857 verified dead/obsolete translation keys** (~1,714 lines / ~254 KB) from `Locales/Locale.lua` while preserving 100% EN/PT/ES parity across every active key.
  - Converted **Settings** (`UI/Settings.lua`) to **lazy per-tab construction** (building only the active tab on first open, deferring the other 3 tabs until clicked or searched) and deferred `Search.synonymLookup` table expansion until the first search query.
- **Phase 3 — Lossless Chat History Compaction & Lazy Thread Hydration (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`):**
  - Implemented **lazy whisper thread hydration** (`Chat:EnsureThreadHydrated`) so unopened historical conversations keep only lightweight headers in RAM at login and hydrate full message arrays strictly on demand when selected, messaged, or searched.
  - Added lossless compact message persistence (omitting redundant `false` booleans, duplicate thread sender/faction fields, and empty zero-timestamp whisper stubs) with automatic idempotent startup compaction for existing accounts.

- **Group Loot Roll UI Cleanup (`UI/LootRollFrame.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `Providers/LootRoll.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Removed the redundant top-of-screen Loot Winner announcement banner (`SocialCentralLootWinnerBanner`) and its Settings/Minimap toggles, keeping the active roll card, notification popup, local chat winner summary, and full Roll Results History window intact.
  - Enabled hiding Blizzard's default `GroupLootFrame1..8` / `GroupLootContainer` roll bars by default (`lootRollHideOfficial = true`) whenever SocialCentral's Group Loot Roll window is active, pre-hooking `OnShow`/`GroupLootContainer_Update` at login and applying combat-safe alpha suppression so Blizzard's bar never overlaps SocialCentral's roll window.
  - Fixed a broken square glyph (`★`) next to players' roll choices in the live Group Loot Roll card and local winner summary by switching to native WoW star/leader textures (`UI-RaidTargetingIcon_1` / `UI-Group-LeaderIcon`) and showing the leader star only once actual roll numbers are known.
- **Notifications Performance & Visual Polish (`Providers/LootRoll.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/LootRollFrame.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Removed group loot winner items from the floating `NotificationToast` queue and Notification History (`PushWinnerToast` / `LOOT_WINNER`), keeping `NotificationToast` focused on messages & social/guild alerts while Loot Roll wins trigger only the dedicated Epic Self-Win Celebration, active roll card, local chat summary, and Roll Results ledger.
  - Eliminated micro-stutters in **Minimap Message Previews** (`MsgPreview`): replaced `BackdropTemplate` NineSlice border rebuilds during expand/collapse with lightweight 1px edge textures, cached anchor geometry & palette/theme styles, quantized pixel transforms at 30 Hz (`15 Hz` idle hold), cached cleaned text snippets, eliminated 40-player raid `pcall` loops on message arrival, and fixed held slots freezing on `curOffsetY` when additional previews spawn (`_holdVisualsSettled` invalidation + `8px` slot spacing so stacked cards never overlap).
  - Replaced per-arrival `OnUpdate` closures in `MinimapBtn:PulseArrival` with native GPU `AnimationGroup` scale/alpha pulses (`0%` Lua CPU), throttled `BadgeCluster:Drive` and `OnEpicWinUpdate` cleanly to 30 Hz, guarded `UpdateBadge` & `AnchorMainOrb` with full dirty-state signatures, and cached fusion-lobe sizes inside `Theme:CreateCounterBadge`.
  - Elevated **Message Previews** & **Notification Toasts** visuals with 100%-opaque layered headers (`headerWash` + gradient `headerRule`), `36x36` class-rimmed avatar medallions (with class-crest fallback on toasts), brief arrival sheen flashes, and glowing ember tips anchored to the progress bar.
  - Scoped the Loot Roll floating dice button (`UI/LootRollFrame.lua`, `Providers/LootRoll.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`) to remove the historical verified-drop counter badge (`tot`), display the button by default only while inside a dungeon/raid and for 3 minutes after exiting/ending a run (`180s` persisted across `/reload`), and default the Loot Roll Results window on every open to the **Last Group Summary** (clustering drops and `/roll` rounds by run/group with an explicit **Full History** toggle inside the window).
  - Added a customizable, draggable floating **Quick Access Bar** (`UI/QuickBar.lua`, `SocialCentralQuickBarFrame`, `/sc bar`) with tailored per-button contextual right-click menus across all 14 actions, timed & condition-based **Do Not Disturb** auto-resume (`15m`, `30m`, `1h`, `2h`, `Until leaving instance`, `Until leaving group` via `dndUntilLeaveGroup` in `Core/Compat.lua` + live orange badge timer), 1-click **Quick Presets** (`Dungeon & Raid`, `Gather & World`, `Social & Guild`), unmistakable GPU-pulsed double-ring active glows vs desaturated dimmed OFF toggles (`UI/QuickBar.lua`, `UI/GatherHighlight.lua`), a concise 1-step Chromie intro guide (`quickBarIntroAck`), Horizontal/Vertical orientation, position lock/reset, and live 5–10 button selection & reordering (`Settings` + `Minimap`).
  - Added **Epic Guild Milestone Lines & 1-Click Welcome/Congrats Actions (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`)**: automatically records Guild join, leave, removal, promotion, and demotion system events into the **Guild** (`ch:guild`) chat feed across both **Quick Reply** and **Main Window -> Conversations**, styled with ornate class-colored milestone banners and clickable **`[Welcome!]`** / **`[Congrats!]`** buttons that send a localized guild greeting in 1 click (or right-click/Shift-click to customize before sending).
  - Updated **Collectibles Highlight** (`Providers/GatherHighlight.lua`, `UI/GatherHighlight.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`) to use WoW's official world-object Soft-Target detection and game-object icon (without hiding quest/enemy outlines or cluttering NPCs) paired with SocialCentral's pulsing node starburst halo and top banner, added a movable 1-click floating toggle button (`SocialCentralGatherToggleButton`, `gatherHighlightQuickButton`) beside the Minimap, fixed missing `LootRoll:GetActiveCount()` (`Providers/LootRoll.lua`) powering active roll badges and card reopen shortcuts, cached `QuickBar` backdrop styles with live tooltip refresh and aligned Settings layout (`UI/QuickBar.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`), condensed all Chromie speech-bubble guides across the entire addon into crisp 1–2 line explanations, added section-level Settings jump targeting (`Settings:Open(tabId, sectionKey)`), Gather/Loot Search synonyms, header **Configure** menu & Full History count inside **Loot Roll Results** (`UI/LootRollFrame.lua`), **More Settings...** in Minimap Preview setup (`UI/Minimap.lua`), and **Configure Notifications...** right-click parity on floating Notification Toasts (`UI/NotificationToast.lua`).

## [0.2.27] - 2026-11-25
### Added & Changed
- **Frontend Visual Overhaul & Zero-Overhead Performance Polish (`UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `UI/LootRollFrame.lua`, `UI/GatherHighlight.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added theme-aware surface lighting gradients, crisp bevel highlights, recessed input inner shadows, stylized custom scrollbars & `UIPanelScrollFrame` skinning, tapered accent-capped section headers synced across theme switches, hover highlights across all directory/chat/alts/run lists, and native `UI-LFG-ICON-PORTRAITROLES` role badges across Guild Events, Raid Grid, and Group Composition cards.
  - Expanded the first-run **Theme Welcome Picker** (`UI/Theme.lua`) into a live 3-card showcase (**Blizzard Official**, **Classic Gold**, and **Modern Theme**) with accurate charcoal-bronze Blizzard styling, updated Quick Reply / Main Window / Notification / Settings Chromie guides (`[?]`), moved the Settings window header & section banners onto the unified palette system, tightened button/title spacing across all modals (eliminating localized PT/ES overlaps), and added direct current-value previews + 6-choice layout in Settings Search cards.
  - Throttled steady-state animations cleanly (`LootRollFrame`, `GatherHighlight`, `ClanSpotter` nameplate arrows, focused `QuickReply` border pulse -> 30 Hz with pixel quantization), added dirty-state caching (`SyncDepthPolish`, `UpdateClanPanel`, `AltsPanel:RefreshTheme`, `SyncHostNativeTabs`, `MsgPreview` HOLD phase, nameplate badges), replaced hot `{ frame:GetChildren() }` array allocations with zero-alloc traversal in `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, debounced Settings Search (`0.09s`) with zero-alloc highlighter reuse, and gated static Watchlist ticker redraws (`15s`).
  - Fixed `InstallErrorOriginTracker` (`Providers/Chat.lua`) capturing its own hook frames in `debugstack`, which caused third-party addons sending `"PARTY"` addon messages while solo to be mislabeled as `[Origin: SocialCentral]`, and required `>= 2` real group peers in `GetActiveGroupChannel()` / `GetActiveGroupSyncInfo()` (`Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`).
  - Added live `HelpTip` detection and direct Chromie spotlight popup (`gatherInteractTipExplained` / `ShowInteractKeyTipGuide`) plus a 3-step Chromie tutorial (`gatherHighlightTutorialShown` in `UI/GatherHighlight.lua`) and synchronized `[?]` help explanations (`UI/GatherHighlight.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`) explaining how Collectibles Highlight uses Blizzard's official Soft-Target system and letting players know they can safely close WoW's yellow `"Interact Key"` tip.
  - Updated `Ctrl+W` feedback in **Quick Reply** (`UI/Minimap.lua`) so attempting to close the permanently fixed **General** (or core fixed) tab states clearly that it is permanent and cannot be closed, instead of telling the user to unpin it.
  - Replaced noisy per-message `[G1]`/`[G7]`/`[G8]` group tags with unified **group-session timeline dividers** (`—— [Group 20:18 · Zone · N players] ——`), added **day dividers** (`Today` / `Yesterday` / `DD/MM/YYYY`) and `>= 2h` time-gap markers inside **Quick Reply** & **Conversations**, unified dungeon formation rosters + `/reload` persistence into a single session ID (`CaptureCurrentGroupSnapshot` & `GetThreadGroupRenderMap` in `Providers/Chat.lua`), and enriched `[HH:MM]` timestamp hover tooltips (`UI/Theme.lua`) with exact full date, clock time, relative age, and group info.
  - Eliminated a fast-typing race condition after pressing `ENTER` (`UI/Minimap.lua`): switched `QuickReply` `ENTER` and `/` trigger buttons to `AnyDown` (with keydown-to-enter double-fire guard), queued `SyncEnterBinding()` retries whenever a blurring EditBox momentarily still held keyboard focus, restricted `DockOfficialEditBox` from hiding `QuickReply`'s composer on non-official tabs (`Party`/`Guild`/`Whisper`), and routed any accidental Blizzard `ChatFrame1EditBox` activation in Addon Chat Mode directly back into the active Quick Reply tab preserving typed text.
- **Group Loot Roll Tracking & Results Visibility (`Providers/LootRoll.lua`, `UI/LootRollFrame.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Integrated native `C_LootHistory` event & drop syncing (supporting both modern Encounter Loot History and legacy roll history tables) alongside reordered/localized `CHAT_MSG_LOOT` GlobalString matching (`LOOT_ROLL_*`, `LOOT_ROLL_ALL_PASSED`, `LOOT_ITEM*`) and player identity deduplication.
  - Prevented active roll cards from closing 12s after the local player rolls while teammates are still deciding; keeps the card alive until resolved, then clearly shows each teammate's live choice (`Need` / `Greed` / `Disenchant` / `Pass`), rolled numbers (`1–100`), and the final **Winner** (`Winner: Name [Roll]`) or **All passed** banner.
  - Added an opt-in **"Hide official WoW roll bar while active"** setting (`lootRollHideOfficial` in **Settings -> Summary & Windows** and inside the first-roll activation prompt) that safely hides Blizzard's default roll bars (`GroupLootContainer` / `GroupLootFrame1..8`) while SocialCentral's roll window is active and automatically restores any still-active official bar if disabled or dismissed via `[X]`.
- **Global CPU, Raid FPS & Memory Optimization Audit (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `Providers/GatherHighlight.lua`):**
  - Eliminated per-hit Combat Log regex, `date("*t")` table allocations, and `table.remove(..., 1)` array shifts in raids by gating live `ChatFrame2` parsing strictly on an open Combat Log drawer (clearing its buffer on hide and backfilling directly from Blizzard's `ChatFrame2` buffer with cached day-epoch math on open, while letting `OpenCopyOfficialChat` read `ChatFrame2` on demand).
  - Optimized always-visible Quick Reply `OnUpdate` loop (`UI/Minimap.lua`): eliminated per-frame unfocused `string.format` allocations, skipped redundant `SetAlpha`/pass-through API calls once settled, and throttled idle unfocused mouse/draft polling (~12.5 Hz) while keeping focused shimmer and fade transitions smooth.
  - Throttled steady-state counter badge animations (`UI/Theme.lua`), reordered `Chat:StepPresenceProbes` cheap guards before API probes, and scoped `GatherHighlight` aura/spell/nameplate events strictly to `"player"` and active opt-in states (`0` aura/plate events when off or answered).
- **Official Collectibles & Resource Highlight (`Providers/GatherHighlight.lua`, `UI/GatherHighlight.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`, `SocialCentral.toc`):**
  - Added an opt-in, 100% Blizzard-policy-compliant **Collectibles & Gathering Highlight** for herbs, mining veins, fishing pools, and chests/resources: snapshots and safely restores the player's original client Soft-Target Interact & Outline settings, anchors an animated pulsing starburst halo + high-contrast dark pill label over targeted resource plates without touching protected frames or nameplate CVars, displays an optional click-through screen indicator banner (movable in preview mode), and prompts politely when activating a profession tracking filter on the Minimap.
### Changed & Fixed
- **Always-Visible Quick Reply, Configurable Idle Transparency (30s Default) & Opt-In Combat Hide (`Core/Init.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Switched Quick Reply default mode to always stay visible (`quickReplyAutoClose = false`), auto-opening silently without stealing keyboard focus when SocialCentral Chat Mode is active and keeping the window open on `ESC` (closing only via `[X]`, Minimap toggle, or optional Auto-Close mode).
  - Made Quick Reply's idle transparency delay user-configurable (`quickReplyIdleFade = true`, `quickReplyFadeDelay = 30` seconds by default, `5–120s` stepper in **Settings -> Notifications** and presets/`Never` in the Quick Reply header timer menu), restoring 100% opacity immediately on hover, click, or typing.
  - Changed combat hiding/click-through (`quickReplyFadeInCombat = false`) to opt-in (off by default) so Quick Reply stays visible during combat unless explicitly enabled by the user.
- **Decoupled Conversations from Social (O) & Unified Reply Routing (`UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Providers/LFGFilterEngine.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Removed the heavy `chat` (Conversations) tab from the embedded Social/Friends (`O`) side-tab so `O` stays focused strictly on **Watchlist**, **Social**, and **Nearby** without unread chat badge/panel churn inside `FriendsFrame`.
  - Added unified `Chat:OpenPreferredConversation` routing across Social/Watchlist/Nearby whisper buttons & context menus, Group/Recent Companions/Dungeon Run/Guild Professions menus, Blizzard player right-click menus (`Watchlist`), LFG whisper/apply actions, Target Radar HUD & menu, `/sc al`, and `[SC]` player links: sends via **Official WoW Chat** (`Compat.Whisper` / `Compat.BNetWhisper`) when the player opted for Official Chat Mode, opens **Quick Reply** when enabled in SocialCentral Chat Mode, or opens the standalone **Main Window -> Conversations** (`UI:ShowHub("chat")`) when Quick Reply is disabled. Fixed `SetChatUiMode("official")` checking non-existent `UI:IsChatActive()` instead of `UI:IsChatTabVisible()`, and guarded `NavigateToModule("chat" / "rooms")` before mutating `activeThreadKey`.
- **CPU & Memory Performance Optimizations (`UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`):**
  - Eliminated 8 redundant `GetThreadsList` + `table.sort` passes per chat refresh by computing `CollectUnreadBadgeSummary` in a single pass.
  - Added incremental `msgFrame:AddMessage` appending in **Conversations** (`chatPanel:Refresh`) when new messages arrive at the end of the active thread instead of calling `msgFrame:Clear()` and re-formatting all 150–300 historical lines.
  - Coalesced public channel `CHAT_UPDATED` bursts when Main Window or Quick Reply is visible (`QueueChatEmit(0.08)`), cached non-SocialCentral addon prefix owners before calling `debugstack` in `ResolveStackOrigin`, added O(1) early-exit in `InstallEmbeddedTooltipGuard` when no Blizzard host is embedded, skipped redundant `SyncFrameSectionLayers` / `EnsureMainFrameDraggable` / `ReflowTabStrip` / `DiscoverRightSideTabs` scans on steady-state refreshes, added no-op early-returns in `CounterBadge:SetCount`, and reduced `HelpPulseOnUpdate` frequency to 10 Hz.
- **Cross-Module Logical Consistency Fixes (`UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):**
  - Exported `LFGFrameTab:EnsureStandaloneLfgHost()` and allowed `LFGFrameTab:ShowSubPanel(customHost)` when called with an explicit standalone host so clicking **List Group** from **Main Window -> Group -> Find Groups** works even when Group Finder (`I`) integration is disabled in Settings.
  - Added `Clan:SyncOfficialLfgEvents()` so toggling LFG Watchers in Quick Options or Settings immediately refreshes watcher scans and clan UI state.
  - Fixed offline Watchlist `"Guild"` auto-grouping in `UI/MainFrame.lua` calling non-existent `ns.Clan:GetMyGuildInfo()` (now reads `Store.guildInfo.name` / `GetGuildInfo("player")`), fixed Minimap `Shift+Click` close check calling non-existent `UI:HideMain()` (`UI:Hide()`), wired `Providers/Chat.lua` cache invalidation to actual `"SOCIAL_UPDATED"`/`"WATCHLIST_UPDATED"` events instead of unemitted `"SOCIAL_META_UPDATED"`, and replaced unsubscribed `"SETTINGS_CHANGED"` emits in Mention Keywords and `[SC] Addon Chat` tab checkboxes with `"SETTINGS_UPDATED"`.
  - Exposed `Theme:ApplyInputBackdrop(...)` (`UI/Theme.lua`) so Group Finder's listing comment box styles and re-themes properly, implemented `Compat.FormatDurationShort` (`Core/Compat.lua`) so Group Finder lockout tooltips show remaining reset timers instead of blank strings, switched Guild Roster History modal (`UI/MainFrame.lua`) to `MakeMovable({ key = "rosterHistoryModal" })` + `Theme:RestoreWindowPosition` instead of non-existent `SaveFramePosition`/`RestoreFramePosition`, and removed the dead `RequestWorldBuffCatchup` login sync step from `Providers/Clan.lua`.
  - Added missing `Compat.GuildName()`, `Compat.MyFullName()`, `Compat.LowerUtf8()`, and `Compat.TruncateUtf8()` helpers in `Core/Compat.lua` (fixing scoped guild chat storage keys, UTF-8 case folding/truncation, and Forever full self name resolution in Loot Roll), fixed 18 Lua multi-return `and`/`or` truncation bugs across `Providers/Alts.lua` (reagent bag slots, recipe reagent quantities, craft rank/max, recipe info), `UI/MainFrame.lua` (saved main window height + BNet watchlist menu lookup), `UI/Theme.lua` (`GetScreenDimensions`, CENTER drag offset saving, viewport clamping, and cursor Y fallback tracking), `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, and `UI/SocialPanels.lua` (thread AFK/DND status tooltips & chat export names), converted `deleteThreadModal` confirmation input from `SetScript("OnTextChanged")` to `HookScript`, switched `rosterHistoryModal` backdrop from `"frame"` to `"window"`, and removed dead `WORLD_BUFFS_UPDATED` emit from `UI/Settings.lua`.

## [0.2.26] - 2026-11-25
### Fixed
- **Slash Command Prefix Safety (`UI/Minimap.lua`):** Fixed Quick Reply's Official Chat slash-capture hook so unfinished slash commands like `/r` (`/reload`, `/rl`, `/roll`, `/readycheck`), `/p` (`/played`, `/pull`), `/g` (`/gm`, `/grats`), `/i` (`/invite`, `/inspect`), `/o` (`/open`), `/ra` (`/random`, `/range`), and `/al` (`/alias`) wait until **Space** is pressed after the command (or after the target name for `/w`, `/whisper`, `/t`, `/tell`).
- **Chat, Notifications Settings & Group Finder Audit Fixes (`UI/Settings.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `Providers/LFGFilterEngine.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Fixed a Lua crash in **Settings -> Notifications** caused by swapped `CreateChoiceRow` arguments (`mpAnchorRow`), and added missing `AddDeps` bindings for Lock Quick Reply, Chat Shortcut Button, Modifier Quick Actions, and Error Origin Tags.
  - Fixed Quick Reply `ExecuteSlashCommand` stealing keyboard focus back on Blizzard chat fallback, fixed `senderLabel`/`cls` order in `ch:general` self-message recording, enabled `quickReplyNotifySay` incoming alerts, improved `{rt1}..{rt8}` deduplication, allowed `/w <Target> `, `/s `, `/y `, and `/ali ` switching inside Quick Reply's input box, exempted Combat Log / Chromie Help Popup clicks from outside-interaction dim, and preserved the active Combat Log filter highlight on theme switches.
  - Fixed `HandleOfficialActiveEntryUpdate` calling `ShowSubPanel(true)` with a boolean argument, fixed non-transitive `LFG:SortRows` ordering between mixed Group and Solo listings, enforced Raid `need` counts and non-official `classFile` exclusions in `LFG:MatchesAdvancedFilters`, widened `PositionSideDrawer` right offset so Filter Center clears Group Finder's right side-tab, restored `OFFICIAL` source filter across `/reload`, synced `LFG_BROWSE_STATE_CHANGED` across Filter Center / Quick Options and **Main Frame -> Group -> Find Groups**, aligned bottom `Invite` with row `+` Auto-Whisper, fixed Group panel Chromie Tour Step 3 `btnBroadcast` target, wired Guild's **Clans & Allies LFG** button to `TRUSTED` with combat fallback to standalone **Find Groups**, and updated Listing Creation `[?]` help texts, combat module prefixes, and 365-day Recent Companions copy.
- **Chat & LFG Round-2 Deep Polish (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `Providers/LFGFilterEngine.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Coalesced `ChatFrame1` mirroring into **General** on the next frame (`QueueGeneralOfficialFrameLine`) so live events never double-record, pinned `Geral` (`ch:general`) permanently at index #1 in Quick Reply, stopped `ENTER` from hijacking an already-open tab, preserved destination drafts and Forever two-word `/w FirstName Surname ` switching in Quick Reply's input box, exempted chip middle-clicks from outside-interaction dim, blocked combat-faded `QuickReply` from stealing Shift+Click item links, updated docked Quick Reply / Toast / Preview anchors on `ChatFrame1` move/resize, added **Kills & Misc** `"misc"` filtering + timestamp deduplication + resize handle on **Combat Log**, preserved scroll offset across incoming lines in **Conversations**, fixed `/al` without args opening **Conversations -> Alliance**, fixed `Theme:ResetAllWindowPositions()` resetting Minimap previews, suppressed duplicate preview cards when `addonMsgToast` fires, and added `QuickReply:IsViewingThread` auto-dismiss checks on `CHAT_UPDATED`.
  - Added full **Find Groups (LFG)** parity in **Main Frame -> Group** (dedicated **More Filters »** Filter Center toggle docking beside `SocialCentralFrame`, 10-slot filter chip pool, `"Collapse All Sections"`, unknown-leader / standalone **List Group** handling, and live `LFG_BROWSE_STATE_CHANGED` emission across menu toggles), stopped `SyncBrowseFilterToActiveListing` from silently overwriting manual Browse filters on reopen, aligned `"Suitable for My Level"` OFF reset and Favorite level exemption, preserved Tab 4 / hid `nativeBridgeBar` over embedded Group, cleared `st.languages` on `ResetAdvancedFilters`, lifted `±3`/`±5` level cap above 60, loaded Favorites across all categories when viewing `ALL`, fixed `{spec}`, `{role}`, `{ilvl}`, and `{activity_en}` (including Classic talent tabs and `QUESTS`) in Apply & Invite templates, synced Settings `lfgBulletinCb` (`120` run log copy), isolated `clanLfgAlertFilter` from forcing global LFG watchers on, and cleared restrictive filters when clicking `[View in LFG]` chat links.

### Added
- **Exclusive SocialCentral Chat Mode, Docked Official Bar & Full Chat Parity (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**

  - Added an interactive Group Loot Roll window with equipped-item comparison lines, live party **Need / Greed / Disenchant / Pass / Winner** roll tracking, first-roll opt-in prompt & `/sc loot test` preview simulation, and synchronized `[?]` Chromie help integration.
