  - Added unified LFG engine with 2-mode **Smart Filter Center** (`Official Search` vs `Guild & Addon`), Tri-State role matrix, `No My Class`, Solo Recruiting archetypes/presets, Favorites & Whisper Templates, and integrated **Find Groups (LFG)** inside **Main Frame -> Group**.
  - Added **Quick Reply** improvements: permanently fixed session-only **General** (`ch:general`) tab #1 with deduplicated `ChatFrame1` capture/backfill, standalone **Combat Log** header button (`SocialCentralCombatLogFrame`), Say/Yell logs, inline gold chat mention highlighting (removed from Whisper badges), sticky tab persistence + `ENTER` continuation into combat, padlock move lock + 1-click Official Chat dock, separated toolbar & full-width player info strip with tab-only right-click pinning, `Ctrl+Click` Dressing Room previews, and synchronized Chromie interactive guides.

## [0.2.25] - 2026-11-22
### Added
- **Pinned Guild, Group & Manual Whisper Tabs in Quick Reply (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added dedicated Quick Reply tab pinning (`Chat:IsQuickReplyPinned`, `Chat:SetQuickReplyPinned`, `Chat:ToggleQuickReplyPinned`, persisted in `quickReplyPinnedThreads` with session-only isolation for Battle.net-only `bn:*` threads) so players can pin **Guild**, **Officer**, **Party**, **Raid**, **Instance**, **Alliance**, or any **Whisper** / **Room** tab to inspect saved history and reply directly inside Quick Reply while keeping normal Whispers punctual when unpinned and keeping `HasQuickReplyPending()` focused strictly on real unread alerts or drafts.
  - Added a header `[Pin]` toggle button (`f.btnPin`), header & tab chip right-click context menus (`OpenQuickReplyPinsMenu` with quick toggles for Guild/Officer/Party/Raid/Instance/Alliance), `Open Quick Reply` in the Minimap right-click menu, and `Pin in Quick Reply` / `Unpin from Quick Reply` inside Conversations row context menus.
- **Per-Resolution Responsive Positioning & Default Layout Adaptation (`Core/Init.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Stored repositioned frame coordinates (`windowPositionsByRes`), Main Hub window dimensions (`SaveWindowSize` / `GetSavedWindowSize`), and Minimap button angles (`anglesByRes`) **separately per screen resolution profile** (`Theme:GetScreenProfileKey()`, with `<= 24px` windowed border tolerance), automatically migrating any existing custom positions/sizes on first load with zero data loss.
  - Added centralized `DISPLAY_SIZE_CHANGED` / `UI_SCALE_CHANGED` responsive relayout (`Theme:HandleViewportChanged`, `Theme:RegisterViewportListener`) so every repositioned frame (`MainFrame`, `QuickReply`, `NotificationToast`, `MsgPreview`, `SpotterHUD`, `standalone_lfg`, `MinimapBtn`, and movable modals) loads its resolution-specific coordinates or falls back cleanly to a screen-clamped responsive Default layout (including responsive MainFrame default/resize bounds and `ChatFrame1`/Minimap re-anchoring), while `Reset Window Positions` clears only the active resolution without discarding other resolutions' layouts.
- **Persistent Conversations History Across All Categories & Upgrade Lossless Guarantees (`Core/Init.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Expanded persistent local storage across every eligible SocialCentral conversation category: Character Whispers & Direct Chat (`w:*`, `0` to `300` lines, default `100` for new profiles, preserving all existing saved limits), per-guild isolated Guild & Officer history (`ch:guild:*` / `ch:officer:*`, `50` to `500` lines, default `125` with automatic legacy migration and deferred `GUILD_ROSTER_UPDATE` hydration), Party/Instance/Raid chats (`groupChatHistorySave`, default `true`), and Private Rooms, Alliance Chat, & Mentions Radar (`generalChatHistorySave`, default `true`, `25` to `300` lines, default `150`) with `MAX_SAVED_THREADS = 400` and zero BattleTag/Real ID persistence.
  - Protected all channel/room/mentions/guild records in `IsProtectedThreadRecord`, matched in-memory thread caps cleanly (`MergeChronoMessages`, `AppendMessage`), and added compliance invariants in `scripts/verify-compliance.py` guaranteeing zero loss of message history or existing settings across updates.
- **First-Time Interactive Visual Spotlight Quick Reply Tour & Header `[?]` Guide (`UI/Minimap.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Replaced plain text onboarding with a compact 5-step live-spotlight visual walkthrough (`SocialCentralQuickReplyTutorialDialog`, `SocialCentralQuickReplySpotlight`) that highlights the actual Quick Reply UI elements (`chipsBar`, `inputBox`, `btnAutoClose` / `btnHelp`) with a pulsing frame, bouncing directional arrow, live animated border color-cycling demo (Step 1), live lit-vs-dark focus-release demo (Step 4), ultra-short 1-line captions, and `Skip` / `Back` / `Next` controls persisted account-wide (`quickReplyTutorialShown`).
  - Added a `[?]` guide button directly inside the Quick Reply header so players can hover for summary help or click `[?]` anytime to reopen the interactive Quick Reply guide.
- **Battle.net Friend Account Watchlist Tracking Across All Alts (`Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Added a dedicated Battle.net Account picker button (`frame.bnetAddBtn`) in the Watchlist toolbar, inline Battle.net friend autocomplete suggestions in `Watchlist:GetNameSuggestions`, `"Watch Battle.net Account (All Alts)"` in Social Battle.net context menus (even when offline or in Battle.net App), and `"Track all Battle.net / linked alts"` (`watchAllAlts`) in Watchlist row menus.
  - Added privacy-safe Battle.net alt resolution (`AddBNetAccount`, `_sessionBNetWatched`, `PromoteSessionBNetAccount`, and `observedAlts` / live `bnetAccountID` matching in `ScanBattleNet` + `Store:SyncWatchlistObservation`) so one Watchlist entry stays Online and alerts/whispers/invites for whichever character that friend logs in on, persisting strictly WoW character keys without ever writing BattleTags, Real IDs, or `bnetAccountID` into `SavedVariables`.
- **Separate Orange Raid Minimap Notification Circle (`UI/Theme.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Added `"raid"` as an independent notification category (`BADGE_CATEGORY_ORDER`, `Chat:GetUnreadSummary().byCategory.raid`, `NotePreviewFolded`, `ClearPreviewCounts*`, and Minimap `BadgeCluster` / `CreateCounterBadge` 6-lobe Fusion Circle support), rendering a dedicated orange Raid counter circle distinct from Party/Group (blue), Whisper (pink), Guild (green), Battle.net (cyan), and Rooms (purple).
  - Enabled `quickReplyNotifyRaid` by default (`true` + one-time `quickReplyNotifyRaidMigrated` migration) so Raid messages immediately display the orange Raid notification circle on the Minimap while keeping Party/Instance mapped to Group (blue).
- **Quick Reply Official-Chat Docking, ENTER Shortcut & Optional Official-Chat Hiding (`Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Enabled `quickReplyDockToChat = true` by default so Quick Reply aligns over `ChatFrame1Background`/`ChatFrame1` matching its exact live size and position.
  - Enabled `quickReplyEnterOpen = true` by default so pressing `ENTER` with pending Minimap Quick Reply notifications/drafts opens Quick Reply directly over Blizzard chat while leaving normal `ENTER` and `/` unaffected otherwise.
  - Added opt-in `officialHideGroup`, `officialHideGuild`, and `officialHideWhispers` toggles in Settings and the `[SC]` Official Chat menu to keep selected chats out of Blizzard's Official Chat window (preserving server/system notices) while continuing to receive and reply in SocialCentral.
- **Quick Reply Header Auto-Close Selector & Auto-Fit Paginated Conversation Tabs (`UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added a spelled-out header timer button (`Auto-close: X seconds` / `Auto-close: Disabled`) directly inside the Quick Reply header (`5`, `10`, `15`, `20`, `30`, `45`, `60` seconds or disabled), synced live with a matching delay stepper in Settings.
  - Replaced the fixed `11`-byte / `96px` truncated Quick Reply thread tabs with full-width auto-fitting tabs and automatic `< 1/N >` pagination (with mouse-wheel support and hover tooltips) whenever pending conversations exceed one row.
### Fixed
- **Truthful Recent Companions Presence, Online Sorting & Full Cross-Tab Icon Legends (`UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Replaced the misleading fixed blue (`nearby`) dot on `Group -> Recent Companions` with accurate real-time presence resolution (`ResolveRecentCompanionPresence`): Green (`online`) for confirmed online players across Group, Friends, Guild, Battle.net, or Watchlist, Grey (`offline`) for known offline friends/guild/Watchlist entries, and Amber (`unknown`) for unverified pugs outside Friends/Guild; updated `(N Online)` counting, Watchlist context actions (`Check` / `Remove`), and sorted confirmed online teammates to the top.
  - Audited and updated all 6 `Legend` popups (`Group`, `Watchlist`, `Social`, `Guild`, `Conversations`, and `Alts / Items`) plus `Theme:CreateLegendTag` two-column text-tag formatting so `[T]`/`[H]`/`[D]` roles, `[MT]`/`[MA]`/`[ML]` markers, Green/Grey/Amber status dots, `[SC]`, Battle.net globes/`[All Alts]`, `[A]`/`[H]`, `★`/`✔`/`⊘`/🔔, and chat category colors are documented everywhere.
- **Middle-Click & Extra Mouse Button Focus Release on Quick Reply (`UI/Minimap.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Detected `MiddleButton` (scroll-wheel click), `Button4`, and `Button5` presses across `GLOBAL_MOUSE_DOWN`, `GLOBAL_MOUSE_UP`, `WorldFrame` mouse hooks, and live hardware state polling (`CheckExternalMouseButtons` / `IsMouseButtonDown`) so custom mouse shortcuts (such as Auto-Run bound to scroll-click) immediately release Quick Reply keyboard capture, darken the animated border, and start the auto-close timer even when hovered or intercepted as mouse bindings.
- **Animated Chat-Colored Whole-Window Focus Border on Quick Reply (`UI/Minimap.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Added a bright, smooth animated whole-window border, outer halo glow, and sweeping edge shimmer around Quick Reply (`UpdateFocusVisuals`, `_focusBorderEdges`, `_focusHaloLayer`) colored to the active conversation (`Guild` green, `Whisper` pink, `Group` blue, `Raid` orange, `Battle.net` cyan, `Rooms` purple) whenever keyboard focus is active inside Quick Reply, dimming smoothly to a neutral dark border the moment focus leaves on outside click or combat.
- **Quick Reply Continuous Multi-Message Keyboard Focus & Idle Auto-Close Timer (`UI/Minimap.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Preserved active EditBox cursor and keyboard capture in Quick Reply across consecutive sent messages (`Enter` / `Send` / Quick Pings), switching to inactive visual hint (`"Click here to type..."`) and releasing keyboard focus only when clicking outside the Quick Reply frame, pressing `Escape`, opening Blizzard chat, or entering combat.
  - Updated the Quick Reply inactivity detector (`FocusInputBox`, `OnKeyDown`, `OnMouseWheel`, `GLOBAL_MOUSE_DOWN` inside/outside checks) so any typing, cursor navigation, scrolling, or in-window clicks pause and reset the timer cleanly while `Closing in Xs` immediately appears once idle (`2.0s`) or right after clicking outside.
- **Combat Deferral Chat Spam & Official Chat Print Audit (`Core/Init.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Group.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Made `ns:RunAfterCombat` silent by default (`noticeText` opt-in only) and deduplicated queued notices (`alreadyQueued`) across all background tasks (`lfg_try_attach_all`, `focus_env_settings_sync`, `focus_dnd_expired_sync`, `clan_pew_sync_steps`, `clan_pew_init_sync`, `chat_restore_edit_history`, `dungeon_log_first_prompt`), eliminating the repeated `"Will finish right after combat ends."` chat spam during combat.
  - Added a 3-second consecutive-duplicate anti-spam guard on `ns:Print` (excluding indented `/sc` help lines) and corrected Minimap preview `Shift+Click` combat feedback.
- **Quick Reply Combat & Keyboard-Capture Protection, Live Sync & Instant Outside-Interaction Countdown (`UI/Minimap.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Released EditBox keyboard focus and disabled input key capture immediately upon clicking outside Quick Reply (`GLOBAL_MOUSE_DOWN`), entering combat (`PLAYER_REGEN_DISABLED`), moving (`PLAYER_STARTED_MOVING`), casting (`UNIT_SPELLCAST_SENT`), opening Blizzard chat (`ChatEdit_ActivateChat`), or sending a message (`Enter` / `Send` / Quick Pings), and started the auto-close countdown immediately while preserving typed drafts.
  - Guaranteed live channel messages always record and refresh when Quick Reply or Conversations is viewing a channel (`viewingChan`), isolated shield/mention checks with `pcall`, and added a `lineID`-deduplicated official chat filter fallback (`OfficialChatShieldFilter` -> `OnChatEvent`).
### Removed
- **Oversized Minimap Hover Tooltip (`UI/Minimap.lua`):**
  - Removed the large summary tooltip popup on Minimap button hover so hovering the Minimap icon stays clean while preserving the orbital Fusion Circle split showing notification counts per category.

### Fixed
- **Quick Reply Position Persistence & Universal Dragging (`UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `AGENTS.md`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Added **Supreme Rule #0** in `AGENTS.md` and automated compliance checks; fixed `Theme:MakeMovable` / `AttachDragHandle` to initiate dragging immediately on `LeftButton` `OnMouseDown` on non-button surfaces, attached all structural surfaces of `SocialCentralFrame` and `QuickReply` (`header`, `chipsBar`, `msgBg`, `msgFrame`, `quickPingsBar`, `f`), paused auto-close during active drags, and normalized anchor restoration on `EndDrag` / `OnShow` / open calls.
- **Post-Login Combat `"Action Failed Due to an AddOn"` Guard (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`):**
  - Hardened Focus Watcher secret-value checks, deferred combat `SETTINGS_UPDATED` / `RefreshGameCalendar` / `RestoreSavedEditHistory` / embedded `LFG` attachment safely out of combat.
- **AAA Notification & Messaging Overhaul (`Core/Init.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - **Single-Surface Notification Routing:** Separated human conversations (Whispers, Battle.net, Direct Chat, Rooms, Alliance) to Minimap Previews cleanly by default (`addonMsgToast = false` opt-in) while keeping floating Notification Toasts dedicated to Room Invites (`RMI`), Mentions, Friend/Watchlist alerts, Clan LFG/Events, World Buffs, and Avoid warnings—eliminating duplicate on-screen popups while preserving taskbar flashes and `[SC]` chat echo.
  - **Combat Flow Protection & Post-Combat Resume:** Enabled combat retraction/hold by default (`msgPreviewSilenceCombat = true`) across both Minimap Previews (`combatHeldQueue`) and Notification Toasts (`Toast:LayoutStack`), hiding non-Avoid popups cleanly during combat and re-showing unread previews/toasts automatically once combat ends.

  - **Quick Reply 1-Click Instant Replies, Smart Draft-Safe Auto-Close, Distinct Urgency Chimes & `"New Messages"` Divider:** Added 3 1-click Quick Reply chips + full menu inside the Minimap Quick Reply popup, fixed auto-close so unsent drafts never close mid-thought while auto-closing cleanly after sending, differentiated notification `SOUNDKIT` audio signatures by urgency (Mentions vs Direct/Whispers vs Rooms/Alliance vs Channels), removed first-whisper setup modal interruption, and added `"── New Messages ──"` dividers across both Quick Reply and Conversations.

  - **World Buffs (`M` / `/sc`) & Group (`I` / `/sc`):** Replaced dropdown/isolated view switchers with 1-click view chips (`All Buffs` / `My Buffs` / `Drop History`, and `Group` / `Recent` / `Dungeon Log`), compressing World Buffs header height and moving secondary group tools into a compact `More v` menu.
  - **Conversations (`O`):** Added a narrow-mode `Focus Chat` / `Show Threads` header toggle (`pw < 540`) letting players collapse the thread list and dedicate 100% of the height to reading and replying inside `O`.
  - **Guild (`J`):** Reduced narrow-window sidebar width (`136 -> 118px`) and tightened sub-tab toolbar button spacing and search proportions so sub-tabs stay cleaner in compact hosts.
- **Compact Minimap Quick Reply Popup, 10s Auto-Close with Draft Recovery, Channel Toggles & Universal Movable Windows (`Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - **Minimap Smart Notification Click:** Clicking the Minimap icon while any enabled chat notification or saved draft is pending now opens the compact, 100% opaque **Quick Reply Popup** directly on the latest notification (only opening the main SocialCentral hub when zero notifications/drafts are pending).
  - **Quick Actions, 1-Click Chips, ESC/X Close, 10s Inactivity Auto-Close & Draft Persistence:** Includes `+` Invite to Group, `+ Watchlist`, `Open` (full conversation), multi-notification 1-click chips, ESC/`X` close, and an optional 10-second inactivity auto-close timer that pauses while typing/hovering and automatically saves unfinished text as a per-conversation draft restored on the next Minimap click.
  - **Per-Channel Notification Controls & Universal Window Dragging:** Added individual Settings toggles (Whispers, Battle.net, Party/Instance, Raid, Guild/Officer, plus 10s auto-close) with `[?]` help and Search integration, and ensured all addon popups/windows (`SocialCentralQuickReplyFrame`, `SocialCentralMinimapLeftChooser`, `SocialCentralMsgPreviewConfirmPopup`, and existing frames) are freely draggable anywhere on screen and saved in `windowPositions`.
### Changed
- **Social (`O`) Frontend Redesign — Hybrid Modular Hub (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - **Single-Line Top Toolbar (`filterBar`):** Eliminated multi-row toolbar wrapping inside the narrow `O` Friends window by keeping Search, Filter dropdown, Sort dropdown, compact `+` (`+ Friend`) button, and `[?]` help on one clean `26px` row.
  - **1-Click Quick Filter Chips Bar (`socialChipsBar`):** Added a slim `24px` 1-click filter strip (`All`, `Online`, `* Fav`, `[SC]`, `Guild`, plus `[x] Offline` pinned on the right) directly below the Search bar in the **Social** tab.
  - **Inline Quick-Inspect Drawer (Progressive Disclosure):** Clicking any player in **Social** expands a connected inline detail card immediately underneath displaying their full personal note, Battle.net/Guild/Main context, and 1-click `Alerts`/`Muted`, `Folder / Note`, `+ Watchlist`, and `...` buttons without opening popups; right-click continues opening the full actions menu.
  - **Cleaner Social Rows & Non-Overlapping Icon Actions:** Simplified Social sublines to prioritize Zone/Activity, Guild, and Folder, removed redundant `[Offline]` right-side brackets, and pinned compact `24x22` Whisper/Chat and Invite icon buttons cleanly on the right so row text never overlaps or jumps on hover.
- **Watchlist, Social & Conversations Section Organization, Yellow Star Favorites & Drag-Reorder Pinned Chats (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - **Strict SocialCentral-Only Favorites:** Decoupled SocialCentral favorites completely from Blizzard Battle.net favorites (`bnetFavorite` / `SetBNetFriendFavorite` no longer read or written), keeping favorites strictly limited to players marked through the addon and synced across Social, Watchlist, Conversations, Guild, Group, Nearby, and Minimap tooltips.
  - **Yellow Star Icon Across All Tabs:** Replaced `*` text markers with the yellow star icon (`Compat.FavoriteStarMarkup`) on every favorited player/character across Watchlist, Social, Conversations (list & header), Guild Roster/LFG/Professions, Group/Recent Companions, Nearby, New Chat modal, and Minimap tooltips.
  - **Collapsible Sections (`Favorites`, `Online`, `Guild`, `Offline`) in Watchlist & Conversations (plus `Guild` in Social):** Organized Watchlist (`All Groups` mode) and Conversations (`All` filter) into collapsible sections matching Social, with automatic expansion during text search.
  - **Drag-to-Reorder Pinned Conversations:** Added click-and-drag reordering (`SetupChatThreadDragReorder` + `Chat:MovePinnedThread`) for conversations inside the **Pinned** section of Conversations, persisting order per character (keeping `bn:` Battle.net threads session-only).
  - **Main `/sc` Window Drag & Resize Stability:** Added active-only mouse-release guards in `Theme:MakeMovable` and `SetupMainFrameResizeHandles` so resize state never locks dragging, expanded Blizzard chrome mouse suppression (`depth = 6` + click/motion flags) and title/portrait drag handles, and normalized UIParent relative scaling.
- **Group Finder Leader Name Resolution, Relationship Markings & Unified Custom Button Styling (`Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`):**
  - **Real Leader Name on Official LFG Rows:** Cleaned whitespace/unparseable leader candidates (`CleanOfficialLeaderCandidate` + `PickOfficialNameFromTable`), inspected all official group member slots before falling back, skipped stale 12s scan-cache throttling while a listing lacks a resolved leader name, promoted the inspected leader/member name post-scan cleanly across `Official LFG` rows, and cleaned `"official_*"` stale cache keys.
  - **Visible Watchlist, Guild, Allied Guild, Enemy Guild & Avoid Markings on LFG Rows:** Added left accent stripes (`relStripe`), separated Enemy Guild (`BattleMasterGossipIcon`) from Avoid (`UI-TargetingFrame-Skull`) icon badges, added inline colored status pills on row sublines and Bulletin Board dungeon headers (`watchCount`, `clanCount`, `allyCount`, `enemyCount`, `avoidCount`), and propagated leader `AVOID`/`GOOD` metadata consistently.
  - **Removed Standard WoW `UIPanelButtonTemplate` From SocialCentral Tabs & Windows:** Removed `_blizzBtn` (`UIPanelButtonTemplate`) completely from `UI/Theme.lua` so all primary/danger/CTA buttons across SocialCentral windows, embedded `[SC]` panels, and Settings render with the unified flat obsidian/crimson-gold custom theme rather than mixed WoW buttons.
- **Quick Offline Visibility Toggle, Cleaner Centered Section Headers & Offline Visual Dimming (`Core/Init.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - **Quick `[x] Offline` Toggle Beside `Sort: Status` / `Sort: Smart` & In Conversations Filter:** Added dedicated quick toggles (`watchOfflineBtn` next to `Sort: Status` in **Watchlist**, `socialOfflineBtn` next to `Sort: Smart` in **Social**, and `Show Offline` checkbox in **Conversations** filter dropdown). Unchecking hides offline characters everywhere AND removes the entire **Offline** category section cleanly.
  - **Cleaner Section Headers & Offline Contrast:** Centered collapsible section headers vertically without repetitive `"Click to collapse section"` sublines, replaced redundant `(0/3)` / `(1/1)` counts on uniform Online/Offline/Pinned headers with `(%d)`, and softly dimmed/desaturated offline player class icons and names so online players stand out immediately.
  - **Right-Aligned Badges, Hover Quick Actions, Clean Toolbars, Compact 1-Line Mode, Favorites Migration & Combat-Safe Standalone Hub (`Core/Init.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
    - **Mandatory Zero-Data-Loss Startup Migration (`Store:MigrateLegacyFavorites`):** Automatically reconciles and preserves all existing SocialCentral favorites across updates without overwriting saved metadata.
    - **Right-Aligned Badges & Hover Quick Actions:** Moved secondary bracket tags (`[SC]`, `[BNet]`, `[Faction]`, `[Group]`, `[Alt]`, `[Current Character]`) cleanly to the right edge (`row.metaRight`) and revealed Whisper/Invite action buttons smoothly on row hover (`row:_syncHover`) while keeping Watchlist & Social toolbars uncluttered (`Group` / `Sort` / `[x] Offline` / `?`).
    - **Optional 1-Line Compact Row Density (`compactListRows`):** Toggleable directly from the Social filter menu (`Compact (1L)`) and Settings (`Social (O), Watchlist (/who) & Nearby`), condensing Watchlist, Social, Nearby, Alts, and Guild Roster into crisp 1-line rows (`26px` / `28px`) via `Theme.CreateScrollList:SetRowHeight`.
    - **Safe Standalone Addon Access During Combat:** Opening or navigating to standalone SocialCentral tabs (`Watchlist`, `Social`, `Conversations`, `Guild`, `Group`, `Nearby`, `Buffs`, `Alts`) now opens cleanly even during combat (`UI:ShowHub`), reserving combat deferral strictly for Blizzard's own protected frames (`Settings` and Official Group Finder `I`) and `/who`.

## [0.2.23] - 2026-11-21
### Added
- **Target Radar Toast Display Duration (`10s` Default), Instant Teleport/Hearthstone Target Clear & Reorganized AddOn Settings (`Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added configurable **Target Radar toast display duration** (`spotterToastDuration`, **10 seconds by default**, adjustable `5s`–`60s` in **Settings -> Radar & Map** and searchable in `/sc`), automatically dismissing passive Target Radar detections after the chosen duration while pausing the timer whenever the target is actively selected in-game or the Radar HUD/drawer is hovered or open.
  - Added instant Target Radar clear (`Spotter:ClearTargetsOnLocationChange()`) on teleports, Hearthstone/Astral Recall/Teleport spell completions, portals, instance transitions, loading screens (`PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD`), and zone transitions (`ZONE_CHANGED_NEW_AREA`), plus strict `UnitMatchesEntry` validation on `visibleUnits` so recycled nameplate tokens in a new city/zone never keep old targets stuck on screen.
  - Reorganized **Settings (`/sc` & Blizzard `Options -> AddOns -> SocialCentral`)** with native WoW left-sidebar AddOn subcategories (`Settings.RegisterCanvasLayoutSubcategory`), a pinned **Quick Jump to Section** bar (`1..N`) at the top of every tab with `"↑ Top"` return buttons, numbered section headers, clean field labels (`CreateFieldLabel`), and logical section consolidation (`Radar Monitored Categories`, unified `World Buffs & World Map (M)`, and `Social (O), Watchlist (/who) & Nearby`).
- **Native Damage Meter (`C_DamageMeter`) & Full Built-In Combat/Progress Metrics in Dungeon & Raid Log (`Providers/Group.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Integrated WoW: Forever `16001`'s built-in **Blizzard Damage Meter (`C_DamageMeter`)** directly into every recorded Dungeon & Raid run (100% native and event-driven, zero `COMBAT_LOG_EVENT_UNFILTERED`, handling `DAMAGE_METER_RESET` mid-run via baseline delta + per-pull fallback) capturing **Damage Done**, **DPS**, **Healing Done**, **HPS**, **Damage Taken**, **Interrupts**, **Dispels**, and **Deaths** for both group totals and each ranked teammate.
  - Added built-in native WoW run metrics captured via state transitions: **Instance Difficulty**, **Active Combat Time & Pull Count**, **Group Deaths & Wipes**, **Player XP Gained & Level-Ups**, **Gold Looted**, and up to 8 **Notable Loot Drops** (searchable alongside dungeon, character, bosses, and teammates), rendered as clean row badges, ranked hover tooltip leaderboards, first-run modal totals, and 1-click **"Copy Damage Meter Breakdown"** (`UI/SocialPanels.lua`).
- **Unified Social Ecosystem Upgrades, Official Window `[SC]` Controls & Interactive Integrations (`GAP-SOC-01/02/05/07`, `GAP-CHAT-04/05`, `GAP-GUILD-02/05/06`, `GAP-LFG-04`, `GAP-ALT-05`, `GAP-WB-01/02`):**
  - **Social (`O`) & Official Popups:** Added collapsible folder grouping (`Favorites`, `Online Friends`, `Offline`, custom folders) in **Social (`O`)**, **Personal Nicknames** in Social Cards rendered across lists/tooltips/Official Chat, versioned **`SC1` Export / Import** backup modal (`/sc export` and `/sc` General), **Preventive Official Popup Alert Banners** above incoming Blizzard Party Invite, Trade, and Duel dialogs from Account-Ignored or Avoid-flagged players (`SocialCentralPopupAlertBanner` with quick `[SC]` toggle), and standardized **`[SC]`** quick-control buttons across **`O`**, **`J`**, **`C`**, and **`M`**.
  - **Chat & Guild Alliance (`ChatFrame1` / `J`):** Added **GreenWall Official Chat Mirror** echoing Alliance Guild chat directly into `ChatFrame1` alongside `/ali` and `/ally` slash aliases, automatic Main extraction from Guild Public Notes (`Alt de X` / `Main: X`) displayed on rosters & tooltips, **Boolean (`+must` / `-exclude`)** keyword rules with clickable Official Chat `[Whisper (/w)]` alerts, **{rt1}..{rt8}** Raid Icon rendering & composer picker across Official Chat and Conversations, and **Guild Roster CSV Export**.
  - **Group Buff Auditor, Alt Equipment Audit & World Map (`I` / `O -> Grupo` / `C` / `M`):** Added `C_UnitAuras`-powered **Missing Raid Buffs Auditor** (`Audit Buffs` button in `Group` + automatic summary on official `Ready Check` with clickable chat draft link), **Missing Enchants & Durability % Audit** inside `Meus Alts -> Equip.`, **9 Felwood Songflower node pins** + always-scheduled **Darkmoon Faire pin** on `WorldMapFrame` (`M`), and **Capital World Buff Ready Alerts** outside combat when tracked Onyxia/Nefarian/Rend/Stormwind cooldowns finish.
- **Group Finder Bulletin Board Mode, Official Chat LFG Watchers, Anti-Lockout/Full-Group Filters & Editable Whisper Template (`GAP-LFG-01` + `GAP-LFG-02`) (`Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added collapsible **Bulletin Board Mode** (`lfgBulletinMode`) inside **Group Finder (`I`) -> Browse**, automatically grouping active listings under dungeon, raid, and quest headers with level spans, active open-slot summaries (`T / H / D`), trusted/good-partner counters, Saved lockout badges, Watched indicators, and expand/collapse memory (`collapsedBuckets`), plus a quick header toolbar icon toggle.
  - Added **LFG Watchers in Official Chat** (`lfgWatchersEnabled` + `lfgWatchedActivities`), allowing players to mark dungeons/raids from any Bulletin Board header, the Activity dropdown, the `[SC]` quick-control menu (both Official Tab 1 Mirror Bar and Browse header), or `/sc` Settings; matching Official LFG or public/clan chat listings print discreet Official Chat alerts (`MaybeTriggerLfgWatcher`, `120s` per-leader cooldown, skipping self/`AVOID`/Enemy/`AccountIgnore`) with clickable `[Apply (/w)]` (`addon:SCentral:lfgapply:`) and `[View in LFG]` hyperlinks plus optional toast & chime.
  - Added **Hide Full Groups** (`lfgHideFullGroups`, hiding capped LFM groups or groups with `0` open slots for the selected role filter) and **Hide Saved Lockouts** (`lfgHideSavedLockouts`, keeping self listings visible), synchronized across `/sc` Settings, Browse dropdown menus, and the standardized `[SC]` quick-control button in `I`.
  - Added the **Editable Apply Whisper Template (`/w`)** (`lfgWhisperTemplate` + `SocialCentralLFGWhisperTemplateModal`) supporting `{level}`, `{class}`, `{role}`, `{ilvl}`, and `{activity}` tokens, 1-click quick presets (*Standard*, *Short*, *With Gear/iLvl*), and a live preview inside a 100%-opaque ESC-closable modal.
- **Global Alt & Guild Learned Recipe Catalog + Direct Official Item Tooltips & Work Order Queries (`GAP-ALT-01` + `GAP-GUILD-01`) (`Core/Init.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/Alts.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added passive, low-footprint learned recipe capturing across all Forever `16001` profession engines (`C_TradeSkillUI`, legacy `GetNumTradeSkills`, and `GetNumCrafts`) backed by a globally shared `SocialCentralDB.recipeCatalog` and compact per-alt recipe key strings (with safe orphan pruning in `CompactSavedAlts` and active-filter merge protection).
  - Enhanced official WoW `GameTooltip` & `ItemRefTooltip` item hovers (Bags, Bank, Auction House, Mail, Merchant, and Chat links):
    - Displays **`Craftable by Alt:`** (`World` & `Faction` scoped) and cached/queried **`In Guild:`** crafters directly on any craftable item tooltip.
    - Detects Recipe scrolls (`classID == 9`) across EN/PT/ES titles and immediately displays **`Recipe already known by:`** and **`Can still be learned by:`** Alts who have that profession.
  - Attached the standardized `[SC]` quick-control button directly on Blizzard's Official Profession windows (`ProfessionsFrame` / `TradeSkillFrame` / `CraftFrame`) with live indexed recipe counts, quick tooltip/guild-reply toggles, direct navigation to Alt & Guild recipes, and 1-click **"Disable in this window"**.
  - Added **`Recipes`** filter view inside **My Alts (`C`) -> Items** (plus **`/sc recipes [item]`**, Spell tooltip fallback, material requirements summary, and **"Find crafters in Guild (J)"** right-click jump).
  - Upgraded **Guild (`J`) -> Professions** with rate-limited on-demand **`CRQ`/`CRP`** guild recipe search (**`Ask Guild`** button + Enter key, `>= 8s` cooldown, Trickle response suppression, `<= 225B` opt-in `clanSync` payloads), matching-recipe badges on crafter rows, and recipe-specific pre-filled **`Request Craft`** whispers.
- **Official Chat Enhancements & Standardized Official-Window `[SC]` Quick-Control Button (`GAP-CHAT-01`) (`Core/Init.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Providers/Alts.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added a standardized, theme-aware `[SC]` quick-control button (`Theme:CreateOfficialQuickButton`, with live active/partial/off status dot, crisp hover tooltip, feature toggles, `Hide quick [SC] button here`, `Disable in this window`, and `Settings` jump) mounted beside Blizzard's **Official Chat (`ChatFrame1`)**, **Official Mailbox (`SendMailFrame`)**, and **Group Finder (`I`) Tab 1 Mirror Bar**.
  - Added taint-free **Official Chat (`ChatFrame1..N` & `EditBox`) enhancements** controllable anytime via `[SC]` beside chat or `/sc` (`Summary & Windows` and `General & Tools`):
    - **Short public channel labels** (`[LFG]`, `[General]`, `[Trade]`, `[Defense]`, `[WorldDefense]`, `[LocalDefense]`, `[LookingForGroup]`, stripping verbose `- Zone/City` suffixes safely via `AddMessageEventFilter`).
    - **Clickable inline sender context badges** (`[+]`, `[AVOID]`, `[Main]`, `[Lv.X]`) read strictly from in-memory caches (friends/guild/Social notes, zero automatic `/who`).
    - **Copy Official Chat (`/sc copychat [tab]`)** opening a clean `Ctrl+C` window with the latest captured lines from the active chat frame.
    - **Persistent `EditBox` typed history** across `/reload` (up to 25 sent lines per character, automatically skipping Battle.net privacy tokens) and **Fast Chat Scrolling** (`Shift+MouseWheel` top/bottom, `Ctrl+MouseWheel` 4x page step).
  - Added dedicated **Official Window Toggles** (`Official Chat (ChatFrame)`, `Official Mailbox (Alts)`, `Show Official Tab 1 Mirror Bar in Group Finder (I)`), Settings Search synonyms, `[?]` help buttons, and EN/PT/ES localization parity.
- **Dungeon & Raid Group History Log (1st Pull to End, Ultra-Lightweight Event-Driven Engine) (`Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/Group.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Added automatic, event-driven recording of started dungeon and raid groups (`SocialCentralDB.dungeonHistory`, capped at 80 compact runs, zero `COMBAT_LOG_EVENT_UNFILTERED`, zero polling) tracking every teammate's name, class, role, level, guild, mid-run joins/early leaves, defeated bosses (`ENCOUNTER_END`), and total run time from the **1st enemy pulled** (`PLAYER_REGEN_DISABLED` / `ENCOUNTER_START`) until a member leaves after finishing, the group disbands, the player exits alive (ignoring ghost/corpse runs and mid-run replacements that resume combat), or the instance resets.
  - Added the searchable, filterable (`All` / `Dungeons` / `Raids` / `This Character`) **Dungeon & Raid Log** right inside the main addon window (**Group -> Dungeon Log**), directly accessible from **`/sc`** (Overview quick-jump + `Record started dungeon & raid groups` toggle + `Open Dungeon & Raid Log` button) and via **`/sc runs`** (`/sc dungeons` / `/sc masmorras`), with full hover tooltips, teammate quick-actions, copyable run summaries, and confirmation-guarded deletion/clear.
  - Added a one-time account-wide completion reminder popup (`SocialCentralDungeonLogPromptModal`, deferred safely outside combat via `ns:RunAfterCombat`) shown after finishing the player's first dungeon/raid so they can view the summary, open the log, keep recording, or disable recording in 1 click.
- **Anti-Spam Message Notification Sound (6s Default, Minimum 3s Cooldown) (`Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Enabled crisp incoming message notification chimes across Whispers, Battle.net whispers, Direct Addon chats, Private Rooms, Alliance Chat, Mentions, and optional Guild/Group channels (even when channel mirroring is off), with **6 seconds default** (`>= 3s` minimum floor) global + per-sender anti-spam cooldown so message bursts never repeat the chime rapidly.
  - Added **Minimum interval between message sounds (anti-spam)** (`3s`–`30s`, default `6s`) and a **`Test Message Sound`** button in **Settings -> Notifications** (with `[?]` help tooltips and Settings Search support), and wired Minimap Preview test buttons (`Test Queue (4)`) to demonstrate the anti-spam chime.
- **Phase 1 — Mentions & Custom Keywords Radar, Unlimited Account Ignore, Public Anti-Spam Shield, Clickable Links & Upgraded Chat Search/Quick Replies (`Core/Init.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Group.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - **Mentions & Custom Keywords Radar:** Passively monitors chosen chat channels (Guild/Officer, Party/Raid/Instance, Say/Yell, and Public channels) for your character name, personal nicknames/alt names, and comma-separated custom keywords (accent- and case-insensitive), firing a below-chat Notification Toast (with crossed-bell silence confirmation), optional audio chime, and logging matches in the dedicated `Mentions Radar` view inside Conversations (`Mentions` filter, clickable sender reply links, quick `Keywords` config modal, and `/sc mentions [config|test]`).
  - **Unlimited Account Ignore & Optional Public Chat Anti-Spam Shield:** Added account-wide unlimited ignores (`SocialCentralDB.accountIgnores`, zero BattleTag/Real ID storage) with reason tags (`Spammer`, `Scammer`, `Ninja Loot`, `Toxic`, `Other`), optional duration (`24h`, `7d`, `30d`, `Permanent`), notes, automatic official chat hiding, `Account Ignores` filter in Social (`O`), right-click unit/row menus, player hover tooltip warnings, Group Avoid & Target Radar integration, and an opt-in Public Chat Anti-Spam Shield (blocking RMT/gold-seller/boost spam, custom blocked words, and 45s stranger flood while strictly whitelisting Friends/Guild/Group) backed by a transparent `Filtered Chat Log` audit modal (`/sc spam` and `/sc ignore [name]`).
  - **Clickable Discord/Web URLs, 1-Click Conversation Export, Accent-Insensitive Global Search Highlight & Categorized Quick Replies:** Automatically converts `https://...`, `http://...`, `discord.gg/...`, and `www....` links into clickable `[Copy Link]` hyperlinks in both Conversations and official WoW chat, adds a 1-click `Copy Conversation` icon in the active chat header, upgrades Conversations search to accent-insensitive multi-match counting with inline `[»]` message highlights, and enriches Quick Replies with categorized presets (*Group*, *Trade & Craft*, *Guild*, *Busy / AFK*) and live `{zone}` / `{level}` / `{class}` placeholders.
- **Crossed-Bell Mute Button & Confirmation Dialog on Minimap Message Previews (`UI/Minimap.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Added the crossed-bell (`bell-off.tga`) quick-silence button beside `[X]` on every Minimap Message Preview card (and routed right-click preview category muting through the same guard) opening a 100%-opaque top-strata confirmation popup (`SocialCentralMsgPreviewConfirmPopup`, ESC-closable, pausing countdowns while open) with choices to disable that preview channel type, silence only that sender/thread, pause all via Do Not Disturb, or cancel.

### Fixed
- **Full Addon Programming Logic & Mathematics Audit Fixes (78 Verified Fixes Across Core, Providers & UI) (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `Providers/WorldBuffs.lua`, `Providers/Group.lua`, `Providers/Nearby.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Providers/Alts.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`):**
  - **Group Finder (`I`), Guild (`J`) & Calendar Math:** Fixed multi-return truncation in `ScanSingleOfficialSearchResult` and `InspectOfficialGroupMembers` so non-self Official LFG listings always keep their real leader/member names; fixed `Safe.Bool` quest-log reader in `Clan:GetActiveGroupQuests`; eliminated the 3-cycle strict-weak-ordering violation in `GetClanLFGList`; fixed 1st-return `lookupKey` unpacking in `IsSenderConfirmedInGuild` and `GetMemberGuildRank`; fixed 0-based `rankIndex` vs 1-based `GetGuildRankOrder`/`SetGuildRankOrder` off-by-one across `IsGuildLeader`, `CanInviteToGuild`, `GetSelfGuildRank`, `PromoteMember`, `DemoteMember`, and `SetMemberRank`; added 15s TTL to `_heardIntelRev` (`CREQ`) and `_heardGuildEvtSig` relay suppression; scheduled follow-up `LFG~`/`LFX` packets after `TAG~` in `SetMyStatus` and staggered `PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD` syncs; sequentially capped role deficits (`needT + needH + needD <= openSlots`) and included role overflow in 5-man slot icons; fixed pixel-based scroll offset (`(foundPendingIdx - 1) * rowHeight`), extended lockout detection (`lk.locked or lk.extended`), recycled `nameFs:SetWidth(0)` measurement, `roleFilter == "MINE"` in `IsItemFullForBrowse`, word-boundary category matching, embedded tab badge coalescing, and all 5 calendar/event date/time parsers.
  - **Target Radar, World Buffs (`M`), Group & Nearby:** Replaced undefined `IsInInstanceCached()` with `IsInInstanceFast()` in `ClanSpotter:InspectUnit`; coerced `onScreen` to boolean in Radar sort comparators; migrated manual tracking state in `InspectChatSighting`; updated non-protected HUD identity/stance visuals during combat target switches and fixed `Theme.PALETTES` border/surface keys; decoupled `worldBuffsExpiryAlert` from `worldBuffsReadyAlert`, scheduled expiry/ready timers on local/synced drops (`elapsed >= -120`), normalized `GetPeakWindowText` tie-breaker, made `hist._last` monotonic, and added a trailing `UNIT_AURA` flush after Chronoboon unboon; replaced Lua 5.1 single-byte accent character classes in `FoldDungeonSearchText` with multi-byte UTF-8 mapping; resolved full Forever names/GUIDs in `READY_CHECK_CONFIRM` and `raidMetaByIndex`; fixed active dungeon run `memberCount` (`count + 1`), `row.guild` lookup, and 40-man raid early-finalization threshold; and enforced fail-closed faction/map checks in `Nearby`.
  - **Social (`O`), Watchlist, Alts (`C`), Chat & UI Surfaces:** Fixed `SetAccountIgnore` (`hideChat = false`), `Store:GetAccountIgnore` multi-return unblocking via linked alts, and `ns.Safe` in Account Ignore `Target`; fixed `GetPlayerAffinityScore` reading `th.lastAt`; preserved `socialFolderFilter == "__none"` (*Unassigned*); prevented offline Battle.net scans from overwriting live Friend/Guild online state in `Social:Scan`; restricted bare-`firstName` Watchlist fast-index entries to sourceless/no-surname records and fixed VOI scheduler passive penalties & offline age cap; fixed `Alts` TradeSkill/Craft debounce, `BuildRecipeKey` multi-recipe output collisions, `_recipeIndexCache` invalidation, `GetXPExhaustion() == nil` (`0%` rested), `AccountMatrix` `0%`/`1%` XP calculation, dual-faction Gold Breakdown tooltip denominator, and `INVTYPE_HOLDABLE` off-hand enchant audit; fixed `Chat:LinkifyUrls` outside hyperlinks, `CHAT_MSG_BN_WHISPER_INFORM` recipient verification, transitive `GetThreadsList` sorting, punctuated mention keywords, `{clase}`/`{nível}` quick replies, 7th return of `GetChatWindowInfo`, Private Room BNet target cleanup, `btnRaidMark` & `btnAuditBuffs` layout anchoring, Notification Toast `_lastProgressW` / `activeIndex` pagination / HUD drag detach, Minimap preview midpoint drag math (`cardW * 0.5`), and Settings Search / choice-row module dependencies.
- **Forever `16001` Cluster/Shard Scope Persistence, Watchlist Capitalization & Embedded `O` Header Spacing (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Collapsed all dynamic WoW: Forever shard/cluster prefixes (`CAMWOWUS1...PvP`, `CAMWOWUS2...PvP`, `CAMWOWSA1...PvP`, etc.) into canonical ruleset pools (`camwowrealmpvp`, `camwowrealmpve`, `camwowrealmrppvp`, `camwowrealmrppve`, `camwowrealmrp`), fixed `Compat.Realm()` so Forever's surname return from `UnitFullName("player")` can never impersonate a server realm or strip a player's surname when `GetNormalizedRealmName()`/`GetRealmName()` are `nil`, added non-destructive SavedVariables fallback & self-healing rescue (`Store:EnsureScopedBucket`, `Store:InvalidateScopeCaches`, `surnamePoolSet` / `::guild` / `worldBuffsPersonal` / `alts` repair across `Store.lua`, `Compat.lua`, `Alts.lua`, `WorldBuffs.lua`, `Watchlist.lua`, `Chat.lua`, and `Init.lua`), and removed automatic time-based expiration from saved Conversations and verified `[SC]` peers (raising retention limits across Recent Companions, Friend Presence, Guild Events, and Dungeon History) so updates and breaks never discard user data.
  - Added UTF-8-aware name capitalization (`Compat.CapitalizeNameWord`) inside `ParseCharacterName` / `ParseForeverFullName`, adopted canonical in-game casing from `Social` directory / live observations in `Watchlist:Add`, `Watchlist:NormalizeStoredEntries`, and `Store:SyncWatchlistObservation`, and fixed row/menu `Watchlist:Add` call sites in `UI/MainFrame.lua` and `UI/SocialPanels.lua` so adding friends (whether typed in lowercase or added from Social/Nearby/Guild/Group) always preserves proper Title Case (including accented letters).
  - Fixed embedded **Friends (`O`)** header tab overlap (`Por Perto` running under `?` and `[SC]`) by dynamically measuring visible right-side header controls inside `AnchorEmbeddedTabBar` (`_scTargetAvailW`), lowering `ReflowTabStrip`'s compact shrink threshold, hiding the embedded-only `[SC]` quick-control button in the standalone Hub header, and including personal **Nicknames** in both Social and Watchlist search filters.
- **Third-Party Addon (`OldFriendsList`) & Native Pane Mouse/Tooltip Isolation Across Embedded Windows (`O`, `I`, `J`, `C`, `M`) (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Expanded `SuppressSocialHostPanes` to cover Forever `16001`'s `SocialUIFrame.FriendsList` (plus `.IgnoreList`, `.WhoList`, `.GuildList`, `.RaidList`, `.QuickJoin`, `.RecruitAFriend`, and dynamic content-area children below the top BattleTag header bar) at `alpha = 0` (preserving 2nd-press `O` close toggle).
  - Upgraded `SuppressHostSubtreeMouse` and added `SuppressedFrameIsMouseOver` + `InstallEmbeddedTooltipGuard` (post-hooking `GameTooltip:SetOwner` / `GameTooltip:Show` via `IsSuppressedHostFrameOrDescendant` and `_G.FriendsTooltip` `OnShow`, plus `0.16s` delayed re-suppression after opening and uniform subtree suppression across `O`, `I`, `J`, `C`, and `M`) so third-party 20Hz `IsMouseOver()` hover tickers (such as `OldFriendsList`'s integrated cards) and hidden native panes can never steal `GameTooltip` or intercept mouse/scroll events while SocialCentral's embedded tabs are active.
- **LFG Window (`I`) Memory Churn Optimization & Embedded Friends (`O`) Tooltip/Hover Isolation (`UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Eliminated runaway Lua heap growth (~`105+ MB`) when Blizzard's Group Finder (`I`) was open by removing redundant Blizzard `GenerateMenu()` dropdown tree/closure rebuilds during label syncs (switching to `OverrideText` + cached labels), memoizing official LFG categories/activities/fold strings/lockouts/group quests, incrementally updating only dirty `LFG_LIST_SEARCH_RESULT_UPDATED` result IDs with scan-signature skipping, converting `GetTrustedLFGBreakdown()` into an in-place zero-clone counter, reusing scratch tables across member/row inspection, coalescing refresh cascades, and running idle GC steps on LFG close/scans.
  - Fixed native `FriendsTooltip` (`FriendsListFrame`) & BNet friend row highlight bleed-through when hovering SocialCentral Watchlist/Social rows embedded inside Blizzard's Friends (`O`) window (e.g., showing another Blizzard friend's offline tooltip while hovering a Watchlist row): alpha-zeroed (`alpha = 0`, preserving 2nd-press `O` close toggle) and mouse/wheel-disabled underlying native `FriendsListFrame`/`FriendsTabHeader`/`IgnoreListFrame`/`WhoFrame` and `CharacterFrame` panes, hid stale `FriendsTooltip` popups cleanly, deepened host frame-level scanning, and blocked empty scroll viewport pass-through.
- **Anti-Jitter Layout Stabilization Across `My Alts -> Items` & All Addon Panels (`UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Fixed horizontal trembling/reflow feedback loops on the **My Alts (`C`) -> Items** toolbar (`Search items...`, `Recipes v`, `All Alts (All Worlds)`, `List`) when window dimensions sat near the 1-row/2-row wrap threshold or during UI scale subpixel rounding: added integer button width caching (`_scAutoFitW`), 16px wrapping hysteresis (`_scSingleRow`), layout-signature caching (`_scToolbarSig`, `_scSubTabAnchorSig`), consistent right-pane width sync from `RelayoutAltsPanel`, widened `All Alts (All Worlds)` cap (`184 -> 212`) so long localized text clears button borders cleanly, and preserved scrollbar/scroll-container anchor rects across refreshes.
  - Audited and applied the same anti-jitter invariants across all sibling UI controllers and Settings (`Theme` text offsets/input backdrops/scrollbars, `Group`, `Chat`, `Buffs`, `Clan`, standalone `TabStrip` / `FilterBar` / `WatchBar` / `UI:Refresh`, and `/sc` Settings `scrollFrame:OnSizeChanged`), backed by regression checks in `scripts/verify-compliance.py`.
- **Professional UI Streamlining & Redundant Control Cleanup (`UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Removed the leftover `Announce in Chat` action from World Buff floating notification toasts (clicking the toast navigates directly to Buffs), removed the redundant `Post in Chat` top-bar button from **Group** (keeping `Broadcast Group` and `Ready Check`), and replaced the 2-option `+ Room v` dropdown with direct 1-click Private Room creation (removing the confusing `Temp Channel` modal).
  - Streamlined **Conversations** by keeping `Copy Conversation` and `Mark Read`/`Mark Unread` inside the header actions gear menu (`...`) instead of dedicated chrome buttons and removing the manual ping refresh icon next to automatic peer status verification.
  - Streamlined **My Alts (`C`)** by consolidating `Armory` inside `Actions v` so character headers display long two-word names cleanly without truncation, and unified **Guild (`J`)** manual sync controls (`Sync`, `Refresh`, `Sync Intel`, `Sync Hierarchy Rules`) into the single `Sync` action inside `My Status v` (preserving strict `>= 5s` cooldown/combat gating across guild tags, Allied events, Calendar, and guild intel).
  - Simplified **`/sc` Settings** by removing the duplicated `Quick Notification Switches` block from **Overview** (kept `Do Not Disturb`, auto-silence, and dedicated alert controls on **Notifications**) and trimming QA/tutorial-replay button clutter across **Notifications** and **Radar & Map**.
- **Cleaner World Buffs Panel & Direct Map Tracker Click (`UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Removed the redundant per-row `Report` (`Informar`) button and header `Post in Chat` (`Anunciar Chat`) button from the **Buffs** tab so every buff row displays full-width source and cooldown details without truncation, added a complete hover tooltip per buff row, and changed World Map (`M`) Buffs badge/pin left-clicks to open the Buffs tracker directly instead of pre-filling chat.
- **Full-Surface Side-Tab Clickability & Z-Order Across Character (`C`), Social (`O`), LFG (`I`) & Guild (`J`) (`UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Fixed an issue on `CharacterFrame` (`C`) where the upper half of the SocialCentral right-side bookmark tab could be blocked or unclickable until clicking near the bottom edge, and hardened all 4 embedded host side tabs (`C`, `O`, `I`, `J`) uniformly.
  - Added `SyncRightSideTabHitboxAndLayer` (syncing full button bounds to `lastNative` and `common-sidetab` atlas dimensions, explicitly resetting `SetHitRectInsets(0, 0, 0, 0)`, matching highest host/tab strata, and keeping the addon side tab idempotently elevated strictly above host chrome, vertical tab containers, and native tabs/descendants), pruned nested/container frames in `DiscoverRightSideTabs`, improved bottom-up non-overlapping `MeasureTabGap`, re-synced tab layout on `ShowSubFrame`/`UpdateTabs`/`ResetTabs`/`CharacterFrameTab_OnClick`/`PanelTemplates_SetTab`, and aligned `ElevateEmbeddedHostChrome` to elevate addon side tabs to `minControlLevel + 35`.
- **Auto-Clear Minimap Unread Counters, Previews & Toasts When Replying in Official WoW Chat (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Replying via official Blizzard chat (`/w`, `/r`, Battle.net whisper, `/p`, `/ra`, `/rw`, `/i`, `/g`, `/o`) now immediately marks that conversation/channel (and any linked Battle.net/character sibling thread) as read, persists `unread = 0`, clears per-thread Minimap preview counters (`previewCountsByThread`), smoothly collapses and purges matching active/queued Minimap Message Previews without re-incrementing badges, dismisses matching floating Notification Toasts, and refreshes Minimap Fusion/Orbital badges and side-tab counters instantaneously.

## [0.2.22] - 2026-11-19
### Added
- **Below-Chat Single-Card Paginated Notification Toast (`1/N`), Configurable 6s Timer & Account-Wide One-Time Tutorial (`Core/Init.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Repositioned the floating **Notification Toast** (`UI/NotificationToast.lua`, distinct from the Minimap Message Previews in `UI/Minimap.lua`) to appear right below the chat window (`ChatFrame1` / `ChatFrame1EditBox`) by default (with automatic one-time position migration `toastBelowChatMigrated`, and automatic vertical/horizontal offset when `SocialCentralSpotterFrame` is also shown below chat).
  - Enforced **single-card display (`MAX_VISIBLE_TOASTS = 1`)** with a Target-Radar-style pagination header (`<< < 1 2 3 4 5 > >> 1/N` + mouse wheel cycling) whenever 2 or more notifications are active, so notifications never stack vertically on screen.
  - Set default auto-dismiss duration to **6 seconds per notification** (`toastDuration = 6`, pausing on hover, combat, or confirmation/tutorial popups and advancing through remaining notifications one by one), configurable from `3s` to `15s` in **Settings -> Notifications** alongside **`Test Toast Pagination (3)`** and **`Toast Tutorial`** buttons.
  - Added a one-time account-wide tutorial dialog (`SocialCentralToastTutorialDialog`) on first notification appearance and synchronized all addon tutorial flags (`toastTutorialShown`, `spotterPlateTutorialShown`, `spotterRadarTutorialShown`, `msgPreviewPromptAnswered`, `themeChosen`, `shortcutPromptAnswered`) into account-wide `SocialCentralDB.accountTutorials` so tutorials never repeat across different characters of the same player.

### Fixed
- **Idempotent Frame-Level Ceiling & Minimap Notification Chat Open Layout (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Eliminated a runaway frame-level feedback loop where `MaxHierarchyFrameLevel(frame.blizzChrome, 3)` included `blizzChrome.CloseButton` and `RaiseWindowCloseButton` added `+50` on every `ApplyTheme()` / `OnShow` / deferred pass, eventually clamping `interactiveBase` and all standalone window sections (`header`, `tabBar`, `content`) at WoW's `10000` ceiling and causing opaque card/button backdrops to draw over tab labels (`Grupo`, `Buffs`), header buttons (`Configurações`, `[?]`), conversation rows, action buttons, and chat messages.
  - Eliminated the matching embedded-host frame-level feedback loop in `ScanEmbeddedHostContentMax` across `O`, `I`, `J`, `C`, and `M` by tracking elevated Blizzard chrome via a taint-free weak-key table (`elevatedHostChrome`), skipping all `embedContainers` and addon side tabs, and enforcing bounded, idempotent level ceilings.
  - Added shared `Theme:SyncSubtreeLayers` across `Theme:PrepareTopOverlayModal`, virtual scroll list row updates (`Theme:CreateScrollList`), LFG host switching (`ShowSubPanel`), and remaining shared modals (`EnsureModal`, `OpenConfirmModal`, `OpenDeleteThreadModal`, `EnsureAltRemoveModal`), synchronized `Theme:SyncButtonLayers` after creating Blizzard button plates in `ApplyButtonColors`, and aligned `UI:ShowHub("chat")` to reset active conversation filters cleanly when jumping from Minimap notifications.

## [0.2.21] - 2026-11-19
### Added
- **Chat-Colored Backgrounds, Prismatic Multi-Color Fusion Orb & Non-Overlapping Orbital Minimap Counters (`Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Applied authentic WoW chat colors uniformly across **Minimap Message Previews** (100%-opaque rich channel gradients), **Notification Toasts**, **Conversations** unread thread pills, **Embedded Side Tabs (`O`, `I`, `J`)**, and **Minimap Notification Counters**: **Whisper Pink**, **Battle.net Cyan**, **Group/Party Blue**, **Raid Orange**, **Guild Green**, and **Private Room Violet**.
  - Added **Freely Movable 3-Row Preview Stack Anywhere on Screen (`minimap_msg_preview`)**: players can drag any active preview card directly or toggle the 100%-opaque 3-slot placement guide via **Settings -> Notifications -> Move Preview Position**, preview right-click menu, or `/sc preview move` (and reset via **Reset Preview Position**, `/sc preview resetpos`, or `/sc resetpos`), while every preview continues launching from and arcing cleanly back into the SC minimap icon to trigger the Prismatic Fusion burst.
  - Added the **Prismatic Multi-Color Fusion Circle** directly centered beneath the SocialCentral minimap icon: when unread messages arrive from 2+ chat types (or when message previews fold back into the SC icon), colored badges briefly orbit around the icon without overlapping, spiral inward, and burst into a single smoothly blended, gently swirling multi-color circle (e.g., Pink + Blue + Green) with crisp chat-sized (`>= 15pt`) white digits.
  - Hovering over the SC minimap icon smoothly blooms the fused circle outward into mathematically spaced, zero-overlap orbital category counters around the icon (re-fusing on leave); added **Minimap Counter Badge Style** (`Fusion Circle (Under Icon)` vs `Around Icon (Separate)`) + **`Test Fusion Badge`** in **Settings -> Notifications** and `/sc preview badge [fusion|all|whisper|group|guild|bnet|room|clear]`.
- **Professional Multi-Tab Settings Search (`UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added a pinned, responsive search bar (`SocialCentralSettingsSearchBox`) across all four Settings tabs with UTF-8 accent-insensitive matching, 23 multilingual (EN/PT/ES) gameplay synonym clusters, multi-word weighted relevance ranking, and warm-gold title highlighting.
  - Renders pooled, theme-aware **interactive result cards** with tab breadcrumbs, tab filter chips (`All` + per-tab match counts), direct inline editing (toggle checkboxes, switch choice pills, adjust `-`/`+` steppers, and trigger actions directly from search results), module-disabled indicators, `[?]` help tooltips, a one-click **`Go to`** locator that switches tabs, auto-scrolls directly to the setting, and pulses a 2.4-second golden highlight beacon around it, plus quick-topic chips on empty results.
- **Animated Minimap Message Preview Stack (Up to 3 Simultaneous Rows + FIFO Queue + 6s Default) & First-Time Setup Prompt (`Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added a compact, 100%-opaque animated preview stack vertically centered on the left side of the Minimap supporting **1 to 3 simultaneous rows** (`msgPreviewMaxVisible`, default `3`, newest at the bottom) with a FIFO queue (`+N` badge) that smoothly glides lower previews upward and promotes the 4th+ queued message as soon as the top preview finishes its **6s default** countdown (`3s`–`12s` configurable) and morphs back into the SC minimap icon.
  - Displays the sender's character icon (live portrait when visible, race/gender face portrait, class crest, or Battle.net emblem) along with their name, channel tag, and the opening words of the incoming message, pausing on hover (showing full text in tooltip).
  - Added a one-time top-strata interactive setup dialog (`SocialCentralMsgPreviewSetupModal`) triggered on the first incoming message (safely deferred out of combat) letting the player choose whether to enable previews, select channels (**Whispers & Direct Chat**, **Battle.net Friends**, **Group / Raid**, **Guild / Officer**), and automatically silence previews inside **Dungeons, Raids, Battlegrounds/Arenas**, or **Combat**.
  - Added full configuration under **Settings -> Notifications -> Minimap Message Previews** (with `[?]` help tooltips beside every control, live single & 4-message queue test buttons, setup wizard button, right-click channel muting, and `/sc preview [whisper|bnet|group|guild|room|queue|setup|clear]`), cleanly respecting Focus Mode (`Do Not Disturb` / `Auto-silence in Instances`) and `Conversations (Chat)` module isolation.

## [0.2.20] - 2026-11-19
### Fixed
- **Standalone Blizzard Chrome Header/Portrait, 1:1 Resize Anchor & Side-Tab Recovery (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Elevated `SocialCentralBlizzChrome` (`baseLevel + 1`), `Inset` (`+2`), `NineSlice` (`+5`), `TitleContainer` (`+10`), `PortraitContainer` (`+12`), and `PortraitOverlay` (`+13`) strictly above `frame._solidOpaqueBg` (inset `(5, -24, -5, 5)` in Blizzard standalone theme) so the portrait icon, title bar, and top frame border are never occluded by the opaque window backdrop.
  - Pinned the opposite window corner (`TOPLEFT` for `BOTTOMRIGHT`/`RIGHT`/`BOTTOM`, `TOPRIGHT` for `BOTTOMLEFT`) before calling `StartSizing` and restored saved `CENTER` placement on release, eliminating 2x symmetric cursor drift and edge jumping when resizing the standalone window.
  - Moved `IsSafeUiObj` ahead of `DiscoverRightSideTabs` in `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua` (fixing missing SocialCentral side tabs across `O`, `I`, `J`, `C`) and added an automated top-level `local function` forward-call detector across all Lua files in `scripts/verify-compliance.py`.
- **Cross-Window & All-Tab UI Layering (`Z-Order`) Fix (`UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):**
  - Added unified `Theme:SyncButtonLayers` (synchronizing `_blizzTabFrame`/`_blizzBtn` and `_textLayer` frame levels across `ApplyButtonColors`, `OnShow`, `SetButtonText`, and `SetButtonActive`), `SyncScrollListLayers` inside `Theme:CreateScrollList`, and depth-12 `UI:SyncFrameSectionLayers` across `ApplyTheme`, `UI:Refresh`, `UI:ShowHub`, `UI:OpenTab`, `UI:OpenEmbedded`, and `ApplySingleEmbedTheme`.
  - Eliminated occluded/missing button/tab text labels (`Social`, `Conversas`, `Guilda`, `Grupo`, `Por Perto`, `Configurações`) and missing conversation rows/controls inside `Conversas` (routing `chatPanel` through `SyncFramePanelAttachment`), while preserving search box/scroll/close-button relative layers via explicit `_scLevelOffset` and `_scSkipLayerSync`/`_scIsInternalBtnLayer`/`_scIsInputBackdrop` guards.
- **Primary Native Tab (`Tab 1`) 1st-Click Return Across `C` (`CharacterFrame`), `O`, `I`, and `J` (`UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`):**
  - Upgraded `DiscoverRightSideTabs` to merge all tab pools, global tab arrays, and depth-3 right-edge children; kept Blizzard-disabled active native tabs clickable via `SetEnabled(true)` while dimmed (without ever calling `SetChecked(false)`); added unified `HookNativeHostTab` (`OnMouseDown` + `OnClick` + `OnMouseUp` + child buttons) across all four hosts; and released alpha-hidden `CharacterFrame` subframes (`ReleaseAlphaHiddenCharacterSubframesForTabClick`) immediately on `OnMouseDown` so clicking the 1st official tab switches back immediately on the very first click while preserving 2nd-press shortcut close.
- **Social (`O`) & Unified 4-Host (`O`, `I`, `J`, `C`) 2nd-Press Shortcut Close Fix (`UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Replaced destructive logical `nt:SetChecked(false)` mutations across embedded native host tabs (`SocialUIFrame`/`FriendsFrame`, `LFGParentFrame`, `CommunitiesFrame`, `CharacterFrame`) with visual-only selection-texture dimming (`DimNativeTabSelectionVisual` + `EnsureClickedNativeTabRemainsChecked` click guard), so pressing `O` (and `I`, `J`, `C`) a second time while SocialCentral's tab is active immediately closes Blizzard's window instead of switching back to Blizzard's default tab and requiring a third keypress.
  - Added automated static checks in `scripts/verify-compliance.py` enforcing native-tab toggle preservation and host subframe state across all four integrated shortcut windows (`O`, `I`, `J`, `C`).

## [0.2.18] - 2026-11-19
### Fixed & Improved
- **Main Window Movement & Resizing Reliability (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Fixed standalone `SocialCentralFrame` drag and resize failures in `Blizzard Official` theme caused by `OpenTab`/`RestoreStandalonePlacement` dropping the frame to `MEDIUM` level `120` below `ButtonFrameTemplate` chrome (`NineSlice`/`TitleContainer`/`PortraitContainer` at `500+`) and by chrome child frames intercepting mouse events (`DisableChromeMouseRecursive`, `MaxHierarchyFrameLevel`-aware `interactiveBase`, consistent `HIGH` / `600` standalone strata/level).
  - Expanded standalone top drag coverage across the full Blizzard title bar and portrait region (`blizzTitleDrag` + `blizzPortraitDrag`) and hardened `Theme:MakeMovable` / `Theme:AttachDragHandle` (anchor normalization before `StartMoving`, `OnHide` cleanup for active drags/resizes, and `UIParent` checks before saving/restoring offsets).
  - Unified position persistence on `ns.db.windowPositions["main"]` with automatic one-time migration from legacy `ns.db.window` coordinates, prevented `ShowHub`/`OpenTab` from snapping an already-open standalone window away from its dragged location, and added `DISPLAY_SIZE_CHANGED`/`UI_SCALE_CHANGED` viewport clamping.
  - Upgraded resizing via `SetupMainFrameResizeHandles`: enlarged `BOTTOMRIGHT` grip (`18x18` with expanded hit-rect, trilingual tooltip, and right-click default size `790x520` reset), added subtle `RIGHT`, `BOTTOM`, and `BOTTOMLEFT` border/corner resize handles, and dynamically capped max resize bounds to the player's actual `UIParent` dimensions.
- **LFG Window Borders, Own-Listing Edit/Delete & Solo Listing Flow Overhaul (`UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Eliminated the recurring border, transparent title-bar, and bottom-corner overlap artifacts inside Blizzard's `LFGParentFrame` (`Procurando grupo`): in WoW Forever `16001`, `LFGParentFrame` only owns Portrait/Title/`[X]`/SideTabs while each subframe supplies the window border and title-bar chrome; restored `PortraitFrameTemplateNoCloseButton` on `SocialCentralLFGSubPanel` keeping `NineSlice`/`Bg`/`TopTileStreaks`/`TitleBg` visible (`SyncSubPanelTemplateChrome`), hiding only duplicate portrait/title/close widgets, disabling mouse on `NineSlice`, placing `body` above `NineSlice` while `ElevateLfgParentControls` raises `LFGParentFrame` Portrait/Title/`[X]`/Tabs above `panel`, and aligning opaque backing `(4, -21)..(-4, 4)` + `headerBg`/`bgArt`/`common-insideframe` above the dedicated `y = 5..26` button footer.
  - Fixed own-listing recognition for two-word/accented characters (e.g., `Cidadã Camarada`) across `Compat.IsMe` (`ResolvePlayerFullName` surname refresh) and `Clan:GetClanLFGList` (`PushItem` `isSelf` fallback covering `TAG`, `EVENT`, and `GCHAT` entries), and updated `Clan:ClearMyLFG(true)` to clear `bucket.lfg`, `bucket.peers` (`LFG`/`MPLUS` tags), `externalLFG`, `officialLfgCache`, `globalChatLFG`, `guildChatLFG`, and active `C_LFGList` entries so deleted listings disappear immediately.
  - Added full **Edit Listing** (`StartEditListing`) and **Delete Listing** actions on right-click (`OpenLfgRowContextMenu`), double-click, and bottom Browse buttons whenever the player's own row (`(You)`) is selected (removing inappropriate whisper/watchlist/avoid options from own rows).
  - Aligned solo & group listing flow with Blizzard's official Group Finder: dynamic `List Self` / `List Group` / `Update Listing` / `Edit Listing` labels, automatic solo `"LFG"` vs leader `"LFM"` mode selection, hiding the dead disabled button on the Categories screen in favor of clear step-by-step guidance, pre-carrying browse activity selections into the activity checklist, setting `_hasSearchedThisSession = true` on `SubmitListing()` so newly posted solo listings appear immediately in the list, fixing `SyncOfficialActiveLfg` solo `"LFG"` mode, widening/untruncating header dropdowns (`All Cat.`), and hiding the redundant bridge-bar post button while the addon tab is active.
  - Unified SocialCentral LFG publishing directly with Blizzard's official Group Finder (`C_LFGList.CreateListing`/`UpdateListing` + `C_LFGListRoles.SetRoles`): defaulted broadcasts to `Official LFG + Clan & Allies`, populated category checklists directly from `GetOfficialActivitiesForKind` (keeping Forever `16001` `activityIDs` array parity and bi-directional sync without clobbering richer SocialCentral notes/playstyle), added native takeover suppression (`_suppressNativeTakeoverUntil` across `UpdateTabs` and `LFGBrowseFrame:OnShow`) so publishing keeps the user inside SocialCentral's tab while updating Blizzard's Tab 1 and animated eye live, removed unsafe programmatic edits on `LFGListingComment`, and rendered `_titleLabel` onto `body` above `NineSlice` so the `"Procurando grupo"` title displays cleanly inside the top wooden bar.
  - Upgraded SocialCentral's integrated Group Finder (`I`) browse view to return full official Group Finder results (`C_LFGList.Search` 7-arg signature + `C_LFGList.GetFilteredSearchResults`), automatically pre-filter on open to the player's currently listed dungeon/activity IDs (with a `"My Active Listing"` quick option to snap back), inspect all group members (`C_LFGList.GetSearchResultPlayerInfo` + Blizzard social counts) for Guild, Allied Guild, Friends/Battle.net, Watchlist, Good Partner, Avoid, Enemy Guild, New Player Friendly, and `[SC]` indicators, display rich group member rosters in tooltips, and sort known social matches ahead of unflagged groups while demoting avoid/enemy warnings.

## [0.2.17] - 2026-11-18
### Added & Improved
- **Watchlist / Nearby False Online Alert Fix on Offline Party Members & Battle.net Alt Swaps (`Providers/Nearby.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`):**
  - Fixed `Providers/Nearby.lua` (`InspectUnit` and `ScanGroup`) treating disconnected (`<Offline>`) party or raid members as online nearby players stamped with the observer's current zone (`UnitIsConnected(unit)` now fail-closes disconnected group/target units, clears stale `Store.nearby` rows, clears `"group"` Watchlist online state, and avoids stamping the player's zone when `UnitIsVisible` is false and `GetBestMapForUnit` is unavailable).
  - Hardened Battle.net multi-character transition tracking across `Providers/Social.lua` and `Core/Store.lua` (`CollectOnlineForeverLookups` + `GetBNetLinkInfo.activeLookupKeys`), automatically suppressing stale passive/guild/group online transitions for inactive sibling characters belonging to the same active Battle.net account.
- **LFG Rapid-Toggle Overlap Fix & Cross-Window Integrated Tabs Audit (`UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`):**
  - Eliminated overlapping bottom action buttons (`Voltar`, `Procurar grupos`, `Remover`, `Criar grupo`, `Listar-se`, etc.) when rapidly toggling between `Localizador Oficial` and `Ver Grupos` or Blizzard's LFG tabs by hiding all mode views/action buttons upon panel build, introducing `SyncActionButtonsVisibility` (strictly hiding all inactive action buttons before showing active-mode controls), adding monotonic `_transitionSeq` tokens to cancel stale `C_Timer.After(0)` callbacks, re-asserting native LFG subframe hiding, and syncing the bottom bridge bar immediately across `categories`/`activities` modes.
  - Restored the centered `Looking for Group` (`Procurando grupo`) title over `SocialCentralLFGSubPanel`'s opaque header backing when `TitleText` sits outside `TitleContainer`, widened the `Auto-Select` (`Auto-Selecionar`) button (`148px` max) to eliminate text truncation, and adjusted the role-suggestion label anchors (`TOPLEFT`/`TOPRIGHT` + `14px` height, circles shifted `-23px`) so suggestions display cleanly.
  - Audited and hardened all other integrated Blizzard windows (`O` Social/Friends, `J` Guild/Communities, `C` Character/Alts, `M` World Map, `I` My Group): switched Social window dynamic native tab hooks to per-tab tracking (`_scSocialCloseHooked`), synced native tab highlights when `group` embeds inside `LFGParentFrame`, and added monotonic `_embedSeq` / `embeddedState._seq` + `IsShown()` guards on deferred host timers across `UI/MainFrame.lua` and `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`.
- **Guild Events (`+ Chamado`), MOTD/Calendar Integration, Group Quests, Level <13 LFG & `Procurando grupo` `[X]` Fix (`Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Fixed **`+ Chamado` (`OpenEventModal`)** and sibling embedded Guild modals (`Clan Info`, `Diplomacy`, `Alliance`, `Disambiguation`) failing to appear when opened inside Blizzard's Guild window (`CommunitiesFrame`): migrated them onto `UIParent` with a shared `PrepareTopOverlayModal` helper (`FULLSCREEN_DIALOG >= 840`, `SetToplevel(true)`, `Raise()`, 100%-opaque backing).
  - Upgraded **Guild -> Events & Calendar**: added a `Group Quest` (`Missão em Grupo`) category, level-appropriate quick presets (surfacing active Group Quests and entry-level dungeons like Ragefire Chasm / Hall of Thanes), unified Blizzard Guild Calendar events + Guild MOTD event callout suggestions directly inside `Todos`, one-click `Refresh` + safe `/calendar` command modal (removing `ToggleCalendar`), Guild Chat (`/g`) prefill sharing, and LFG Finder (`I`) broadcast shortcuts.
  - Revised **`+ LFG Guildas & Aliados`** standalone window (`EnsureStandaloneLfgHost`) with a proper 100%-opaque titled header bar, `?` help, clamped positioning, uncluttered non-truncated buttons (`Masmorras`, `Enviar mensagem`), and immediate browse/search initialization.
  - Fixed the missing top-right **`[X]` CloseButton** inside `Procurando grupo` (`LFGParentFrame`) when SocialCentral's tab is active by hiding `SocialCentralLFGSubPanel`'s duplicate template chrome, adding a `-30` top-right backing margin, and dynamically discovering & elevating all native `CloseButton` / `NineSlice` / `TitleContainer` candidates.
  - Improved pre-dungeon (**Lv < 13**) Group Finder usability with automatic `QUESTS` fallback official searches under `All Categories`, classic dungeon abbreviation matching (`rfc`, `wc`, `dm`, `vc`, `sfk`, `bfd`, etc.) in `MatchesBrowseActivityFilter`, upcoming (`+4` levels) dungeon checklist display, clearer `My Roles` labeling, explanatory pre-Lv 13 helper text, and full read-only **Active Group Quests** (`GetActiveGroupQuests`) integration across both Browse quick-actions and the `Quests & Zones` listing checklist.
- **Character (`C`) Alts `Items` & `Account` Sub-Tab Runtime Fixes (`UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Forward-declared `RelayoutItemsToolbar` inside `SocialPanels:CreateAltsPanel` so `RenderItems` resolves it lexically instead of hitting a global `nil` call before populating item counts and the item grid/list.
  - Escaped literal percent characters in `L["Rested 100%%+: %d"]` across `enUS`, `ptBR`, and `esES` so `accountMatrix.Render` builds the Account summary header and character matrix rows cleanly whenever at least one alt is `100%+` rested.
  - Moved `TriggerTabRefresh` above `UI:Refresh` in `UI/MainFrame.lua` so tab-fallback refreshes resolve it lexically, and extended `scripts/verify-compliance.py` to statically catch unescaped `%` in formatted trilingual locale strings.
- **Non-Leader Group Finder (`I`) Adaptation & Blizzard Rule Handling:**
  - Verified Blizzard's Classic / Forever (`16001`) Group Finder restrictions (`UnitIsGroupLeader`): when the player is in a party or raid and another player is the leader, Blizzard restricts creating, managing, or broadcasting group listings and sending party invites (`Only the leader can create a listing for the group`).
  - Added `Compat.CanManageGroupListing()`, `Compat.CanInviteToGroup()`, and `Compat.GetGroupLeaderName()`, guarded `Clan:PostLFG()` / `Clan:PostSmartGroupFormation()`, and adapted SocialCentral's LFG side tab and native bridge bar live on roster/leader changes:
