    - Shows a clear **Only the group leader can manage group listings** notice card inside the Group Finder creation view (naming the current leader and guiding the player to Browse, ask for leadership/listing, or leave the party).
    - Shows a **Member mode** notice strip above the Group Finder browser (preserving read-only browsing and Whisper) while cleanly disabling `List Group`, `List Self`, `Broadcast Group`, and non-authorized `Invite` controls with explanatory tooltips.
- **Strict Social Window (`O`) Isolation & Silent `/who` Suppression (`LFGWhoListFrame`):**
  - Fixed a severe cross-window bug where clicking the SocialCentral side tab inside the Social/Friends window (`O`), switching to the `Watchlist` tab, or left-clicking a row to open its context menu automatically triggered a `/who` query that forced Blizzard's Looking for Group / Who window (`Procurando grupo` / `LFGParentFrame` Tab 3) to pop open simultaneously.
  - Hardened `Providers/Watchlist.lua` to recognize Forever `16001`'s `_G.LFGWhoListFrame` and dynamically registered `WHO_LIST_UPDATE` frames, preserve the popup-suppression guard cleanly through completion/timeout, and defer listener restoration past event dispatch so explicit Watchlist `/who` checks never pop open `Procurando grupo`.
  - Added permanent **Rule #17** (`AGENTS.md`, `forever-16001-notes.md`, `SKILL.md`) and automated `scripts/verify-compliance.py` static checks preventing UI tab/menu navigation from ever triggering `/who` or summoning unrelated Blizzard windows.
- **Dynamic World/Ruleset Filters (`PvP`, `PvE`, `RP PvE`, `RP PvP`) & Alts Window Stability:**
  - Upgraded the Character (`C`) / Alts world filter (`altsWorldScope`) with dynamic per-world filters (`PvP`, `PvE`, `RP PvE`, `RP PvP`, etc.) added automatically only when at least 1 character has been logged and recorded in that world/ruleset, synced consistently across the roster, world dropdown, gold totals, item search, and item tooltips.
  - Optimized the `Items` grid tab with lazy cell pooling, guarded scroll viewport layout updates, and error-safe refresh locking (`xpcall`), while fixing `CharacterFrame` native `PaperDollFrame` restoration on `[X]` close/reopen so character information is never lost.
- **Blizzard-Compliant Guild Invite Permissions & Target Radar Pagination:**
  - Automatically verifies full rank invite authority in the Guild Roster (`+ Convidar` disables cleanly with a rank-aware explanation tooltip when unauthorized) and replaces forbidden addon guild-invite calls with a safe one-click `/ginvite` copy command dialog (`C_GuildInfo.Invite` / `GuildInvite` permanently blocked by Blizzard policy & compliance verification).
  - Upgraded the Target Radar floating HUD with a top numbered navigation bar (`<`, numbers, `>`) and 5-target pagination (`<<`, `>>` when more than 7 targets are active), stance color indicators, and detailed hover tooltips, freeing full width for target names and preventing crowding near `[X]`.
- **Blizzard Protected Panel & Taint Safety Hardening (`UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `tools/local-demo/LocalDemo.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Eliminated all remaining programmatic calls to protected Blizzard UI panel toggles and layout helpers (`ToggleLFGParentFrame`, `LFGVanilla_ToggleFrame`, `LFGVanilla_ShowFrame`, `ToggleFriendsFrame`, `ToggleGuildFrame`, `ToggleCommunitiesFrame`, `ToggleWorldMap`, `ShowUIPanel`, `HideUIPanel`) that could trigger `ADDON_ACTION_FORBIDDEN` out of combat in WoW Forever (`16001`).
  - Added an addon-owned standalone **Clans & Allies LFG** host window (`EnsureStandaloneLfgHost`) when Blizzard's Group Finder (`I`) is closed (such as clicking **+ LFG Guildas & Aliados** inside the Guild window) and routed module jumps cleanly to SocialCentral's standalone hub unless the native host window is already open.
  - Added explicit `IsProtected()` / `IsForbidden()` guards before touching host subframes or tabs inside embedded Blizzard windows (`CommunitiesFrame`, `CharacterFrame`, `FriendsFrame`, `LFGParentFrame`, `WorldMapFrame`, `SettingsPanel`), restricted `SetDisplayMode` close hooks to when our embedded view is actually active, and expanded `scripts/verify-compliance.py` to enforce both these panel rules and direct `plate.UnitFrame` protection statically.
- **Comprehensive Alts System Upgrade: Item Catalog Compression, Multi-World Parity, Live Rested XP, Craft Cooldowns & Account Matrix (`Core/Init.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Alts.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - **Memory & SavedVariables Compression:** Introduced shared account-wide `SocialCentralDB.itemCatalog` alongside compact packed slot serialization (`"itemID\tcount[\tcustomLink]"`) with duplicate unmodified stack consolidation across `bags`, `bank`, and `equipment` (`Alts:CompactSavedAlts` auto-migrates legacy entries and prunes orphan catalog IDs), cutting per-slot SavedVariables table bloat by ~70-80%, eliminating false login/XP `itemIndex` rebuilds, and sharing one per-alt metadata table (`meta`) across indexed items.
  - **Full Multi-World & Cross-Faction Sync:** Updated `Alts:RebuildItemIndex`, `Alts:GetItemOwners`, `Alts:GetAccountItemTotal`, `AppendAltItemTooltip` (with `[PvP]`/`[PvE]` tags), `RenderOverview` server/all-worlds gold, `ShowGoldBreakdownTooltip`, and the 7-day unclaimed mail login reminder (`GetAltsList("all")`) to stay synchronized with `altsWorldScope`; added `Include Opposite Faction` toggle (`"ALL"`) to global Alt Item Search and `Store:CleanupDuplicateSameWorldAlts` to automatically prune same-world deleted/recreated character ghosts.
  - **Smart Classic/Forever Features:** Added real-time offline Rested XP estimator (`Alts:GetEstimatedRested` with Inn/City vs Outdoors rates + `Sort: Rested`), passive Profession Cooldown tracker (`ScanTradeSkillCooldowns` for Transmutes/crafts with `[Craft Ready]` badges), Class Supplies bag summary (`Alts:GetClassSuppliesSummary`), and a click-only Blizzard Mailbox recipient quick-picker (`EnsureMailRecipientPicker` on `SendMailFrame`).
  - **UI Readability & Navigation:** Fixed `charHeader` and tab summary truncation (`Nv X`, race/class subline, `charHeader` hover tooltip), added a draggable scrollbar track/thumb to **Overview**, added **Category** (`Gear / Consumables / Materials & Recipes / Quest`) & **Minimum Rarity** (`Uncommon+ / Rare+ / Epic+`) filters to **Items**, made search section headers clickable to jump directly to that alt, and added a 1-glance **Account** summary matrix (`AltUI.CreateAccountMatrixPane`).
- **SavedVariables Guild Roster Packing, Scope Cleanup, O(1) Locale Memoization & Multi-World Empty Hint (`Locales/Locale.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):**
  - Compressed `rosterSnapshot` in `Providers/Clan.lua` (`UpdateGuildRosterHistory`) from a persistent 1,000+ entry hash table into a single compact packed string (`packed`), wiping temporary diff scratch tables immediately after each run and slashing SavedVariables size & boot memory footprint by ~60%.
  - Added `Store:NormalizeLegacyRealmScopes()` on `PLAYER_LOGIN` to merge/prune duplicate numbered shard scopes (`camwowus1realm6pvp` -> `camwowus1realmpvp`), normalize alt `realmPool` fields, pack legacy guild snapshots on boot, and prune stale inactive-guild snapshots (`> 30d`).
  - Added bounded O(1) `rawset` memoization to `Locales/Locale.lua` `__index` fallback lookups (eliminating repeated regex substitutions on hot scroll/tooltip paths).
  - Added a direct inline footer banner (`panel._worldHintBar`) in **My Alts** when **All Worlds (PvP + PvE)** is selected without any saved characters from another world yet.
- **My Alts Multi-World Pool & Gold Aggregation Fixes (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/Alts.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Fixed Lua pattern matching in `Compat.RealmPool()` (removed unsupported regex `|` alternation so shard names like `CAMWOWUS1Realm6PvP` properly collapse to `camwowus1realmpvp` and all PvP characters appear under **This World Only**, while `Normal` / `PvE` realms normalize cleanly into their own `realmpve` pool).
  - Fixed total gold filtering (`scopeArg`) when switching to **All Worlds (PvP + PvE)** in `UI/SocialPanels.lua` (replacing the `and false or "pool"` Lua ternary pitfall with `"all"`), updated `Alts:GetArmoryURL` for `"Normal"` realms, persisted `altsWorldScope`, and added a 1-click **World** toggle button beside the top character selector with clear guidance for recording characters from other Forever worlds.
- **Eye-Catching Target Overhead Banner & Idle/Startup Memory Footprint Optimization (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Nearby.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added an eye-catching **Target Nameplate Highlight** banner (`spotterNameplateTargetHighlight = true`, toggleable with `[?]` help in Settings) directly above the selected target's nameplate under the pulsing red arrow (`13pt THICKOUTLINE` class/reaction-colored `» Name · Lv X «`, optional `<Guild> · STANCE` subline, and glowing accent halo) while leaving Blizzard's native protected `plate.UnitFrame` untouched (`IsPlateAnchorSafe` guarded, dirty-checked font changes, zero taint).
  - Slashed idle memory usage and background garbage allocation when windows are closed:
    - Made offline guild directory parsing (`ScanGuild` last-online/roster-rank tables and `Compat.SyncRosterForeverNames`) **lazy on demand** (`Social:EnsureFullGuildScan` when opening `O` or `J`), stopping 800+ offline member table churn & GUID-cache thrashing at login and during idle `GUILD_ROSTER_UPDATE`.
    - Gated public channel LFG parsing (`CHAT_MSG_CHANNEL`), `ScanOfficialLfgResults()`, and 1,000-member `UpdateGuildRosterHistory()` diffs when Group Finder (`I`) / Guild (`J`) are idle/closed (keeping O(1) alert checks for known Friends/Watchlist/Guild/Allies and pooling `Clan.globalChatLFG` slot tables).
    - Replaced per-nameplate `C_Timer.After` closure allocations in `ClanSpotter`'s `NAME_PLATE_UNIT_ADDED` handler with a single coalesced `pendingPlateUnits` batch, and optimized `Nearby` to cache zone/subzone strings, gate `FORBIDDEN_NAME_PLATE_UNIT_ADDED` & ambient tooltips while closed, and bound `MAX_NEARBY_ENTRIES = 120`.
    - Added damped post-login and on-hide non-combat incremental GC stepping (`ns:RequestIdleGcStep`) so dead compiler/startup and closed-window garbage is reclaimed smoothly instead of lingering in WoW's addon memory counter.
- **My Alts Multi-World (PvP / PvE) Isolation, Armory Modal Visibility & Mandatory Global Action Confirmations (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Alts.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Fixed `OpenCopyModal` (`SocialCentralModal`) inside `EnsureModal()` so clicking **Armory** from **My Alts** embedded inside `CharacterFrame` (`C`) renders visibly in front of Blizzard frames (`UIParent`, `"FULLSCREEN_DIALOG"`, level `820`, `SetToplevel(true)`, `m:Raise()`), instead of staying hidden inside an unshown standalone parent window, and restored input geometry/button text cleanly across modal modes.
  - Raised `SocialCentralRemoveAltModal` and `SocialCentralDeleteThreadModal` to `"FULLSCREEN_DIALOG"` level `840` (`m:Raise()`) and introduced `UI:OpenConfirmModal` / `UI:ConfirmResetInstances` so **every** removal, clear, leave, delete, profile copy/reset, position reset, and instance reset action across Watchlist, Nearby, Guild Events/Diplomacy, Chat (Clear Chat, Clear Notes, Leave Room, Delete Personal Note, Remove/Reset Quick Replies), Settings, Minimap, Group Composition, and LFG asks for explicit user confirmation first.
  - Preserved explicit WoW Forever world rulesets (`PvP` / `PvE` / `RP` / `RP-PvP`) in `Compat.RealmPool` and `Compat.DetectWorldType` (preventing PvE vs PvP alt lists from silently colliding when only numbered realm tokens differ), saved `worldType` and `realmName` on `Alts:Snapshot()`, selected the matching `pve`/`pvp` ruleset segment automatically in `Alts:GetArmoryURL()`, and added a **World / Ruleset** filter (`This World Only` default vs `All Worlds (PvP + PvE)`) across the Alts roster, character dropdown, gold vault totals/tooltip, and cross-alt Item Search (`This Alt` / `All Alts (This World)` / `All Alts (All Worlds)`) with `[PvP]` / `[PvE]` badges.
- **Memory & Garbage-Collection Optimization (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`):**
  - Eliminated large-guild (1,000-member) O(N) table allocation churn during `GUILD_ROSTER_UPDATE` and Guild Hub refreshes: pooled `Store.guildRosterRanks` subtables via scan epochs (`guildRosterScanGen`), switched `ScanGuild`, `UpdateGuildRosterHistory`, `OnExternalChatPassive`, `ScanOfficialLfgResults`, `QueueCooperativeSighting`, and `Store:GetRecentCompanion` to allocation-free `Compat.ParseCharacterFields`, skipped redundant full-guild `SyncRosterForeverNames` passes inside `Social:Scan`, added `Clan:GetGuildRosterQuickStats` + pooled row objects in `Clan:GetMergedGuildMembers` (avoiding double 1,000-member scans when filtering), and updated `UpdateGuildRosterHistory` to diff `rosterSnapshot` in-place.
  - Optimized `Clan:GetTrustedLFGBreakdown` (single-pass O(K) with 1s memoization instead of dual `GetClanLFGList` builds), gated `LFGFrameTab:SyncAllEmbeddedTabStates` so breakdown counting skips hidden `LFGParentFrame`, and coalesced `SyncEmbeddedIfAnyHostShown`.
  - Shrunk `Compat` parse/GUID cache limits (`1500 -> 500`, `800 -> 400`), bounded `Chat.knownFullByFirst` / `knownFactionByKey` (`400`), added zero-table vararg traversal (`Compat.ForEachChild` / `SyncSubtreeChildren`), hoisted row helpers out of `UpdateListRow` with scratch subline buffers, and made `clanPanel` lazy on demand inside `UI/MainFrame.lua`.
- **Guild & Communities (`J`) Embedded First-Open, Filter Toolbar & Tab Restore Fixes (`UI/MainFrame.lua`):**
  - Hid `SocialCentralFrame` immediately upon creation inside `BuildMainFrame()` so opening an embedded host module (`J`, `C`, `I`, `M`) before opening the standalone window never pops up `SocialCentralFrame` behind Blizzard's UI.
  - Added `SyncSubtreeButtonLayers` and `SyncClanCtxToolbarLayout` plus safe layout fallback widths so the Guild contextual filter/search bar (`peersToolBar` / `ctxBar`) inside `J` keeps elevated frame levels (`_textLayer` / `clearBtn`) and valid geometry immediately on the very first embedded open.
  - Added `appendOnly` mode to `HideCharacterHostSubframes` so the post-layout `C_Timer.After(0)` pass inside `OpenEmbedded()` no longer wipes `hiddenCommunitiesChildren` (`hiddenCharSubframes` / `hiddenCharChildren` / `hiddenWorldMapSubframes`), and fixed `RestoreEmbeddedHostUI(CommunitiesFrame)` to restore all shared native chrome (`Inset`, `CommunitiesList`, `Chat`, `ChatEditBox`, dropdowns) even when Blizzard's active `ChatTab` (`CHAT` mode) is already selected, preventing a blank gray Guild window when returning to Tab 1.
  - Audited and hardened sibling host transitions in `UI/MainFrame.lua` and `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`: kept `CharacterFrameInset`/`CharacterFrameInsetRight` restored when switching from **My Alts (`C`)** directly to Reputation/Currency/Skills/PvP (`otherNativeOpen == true`), synced elevated button text/clear layers (`SyncSubtreeButtonLayers`) for embedded `FriendsFrame` (`O`) tabs, hooked `FriendsFrameTab1..4`/`FriendsTabHeaderTab1..3` clicks in `O` (`EnsureSocialSideTab`) to close the embedded panel cleanly, and added a post-click native-pane restoration guard in `LFGFrameTab` (`I`) when returning directly to the previously active LFG tab.
- **Tooltip Size/Flicker & Interaction Stability Fixes (`Providers/Alts.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`):**
  - Fixed item and player hover tooltips (`GameTooltip` / `ItemRefTooltip`) rapidly oscillating in size: stopped calling `tooltip:Show()` inside `TooltipDataProcessor.AddTooltipPostCall` (reserving `:Show()` strictly for legacy `OnTooltipSetItem`/`OnTooltipSetUnit` fallback hooks), and removed `Alts.lua`'s `< 0.05s` cooldown skip after `OnTooltipCleared` which previously dropped alt item lines during rapid Blizzard compare/refresh redraws.
  - Cleared `Watchlist.suppressPopupUntil` immediately inside `CleanupPending()` so opening Blizzard's Group Finder (`I`) right after a Watchlist `/who` check never accidentally closes the window, and enabled click pass-through on Group Finder row relationship icon badges (`SetMouseClickEnabled(false)` + fallback click forwarding) so clicking directly on Guild/Friend/Watchlist/`[SC]` icons still selects/interacts with the row.
- **Official Blizzard LFG Search Integration, Default Role Pre-Selection & Unified Group Browser (`Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Defaulted Browse role filtering (`lfgBrowseRole = "MINE"`) and level filtering (`lfgBrowseSuitableLevel = true`), persisted across reloads and relogs alongside last category, source filter, and checked dungeon/activity lists (`lfgBrowseSelectedActivities`), while auto-preselecting level-ideal dungeons on initial category selection and deferring group loading/searching each session until the player manually picks a filter or clicks Search/Refresh (`_hasSearchedThisSession`).
  - Integrated Classic/Forever `16001` Blizzard Group Finder searching directly (`Clan:GetOfficialCategoryMap`, `Clan:GetOfficialActivitiesForKind`, `Clan:ResolveActivityLevelRange` reading `minLevelSuggestion`/`maxLevelSuggestion` + Classic/Forever level ranges without `[0-60]` noise, `Clan:SearchOfficialLfg`, and `ScanOfficialLfgResults()`), keeping **all** server groups unified without a redundant `Official LFG` filter toggle.
  - Replaced cut-off text pills with compact 1-click icon filter toggles (**Friends / Watchlist**, **Same Guild**, **Allied Guilds**) beside the search box plus untruncated `"Source · Role"` labels, hoverable icon badges on listing rows (**Guild**, **Allied Guild**, **Enemy Guild**, **Watchlist**, **Friend**, **`[SC]`**), **5 role slots** (`T/H/D/D/D`, grayed when filled / colored when open) for 5-player dungeons, and **3 role slots** (`T/H/D`) exclusively for raids and >5-player activities.
- **Watchlist Status Accuracy & Group Finder (`I`) Audit Fixes (`Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Prevented stale `"Online"` Watchlist entries across logins and long idle gaps by expiring unconfirmed `"online"` rows (`STALE_ONLINE_SECONDS = 300`, `INIT_STALE_ONLINE_SECONDS = 180`) to `"unknown"` (`Unchecked`) without duplicate online toasts (`_expiredOnline`), syncing Battle.net/Friend/Guild offline states even across `/reload` and relogs in `Social:Scan()`, marking Watchlist rows offline on confirmed `"No player named ..."` whisper rejections, and fixing `CheckNext`/`ComputeWatchlistCheckPriority` so missing `Social` entries are never skipped as passive-online.
  - Added a 1-click **Verify Next Watchlist Player** refresh button (`frame.verifyBtn`) beside Watchlist's `+ Add` bar with live combat/cooldown state and an inline **Check** row button on `"unknown"` entries.
  - Opened SocialCentral's Group Finder (`I`) tab directly into the complementary **Browse Groups** view (`viewMode = "browse"`), fixed `RoleShortLabel` declaration order (preventing a Lua error when filtering by role), fixed `browseHeader` and bottom-bar width overflow inside `LFGParentFrame`, added multilingual/Forever dungeon detection (`Hall of Thanes`, `Ruins of Lordaeron`, PT/ES dungeon names) and `forceIsDungeon` tagging, prevented `[Divulgar Grupo]` (`PostSmartGroupFormation`) from posting city/zone names when no dungeon is active (opening the activity picker instead), stripped stale `[X/5]` tags on solo group disband (`CheckAutoCloseFullDungeonLFG`), and fixed `ListingTab` fallback + non-leader guidance in `OpenOfficialLfgForGuild`.
- **Silent LFG Catchup, Class/Race Role Recommendations & Group/Raid Composition Dashboard (`Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Group.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Eliminated `"No player named '...' is currently playing."` chat spam when opening/refreshing Group Finder (`I`) by filtering `CollectExternalLFGRecipients` strictly to live online targets (stopping blind addon whispers to offline Recent Companions/session peers) and installing a scoped 3-second `CHAT_MSG_SYSTEM` filter (`TrackSilentWhisperTarget`) for background sync packets that also updates `sessionOfflineChars` and Watchlist.
  - Added `Clan:GetRecommendedRolesForCharacter()` (evaluating Class, Race, Level, and Talent distribution) to pre-select suitable/frequent roles automatically in Group Finder (`I`) with an **Auto-Select** button, incapable-role dimming, and a **My Class/Race Roles** filter (`MINE`) in Browse Groups alongside smarter `LFM` role filtering.
  - Upgraded Group & Raid Composition (`O -> Group` in `Providers/Group.lua` and `UI/SocialPanels.lua`) with a live **Class & Buff Synergy** dashboard (role totals, missing roles, Resurrection/Battle Res & Dispel coverage, Fortitude/Intellect/MotW/Shout/Blessings/Totems breakdown, Avoid warnings, and Solo readiness + hourly 5/5 instance bar), Raid metadata (`GetRaidRosterInfo` subgroups `G1–G8`, `MT`/`MA`/`ML`, `Dead` state), and a switchable 2-column **Raid Grid (`G1–G8`)** view with full member context menus.
- **Group Finder (`I`), Social (`O`), Guild Events (`J`), Alts (`C`), Minimap & Notification Enhancements (`Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/Group.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Extended LFG chat/sound/toast alerts (`MaybeNotifyClanLFG`) to incoming Allied Guild, Friend, Watchlist, and Recent Companion (`ALFG` & chat) group listings with clickable direct Browse navigation + row pre-selection (`lfgSelectKey`) and a 1-click **Quick Apply (/w)** button (including character `iLvl`) directly on `CLAN_LFG` toasts.
  - Added a visible live keyword **Search Box** and **Suitable for My Level** filter (`CheckActivityLevelSuitability`, green highlight + tooltip) inside Group Finder (`I`), external LFG catchup (`ALFGR` / `RequestExternalLFGCatchup`) on panel open/refresh, and a quick-action Right-Click context menu on `SocialCentralLFGSideTab`.
  - Added automatic `[X/5 · Need: ...]` party-size updates and auto-unlist when completing a 5-player group (`CheckAutoCloseFullDungeonLFG`), shared hourly instance caps between group members (`ICAP` shown per member in Group `O`), pre-reset safety warnings (`ResetDungeonInstances`), 24h chronological instance run history tooltips (`AppendInstanceHistoryTooltip` across `I`, `J -> Locks`, and `C -> My Alts`), visible **Broadcast Group** + **Recent Companions** filters (`Online`, `Good Partner (+)`, `Tank/Healer/DPS`) in `O -> Group`, LFG/Instance cap summary + shortcuts on the Minimap button, Allied Guild event sharing/signups (`EVT`/`EVS`/`SIG`/`DEL`), 1-click **Invite Next Applicant** on Guild Events (`J`), and Good Partner / Avoid signup badges on event listings.
- **Native Group Finder (`I`) Integration Bar, Trusted Network Discovery & Instance Cap Tracker (`Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/Group.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Attached an integrated 100%-opaque action bar (`SocialCentralLFGNativeBridgeBar`) directly beneath Blizzard's native Group Finder (`I`) alongside a live count badge and network breakdown tooltip on the right-side bookmark tab (`SocialCentralLFGSideTab`), featuring 1-click **View Groups (N)** and **Broadcast Group** (`Clan:PostSmartGroupFormation`, automatically calculating needed party roles `[X/5 · Need: ...]` and broadcasting to Guildmates, Allied Guilds, Battle.net/Character Friends, online Watchlist `[SC]`/Ally contacts, and online Recent Companions).
  - Added cooperative Guild LFG catchup responses on `REQ` (rate-limited with Trickle suppression), **Trusted Network** (`TRUSTED`) and **SocialCentral [SC]** (`SC_USERS`) source filters, **Filter by Role** (`All Roles`, `Tank`, `Healer`, `DPS`) in Group Finder (`I`), `[SC]` and `[PARTNER]` listing badges, and inline `[LFG/LFM: Activity]` indicators across Social (`O`), Watchlist (`O`), Guild Roster (`J`), and player mouseover tooltips.
  - Added account-wide **5-per-hour Instance Limit Tracker** (`Group:RecordCurrentInstanceEntry`, `Group:GetHourlyInstanceStatus`) and 1-click **Reset Dungeon Instances** (`Group:ResetDungeonInstances`) surfaced in the Group Finder (`I`) bridge/filter menu, Group & Recent Companions (`O`) panel, and Guild Locks (`J` -> `Locks` summary row + Right-Click Reset on `Refresh`).

## [0.2.16] - 2026-11-17
### Added & Improved
- **Complete Module Isolation, Zero-Idle Background Shutdown & Modular Settings UI (`Core/Init.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/*`, `UI/*`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Expanded Settings Tab 1 (**Summary & Windows**) with master switches for all **10 SocialCentral modules & official window integrations** (`Social & Friends (O)`, `Group Finder (I)`, `Guild & Diplomacy (J)`, `Character & Alts (C)`, `World Buffs on Map (M)`, `Screen Target Radar`, `Watchlist (/who)`, `Nearby Players`, `Conversations (Chat)`, and `Group & Companions`), dynamically disabling/dimming and locking dependent checkboxes, `-`/`+` steppers, keyword EditBoxes, and chat-tab selectors immediately without `/reload`.
  - Unchecking any module completely stops its background scanners, timers, bag/aura tracking, passive discovery pings, tooltips, Minimap stats/badges, side-tab unread pills, right-click unit menu items, `/sc` / `/al` slash commands, `SetItemRef` links, and embedded shortcuts, and falls back `/sc hub` and the `O` side-tab cleanly to the first enabled tab.
  - Added right-click context menus on Blizzard right-side bookmark tabs (`O`, `I`, `J`, `C`) with **Disable in this window** (immediately closing the embedded container and restoring the native Blizzard tab) and **Settings**, plus a right-click menu on the World Map (`M`) badge (`Show World Buff pins`, **Disable World Buffs Module (M)**, and **Settings**), plus **Restore Defaults (This Tab)** on `Radar & Map`.
  - Cleaned guildless characters' filter dropdowns across Social, Conversations, and Group Finder (`I`), suppressed Guild/Ally/Same-Guild Spotter classifications and background Guild calendar/sync tasks when Guild (`J`) is disabled, pruned temporary Global LFG / Nearby entries and World Buff expiry timers on disable, and immediately re-scanned active auras when World Buffs (`M`) is re-enabled.
- **Per-Character Profiles, 1-Click Presets, Focus Modes & Muted Summary (`Core/Init.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`):**
  - Added per-character settings profile isolation (`SocialCentralCharDB` with deep-copied nested settings, live Theme & Minimap refresh, **Copy from Account Profile**, **Reset Profile to Defaults**, and header `Profile: Character` / `Profile: Account` status badge) alongside highlighted 1-click presets (**Full (Default)**, **Friends & Chat**, **Ultra-Light (Alts)**).
  - Added non-destructive **Do Not Disturb** (with 1-click Minimap right-click toggle and **Pause All (Do Not Disturb)** button on the Toast crossed-bell confirmation popup) and **Auto-silence social popups & chimes inside Dungeons, Raids & BGs** (preserving critical Group Avoid safety warnings, pruning queued/active non-critical toasts on instance transitions, and showing status in Settings & Minimap tooltips).
  - Added **Muted Items & Players Summary** + numeric counter on the `Notifications` tab (`Re-enable Alerts` aligned with `Restore Popups`, `Unmute All Players`, Focus-mode reporting, and exclusion of intentionally disabled parent modules), and kept `Mute All Popups` preserving Group Avoid warnings.
- **Whisper Delivery Hardening, Draft Thread Binding & Lua 5.1 Limit Safeguards (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):**
  - Marked `lastManualWhisperSend` and `lastManualBNetSend` as delivered upon Blizzard `CHAT_MSG_WHISPER_INFORM` / `CHAT_MSG_BN_WHISPER_INFORM`, excluded moderation/inappropriate-content system messages from player-not-found detection, required exact target matching in `CHAT_MSG_SYSTEM` instead of broad time-window fallbacks, cleared temporary `sessionOfflineChars` flags whenever live whispers or Friend/Guild/Social updates confirm online presence, canceled redundant `3.5s` addon fallback timers after immediate offline recovery, and added Battle.net draft recovery on system errors.
  - Bound restored drafts (`pendingDraftThreadKey`) strictly to their source thread, focused `replyInput` reliably across all whisper/BNet-redirect composers, cleared composer text after successful **Quick Replies**, and aligned Social/Watchlist `[SC]` badges & double-click routing with `moduleChatEnabled`.
  - Split `UI/Settings.lua` `BuildPanel()` into dedicated per-tab builders (`BuildOverviewTab`, `BuildNotificationsTab`, `BuildRadarTab`, `BuildGeneralTab`) well under Lua 5.1's `200`-local and `60`-upvalue compiler limits, fixing `Settings` panel loading on all characters, and added automated local/upvalue static verification in `scripts/verify-compliance.py`.

## [0.2.15] - 2026-11-17
### Added & Improved
- **Unified 1-Chat-Per-Player System, Dynamic Alt Tracking, Faction Icons & Clean Pair Dividers (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`):**
  - Implemented Disjoint-Set Union-Find player-identity clustering (`Chat:ConsolidatePlayerThreads`) with automatic self-healing migration on load and Battle.net updates, merging duplicate threads (bare first names, composite surnames, alts, and Battle.net threads) into 1 canonical conversation per human player while preserving full chronological history and clearing stale `[desativado]` fallback notices.
  - Dynamically tracks and updates the active connected character (`SyncActiveCharacterOnThread`, `ResolveActivePeerTarget`) across conversation list rows, chat headers, class colors, faction icons, and Battle.net whisper routing without appending redundant `[To <PreviousChar>]:` tags when talking to an active Battle.net character.
  - Fixed unverified `"registered"` peer sends across alts so `SendOrOpenReply` delivers immediately via official WoW/Battle.net whisper while probing in the background instead of timing out with `[desativado]`.
  - Added Alliance / Horde faction icons (`Compat.FactionIconMarkup`, `Compat.ResolveCharacterFaction`) to the left of character names across Conversations, `+ Chat`, Watchlist, Social, Nearby, and My Alts; grouped direct chat messages with clean character-pair dividers (`—— CharA & CharB ——`) and compact first-name-only sender lines (`Aiden:`, `Zul:`).
- **Categorized Settings, Per-Window Toggles (`O/I/J/C/M`), Guildless Auto-Hide & Toast Quick-Mute (`Core/Init.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Redesigned Settings into 4 categorized tabs (`Summary & Windows`, `Notifications`, `Radar & Map`, `General & Tools`) with 1-click notification summary toggles, bulk `Mute All Popups` / `Restore Popups`, and independent toggles to enable/disable Blizzard window integrations (`O`, `I`, `J`, `C`, `M`) live without `/reload`.
  - Added `hideGuildFeaturesWhenNoGuild` (default on) to automatically hide Guild (`J`) side-tabs, Hub Guild tab, Guild/Alliance chat mirrors, and Guild minimap shortcuts on guildless characters (`PLAYER_GUILD_UPDATE` reactive).
  - Added a crossed-bell (`bell-off`) mute button on every floating Notification Toast with a small adjacent 100%-opaque confirmation popup (`SocialCentralToastConfirmPopup`) to prevent misclicks, supporting category-wide disabling or single-player muting, and fixed Friend Online chime suppression when both toast and chat alerts are disabled.
- **Full Logic & WoW Forever (`16001`) Consistency Audit Fixes:**
  - **My Alts (`Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Alts.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`):** Switched alt storage to character `GUID` keys with automatic one-time migration so same-first-name alts never collide; isolated Alliance vs. Horde server gold (with faction subtotals in Gold Breakdown), item search, and item tooltips; fixed bank snapshots (`BANKFRAME_OPENED` tracking), profession unlearning cleanup, automatic login lockout syncing (`RequestRaidInfo()`), level-60 rested XP hiding, proportional narrow-tab sizing in `CharacterFrame`, full character name on Overview gold rows, and honest pending-mail detection reminders across all alts without artificial 30-day expiry countdowns.
  - **Guild Hub, LFG (`I`), Watchlist & Nearby (`Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Providers/Nearby.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`):** Replaced remaining Retail concepts (`Mythic+`, `Keystone`, `Random Heroic`, `Timewalking`, `Inscription`, `Jewelcrafting`) with Level 60 Forever equivalents, added `Fishing` detection and Classic talent-tree role detection; decoupled `LFG`/`LFM` mode from Playstyle in `LFGFrameTab` and enforced `My Clan Only` broadcast filtering + clean `LFGParentFrame` tab switching; added same-faction guards for External LFG, Alliance chat recipients, and `Ally` Diplomacy settings; added Guild Alliance config sync (`ALLC`) and strict event moderation enforcement; prevented unresolved single-word Watchlist homonyms from false notifications or premature rekeying and restored previous `GetWhoToUi()` state; filtered opposing-faction local chat speakers in `Nearby`; and removed dead `Check Next` / hidden modal leftovers.
  - **Cross-Faction Battle.net Chat & Rooms (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`):** Kept `bnet_app` / alt-character Battle.net friends reachable without overwriting character-specific `WHISPER` thread names; dynamically recovered cross-faction Private Room Battle.net targets in memory after `/reload` without persisting IDs; stopped non-BNet cross-faction instance peers from false 1-on-1 verification and removed false offline opposite-faction flagging from `CHAT_MSG_SYSTEM`; forwarded Battle.net identity on `addon:SCentral:w:` link clicks.
  - **World Buffs, Target Radar, Toasts & Settings (`Providers/WorldBuffs.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`):** Keyed World Buff drops per realm shard (`Compat.Realm()` + `WB` shard check), added 25s drop alert debounce, added Chronoboon (`349981`) detection and unboon false-trigger suppression, tracked connected online time for Sayge's cooldown, removed fake city drop timers from Dire Maul Tribute buffs, showed only the active Darkmoon Faire map pin, and hid `Report` on inactive buff rows; cleaned Forever `ITEM_RANGES`, prioritized full names in `GetForeverTargetVariants`, anchored combat Radar fallback to HUD position, included live coordinates in Scout Alerts, added `Lock Target Radar HUD position` setting, kept `RMI` room-invite toasts interactive on left-click, let World Buff toasts follow World Buff settings independently, and aligned all Settings/Tutorial/Minimap copy with live Classic behavior.
- **Logic Audit Fixes (Alts, Radar, Chat, Watchlist, World Buffs, Guild):**
  - Removed the manual "Set Forever Surname" action (surnames are fixed at character creation); Web Armory link now uses the character's real region/ruleset and encodes accents; zero-gold and Archaeology/bank-slot snapshot flaws fixed; Guild Professions/Lockouts only merge same-faction alts.
  - Fixed Target Radar crashes (nameplate refresh, untrack, target arrow), dead Avoid-in-group warning, conversation pinning, Watchlist recent-companion suggestions, guild name on Watchlist, online/offline detection in Conversations, Quote Reply error, leaked globals in the Group panel, duplicate LFG event handler, Private Room invite link navigation, and Official chat tab choices 7-10.
  - World Buff drop yells are ignored inside dungeons/raids/battlegrounds and expiry warnings pause in raids/battlegrounds; Nearby now also reads Instance chat; cross-faction Battle.net friends who both run the addon keep the in-addon chat.
- **Forever Two-Word Group Invite Fix & Addon-Wide Invite Audit (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`):**
  - Fixed Target Radar (`ClanSpotter` / `ClanSpotterHUD`) and Forever unit resolution appending a player's surname a second time (`"FirstName Surname-Surname"` from `UnitFullName` realm return), which caused `"No player named 'FirstName Surname-Surname'"` errors when clicking **Invite** on Target Radar (`SanitizeMacroTargetName`, `CleanForeverUnitRealm`, and `StripForeverSuffixes` in `Compat` parsers + `+ Target` in `+ Chat`).
  - Audited and hardened every group **Invite** button/menu across Target Radar (`ClanSpotterHUD`), `Watchlist`, `Social`, `Nearby`, `Clan Roster`, `Clan Professions`, `Clan Event Signups`, `Group / Recent Companions`, and `Looking for Group` (`I`): fixed `Providers/Social.lua` `sameFaction = false` boolean precedence so opposite-faction BNet contacts no longer show active Invite buttons, added `Compat.IsInMyGroup` and `Compat.IsMe` guards to omit/disable inviting yourself or current party/raid members, and passed resolved `bnetAccountID` cleanly from Watchlist/Radar/LFG callers.
- **Conversations (`O`) Toolbar, Header Actions & Composer Overhaul (`UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Replaced the overloaded `+ Novo` dropdown with three separated controls (`+ Chat`, `+ Room`, and a gear `Manage Conversations` menu) plus a neutral `Unread (N)` toggle, eliminating wasted toolbar gaps and multi-level hover cascades for routine actions.
  - Upgraded the `+ Chat` modal (`UIParent`-parented, 100% opaque, with `?` help) into a searchable live picker of all online Battle.net, WoW, and Guild friends alongside `+ Target` and character name entry.
  - Replaced the confusing red-slashed icon on `Social Card & Notes` with a clean notepad icon, unified per-conversation actions (`Pin/Unpin`, `Mark Read/Unread`, `Copy`, `Clear`, `Delete`) under a crisp header gear menu, snapped compact thread-list height to whole `38px` rows to avoid sliced entries in `O`, clipped `ScrollingMessageFrame` top overlap (`-60`/`-82`), upgraded the scroll-to-bottom button to a clean Blizzard scroll-down icon, and switched the Send button to display a clear `Send` label when the composer has space.
- **My Alts Roster, Responsive `CharacterFrame` (`C`) Layout, Item List/Grid & Management (`Providers/Alts.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Redesigned **My Alts** into a side-by-side **All Alts Roster + Character Inspector** on wide windows (`Overview`, `Items`, `Gear`) and a responsive tabbed layout (`Alts`, `Overview`, `Items`, `Gear`) inside the narrow Blizzard `CharacterFrame` (`C`) window with dynamic header clearance around `Settings` / `Update` buttons and zero clipped columns.
  - Added sortable Alt Roster cards (`Recent`, `Gold`, `Level`, `Mail`, `Name`) displaying class icons, levels, gold, zones, pending mail expiration warnings (`ageDays` / `Expires in Nd`), rested XP caps, low bag warnings, primary professions, and lockout counts, plus a hoverable **Total Server Gold** breakdown tooltip.
  - Upgraded the **Overview** dossier with dynamic anti-truncation rows, visual XP/Rested and Bag capacity bars, profession icons, mail expiry countdowns, and active dungeon/raid **Lockouts** (`Alts:GetLockouts`), and exposed 100%-opaque **Set Forever Surname**, **Web Armory**, and **Remove Alt** management modals.
  - Upgraded **Items** and **Gear** with stack consolidation, 1-click **This Alt / All Alts** scope toggle, **Grid / List** view toggle showing item names and account totals, right-click item context menus (`Search this item across all Alts`, `Insert link in chat`, `Copy Name`), and a 2D 19-slot **Gear** overview showing empty slots.
  - Fixed missing `WHITE8X8` backdrop texture definition in `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, updated `UI:IsAltsVisible()` to detect embedded `CharacterFrame` visibility, and added `Relayout()` throttling to avoid redundant bag/equipment scans.

## [0.2.14] - 2026-11-17
### Added & Improved
- **Cross-Character / Battle.net Whisper Redirect, Clean Icon UI & Delete Conversation Confirmation (`Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added automatic Battle.net and linked-alt whisper redirection when messaging an offline character whose player is online on another character (or on Battle.net), automatically tagging outgoing messages with `[To <Character>]: ...` (`[Para <Personagem>]: ...`).
  - Added session-safe observed alt linking (`Store:RecordObservedAltPair`, `Store:GetAllLinkedCharacters`) when Battle.net friends switch characters, plus 3.5s unconfirmed-addon delivery recovery (`sessionUnverifiedPeers`) so messages to peers without active SocialCentral fall back cleanly to standard `/w` whispers with draft restoration.
  - Replaced cluttered text buttons and `[W]`/`[SC]` tags in Conversations with compact top-layer icons (`_textLayer`) and descriptive hover tooltips, added a **Delete Conversation** right-click option with a 100% opaque WoW-style typed-word confirmation modal (`APAGAR`/`DELETE`/`ELIMINAR`), and polished `O`/`J`/`C`/`I` row alignments, dossier hover tooltips, and Guild MOTD hover tooltips.
- **Direct Chat Navigation, Guild Online Class Breakdown & Update Notifications (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Fixed a forward-declaration bug in `Chat:EnsureWhisperThread` (`FindBNetWoWGameAccountByChar` and siblings) that prevented `"Open in SocialCentral Chat"` from switching to the player's direct conversation when triggered from Guild or context menus.
  - Replaced single-frame embedded sharing with independent per-host containers (`SocialCentralGuildEmbeddedFrame`, `SocialCentralAltsEmbeddedFrame`, `SocialCentralGroupEmbeddedFrame`, `SocialCentralBuffsEmbeddedFrame`, and `SocialCentralFrame`), allowing all official windows (`O`, `J`, `C`, `I`, `M`) to remain open side-by-side in SocialCentral mode simultaneously without closing or reverting one another.
  - Fixed `CharacterFrame` (`C`) native sub-panel text bleed-through (such as *Statistics* / *Estatísticas* rows overlapping the Alts inventory grid) by dynamically hiding all non-chrome `CharacterFrame` child frames on embed and restoring them cleanly on close.
  - Fixed Watchlist cross-faction Battle.net sync in `Providers/Social.lua` (`samePool` without `sameFaction` filter) and prevented `/who` checks in `Providers/Watchlist.lua` from overwriting Battle.net-confirmed online characters back to `"offline"`.
  - Added live session-only `[BNet]` badges (`Social:GetBNetLinkInfo`) on Watchlist rows, active-character subline notices (`Online: <Character>` when a Battle.net friend is logged onto another character), tooltip Battle.net alt lists, opposing-faction detection (`ResolveBNetFaction` via `factionName`/`raceID`/`raceName`/home zones + `MarkBNetOppositeFaction`) with `[Alliance]`/`[Horde]` badges, and cross-faction Battle.net whisper routing on Watchlist.
  - Cleared stale Battle.net `activeLookupKey`/`activeDisplayName` immediately when a friend exits WoW to the Battle.net Desktop App (`bnetState ~= "wow_forever"`), routed opposing-faction direct threads in `Chat:OpenOfficialChatForThread` to `Compat.BNetWhisper`, and guaranteed `"notes:self"` Personal Notes initialization on `Chat:Init`.
  - Fixed `Social:GetBNetLinkInfo` per-character faction resolution (`bnetFactionByLookup` priority and Lua `and/or` falsey trap), routed `Chat.lua` `TryDirectOfficialSend` opposing-faction `WHISPER` sends directly to Battle.net whisper, and added cross-faction Battle.net whisper/invite guards in `SocialPanels.lua` and `LFGFrameTab.lua`.
  - Added WhatsApp/Telegram-inspired messaging upgrades in **Conversations** (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`): Pin/Unpin conversations (`Pin Conversation` with session-only Battle.net pins), deep in-chat message history search (`Match:` snippet), automatic per-thread unsent drafts (`[Draft]`), and 1-click customizable **Quick Replies** (`Quick` templates menu).
  - Added interactive per-message timestamp menu (`[HH:MM]` hyperlink in `UI/SocialPanels.lua`) for **Quote Reply** (`> ...`), **Pin Message** banner at the top of a conversation, **Save to Personal Notes** (Telegram Saved Messages style), and single-message copy, plus live Battle.net opposing-faction header status in Conversations.
  - Added calendar date separators (`Today` / `Yesterday` / `DD/MM/YYYY`) in `UI/SocialPanels.lua` message logs, **Mark Unread** (`Chat:MarkThreadUnread` in `Providers/Chat.lua`) in the conversation list context menu, and rich Battle.net presence notices (`Online: <Character>`, `On App / Other Game`) in the conversation header.
  - Reduced Conversations memory/GC churn (`Chat:GetThreadsList` reusable `self._threadsListOut`, bounded `pongSentAt`/`nackSentAt` caches in `Providers/Chat.lua`), fixed stuck red unread border on selected rows in `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, and added a scroll-to-bottom button (`v`) plus smart `100+/180` character counter in the composer.
  - Preserved WoW item/spell/quest hyperlinks inside local **Personal Notes** (`Chat:SavePersonalNote`), forwarded standard WoW hyperlinks in `msgFrame:OnHyperlinkClick` (`UI/SocialPanels.lua`), and added 2-step confirmation for **Clear Notes** and conversation **Clear** to prevent accidental deletion.
  - Added `@mention` detection and gold `[@]` badge highlighting (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`) in Private Rooms, Alliance Chat, Guild, and Group channels whenever another player mentions your character name.
  - Added 1-click `+ Battle.net Friend` submenu in Conversations `+ New` (`UI/SocialPanels.lua`), added `Confirm Clear?` protection to `+ New -> Clear`, and made `@mention` matching UTF-8-safe for accented Portuguese/Spanish names.
  - Fixed the Conversations header `Note` (`btnTargetCard`) button in `UI/SocialPanels.lua` to resolve canonical `lookupKey` for composite Forever names and support Battle.net (`BN_WHISPER`) conversations directly.
  - Fixed shared-array mutation between `Minimap` unread tooltip and Conversations list in `Chat:GetThreadsList` (`Providers/Chat.lua`), cleared stale pinned message banners when a conversation is emptied, and synced `btnQuickReply` enabled state when no conversation is selected (`UI/SocialPanels.lua`).
  - Cleared stale `hasMention` flags consistently across `Chat:SetThreadMuted`, `Chat:MarkAllRead`, and `Chat:ClearHistory` (`Providers/Chat.lua`).
  - Fixed Battle.net KString (`|Ks...|k`) mangling in `Compat.BNetWhisper` (`Core/Compat.lua`), preserving Blizzard's Real ID display token intact when opening `SendBNetTell` instead of stripping `|` into `"Ks118 k"`.
  - Added click-and-drag scrolling to the Conversations message log (`UI/SocialPanels.lua`) alongside mouse-wheel scrolling, with `OnUpdate` detached automatically on mouse release or hide.
  - Updated `Chat:GetOfficialDestinationInfo` (`Providers/Chat.lua`) and `OpenRowMenu` (`UI/MainFrame.lua`) to display Battle.net whisper labels instead of `/w` when targeting opposing-faction Battle.net friends.
  - Fixed `OpenSocialMetaModal` (`UI/SocialPanels.lua`) losing `charKey` and Battle.net friend metadata when opened from Watchlist rows, highlighted active `Good Partner (+)` toggle state, and emitted `WATCHLIST_UPDATED` on `Store:SetSocialMeta` (`Core/Store.lua`).
  - Changed the Watchlist group/tag filter button (`UI/MainFrame.lua`) from a dropdown menu to direct 1-click cycling (`All Groups` -> `Favorites` -> custom groups), matching the `Sort` button behavior.
  - Integrated Group Finder with official WoW LFG (`Clan:SyncOfficialActiveLfg`, `Clan:ScanOfficialLfgResults`, `Clan:OpenOfficialLfgForGuild`, `"OFFICIAL"` broadcast option, `[Official LFG]` + `[CLAN]` badges), synchronized Guild Status tags (`LFG`/`MPLUS`/`RAID`/`PVP`) with active Guild LFG listings, and added `±5` nearby-level LFG toast notifications (`clanLfgAlertNearLevel`) with a Settings toggle and `[?]` help button.
  - Prevented self-whispering by routing self-targets and adding a WhatsApp/Telegram-style permanent `"notes:self"` (**Personal Notes (You)**) thread in Conversations with clickable per-note `[x]` deletion (`delnote:`) and `"Clear Notes"`.
  - Fixed remaining forward-call declarations (`UpdateClanPanel` in `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `IsCrowdMode` / `GetBystanderCooldownSeconds` in `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`) and removed redundant `FriendsFrame_ShowSubFrame` closure wrapping in `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`.
  - Fixed Guild overview online class summary truncation (`math.min(4, #clsParts)`) so all online classes are counted and displayed (with UTF-8-safe compact labels when narrow and a full `"Classes Online"` tooltip breakdown).
  - Added prominent version update notifications: 1-on-1 direct/Battle.net chat peers now feed `Clan:NoteRemoteVersion`, newer versions trigger an amber `VERSION_UPDATE` Notification Toast (`Click: open update link`), a Guild overview badge, a compact embedded header update badge with tooltip, and highlighted `[SC vX.Y.Z!]` tags in Guild member rows.
- **Context Menu Chat & Cross-Faction Whisper Fixes (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):**
  - Fixed `"Open in SocialCentral Chat"` when right-clicking players inside embedded `CommunitiesFrame` (`J`) or other non-chat hosts by cleanly restoring the host chrome and opening the standalone window focused on the selected conversation (removing the legacy `_G.ChatFrame1` fallback).
  - Normalized Watchlist keys in `OpenRowMenu` so confirmed addon users on Watchlist show `[SC]` / `[SC*]` badges, display `"Open in SocialCentral Chat"`, and switch the row message button to a primary SocialCentral icon/label (`"Chat in SocialCentral"`).
  - Routed cross-faction whisper actions (`sameFaction == false`) automatically to `Compat.BNetWhisper` so opposing-faction Battle.net friends open working Battle.net whispers instead of Blizzard-blocked `/w CharacterName` prompts.
  - Added immediate `UI:QueueRefresh(false)` on Watchlist and `GOOD` / `AVOID` reputation context menu actions for instant row badge feedback.
- **Target Radar Toggle, Watchlist Auto-Tracking & First-Time Tutorial (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added `Spotter:IsPlayerTracked` and `Spotter:UntrackPlayer` so context menus across `MainFrame`, `Watchlist`, `SocialPanels`, and `LFGFrameTab` dynamically toggle between `"Track on Radar"` and `"Untrack from Radar"`.
  - Automatically enable Target Radar tracking when adding characters to the Watchlist and present a one-time proximity explanation dialog (`spotterRadarTutorialShown`) + Settings `"View Radar Tutorial"` button with `[?]` help button.
- **Stale Guild Roster Disconnect Protection (`Providers/Social.lua`):**
  - Reordered `Social:Scan()` scanners to run `ScanCharacterFriends` before `ScanGuild`, preventing stale cached `GetGuildRosterInfo` online flags from overriding real-time character friend logoffs or triggering false online alerts.
- **Friend Disconnect / Logoff Detection & Alert Accuracy (`Providers/Social.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Resolved character logoff alert glitch where Battle.net friends exiting their character or returning to desktop/character selection flipped tracking keys, triggering false "Online" toasts at the exact moment of logout.
  - Prioritized Battle.net account IDs as consistent presence anchors and decoupled game account state (`wow_forever`) from Desktop App presence, preventing false logins and properly identifying true logoffs.
  - Added dedicated Friend Disconnect floating toast notifications (`FRIEND_OFFLINE`, default on) and optional chat notices (`friendOfflineAlertChat`, default off) with clickable `[Social]` shortcuts, full trilingual coverage, and settings toggles with `[?]` help buttons.
- **Lightweight Zero-Idle Performance Optimizations (`Providers/Nearby.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Alts.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`):**
  - Replaced the continuous 8-second `Nearby` scan ticker with zero-idle event-driven sweeps: solo players with the window closed consume 0ms background CPU, only checking party/raid members when grouped.
  - Replaced unconditional 30-second Guild event reminder tickers with dynamic event-driven scheduling (`Clan:ScheduleNextEventReminderCheck`), sleeping up to 10 minutes when no guild events are approaching.
  - Optimized the 60-second social roster refresh ticker to sleep silently whenever neither `SocialCentralFrame` nor Blizzard's `FriendsFrame` is shown on screen.
  - Optimized `Alts` module to unregister all 8 chat listener events (`CHAT_MSG_SAY`, `CHAT_MSG_YELL`, `CHAT_MSG_WHISPER`, `CHAT_MSG_GUILD`, etc.) immediately once the player's surname is confirmed or already known at login.
  - Optimized `MainFrame` `UpdateClanPanel`: skips sorting 500+ guild members when browsing non-roster views (*Events*, *Diplomacy*, *Hierarchy*, *Professions*, *Locks*), avoiding duplicate sorting and table allocations.
  - Optimized `MainFrame` 1-second refresh ticker: returns immediately on tick 1 when viewing non-who tabs (e.g. `social`, `clan`, `alts`, `buffs`).
- **Group Avoid Warning on Party/Raid Join (`Providers/Group.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added automatic alert (`groupAvoidAlert`) upon party or raid join: checks newly joined players against your SocialCentral Avoid list (including linked alts) and alerts you immediately with a warning toast and chat alert before dungeons or raids start.
- **Classic Dungeon Acronym Recognition & LFG Anti-Spam Filter (`Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Expanded dungeon keyword dictionary with classic acronyms (`vc`, `dm`, `strat`, `scholo`, `zg`, `mc`, `bwl`, `aq`, `naxx`, `ony`, `bfd`, `rfk`, `rfd`, `st`, `kara`).
  - Added public LFG chat anti-spam filter (`lfgAntiSpam`): suppresses WTS advertisements, GDKP carries, and real-money boosts from Group Finder and chat scans.
- **Guild Roster Inactivity Filter (`Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added Guild Roster filters for inactive members (`Inactive > 30d`, `Inactive > 60d`, `Inactive > 90d`) powered by safe `GetGuildRosterLastOnline` queries.
- **Alliance & Clan Chat Quick Command (`Core/Init.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Registered `/al <msg>` slash command shortcut for quick Alliance/Clan chat broadcast or opening the Alliance conversation tab.

## [0.2.13] - 2026-11-16
### Added & Improved
- **Nameplate Stance Badges, Animated Red Target Arrow & Ultra-Short Sonar Notification Audio (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`):**
  - Attached prominent, distance-scaled stance badges (`MESMA GUILDA` / `GUILDA ALIADA` / `GUILDA INIMIGA` / `WATCHLIST` / `AMIGO` / `BLOQUEADO`) and a pulsing, animated downward red target arrow above selected or focused player nameplates (including bystander targets) with `IsPlateAnchorSafe` (`IsForbidden` / `IsAnchoringRestricted`) instance guards, `OnShow`/`OnHide` `OnUpdate` detachment, and a one-time manual `Shift + V` / `V` nameplate tutorial (`spotterPlateTutorialShown`, `View Nameplate Tutorial` in Settings).
  - Replaced whisper-like `TELL_MESSAGE` / `RAID_WARNING` notification sounds across `NotificationToast`, `ClanSpotter`, `Chat`, `Clan`, `Social`, and `Store` with an ultra-short, distinct sonar chime (`SOUNDKIT.MAP_PING`) and subtle threat blip (`SOUNDKIT.INTERFACE_SOUND_LOST_TARGET_UNIT`) so addon alerts never imitate incoming whispers or double-chime over official Blizzard whispers.
- **Confirmed-Only Addon Chat Routing & Unified Addon/Official Messaging Audit (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`):**
  - Audited and hardened both `SCentral1` addon messaging and official Blizzard chat routing: gated clickable `[Chat]` guild LFG hyperlinks (`|Haddon:SCentral:w:...|h`) and unit/list context options behind `Chat:IsPlayerAddonConfirmed(...)` with automatic official `/w` fallback for non-addon players; fixed `OpenDirectConversation` `autoProbe` so `+ Chat -> Start & Verify` immediately probes unprobed peers; filtered `+ Chat -> Pick Online Friend` strictly to online players without BattleTag leaks.
  - Hardened `TryDirectOfficialSend` and `IsInAllowedOfficialChannel` for WoW Forever (`16001`): prevented invalid `"PARTY"` channel sends while inside a Raid, added `C_BattleNet.SendWhisper` / `BNSendWhisper` dual fallback, echoed manually sent Guild/Party/Raid messages directly into Conversations when `chatMirrorChannels` is disabled, and added case-insensitive / hyphen-normalized Forever target rejection recovery in `CHAT_MSG_SYSTEM`.
  - Fixed duplicate `GROUP_ROSTER_UPDATE` registration in `Providers/Clan.lua` (preserving both automatic full-dungeon `LFX` closing and group version checks), unified shared `RecordClanThrottleStrike` / `backoffUntil` circuit-breakers between `Clan` and `Chat`, and resolved bare Forever guild sender names to full `"FirstName Surname"` before `MarkPeerVerified`.
- **Streamlined Target HUD, Non-Overlapping Stackable Toasts & Native Resource Safety (`UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `Providers/WorldBuffs.lua`, `Providers/Alts.lua`, `Providers/Nearby.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`, `AGENTS.md`):**
  - Permanently removed Blizzard-blocked `SetRaidTarget` submenus (`ADDON_ACTION_BLOCKED`), programmatic `SetCVar("nameplateShow*")` calls (`ADDON_ACTION_FORBIDDEN`), guildcomer welcome popups, and redundant `PARTY_INVITE_REQUEST` toasts.
  - Redesigned the floating Target Radar pill (class crest medallion, full-row highlight tint, dedicated 58px header clearance so 1-click `[X]` and cycle buttons never collide with the secure target button, single unified `Whisper` button, and `[Reset Position]`) and anchored `NotificationToast:LayoutStack()` cleanly below `SocialCentralSpotterFrame` so notifications never overlap the Radar HUD.
  - Replaced `WorldBuffs` polling ticker with exact-expiry single timers (`ScheduleNextExpiryCheck`), throttled solo closed-UI `Nearby` sweeps, added event-driven Hearthstone (`6948`) readiness to Character (`C`) Dossier, Wait-Free Alts unclaimed mail/rested XP/low bag badges, World Buff catch-up sync (`WBR`/`WBC`), `O`/`I`/`J`/`C`/`M` polish, and smart `/who` cooldown conservation.
- **Zero-Delay Blizzard Chat Echo & 1-Click Official Chat (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added `chatDirectOfficialSend` (hardware-event only synchronous send for `/w`, Battle.net, Guild, Officer, Party, Raid, and Instance chat inside Conversations) plus immediate `0ms` `[SC]` chat frame echoing across configurable Blizzard chat tabs and `[SC] Addon Chat` toggles on Blizzard's native `ChatConfigFrame`.
  - **Auto Instance Exit Sync & Guild/Calendar Event Reminders:** Fixed `ApplyAutoInstanceStatus` resetting `TAG~NONE` to guildmates upon exiting instances via `ZONE_CHANGED_NEW_AREA`, and added configurable `15m` / `5m` reminders (`clanEventReminders`) for confirmed SocialCentral Guild Events and Blizzard Calendar events.
  - **World Buff Live Countdown Toast & Smart Minimap/Compartment Click:** Added live-ticking countdown Toasts with a 1-click **Announce in Chat** draft button when local Classic World Buff NPC yells fire, and added configurable smart Left-Click routing (`minimapLeftClickAction`: unread chat or hub) shared cleanly across the Minimap icon and Addon Compartment.
- **1-Click Hardware-Event Official Chat Send (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added `chatDirectOfficialSend` (enabled by default, toggleable in Settings with `?` help) so clicking **Send** or pressing **Enter** in **Conversations** immediately sends non-empty replies to non-addon `/w` whispers, Battle.net friends, Guild, Officer, Party, Raid, and Instance channels without requiring a second `Enter` in Blizzard's chat bar.
  - Enforces strict Blizzard hardware-event and anti-automation guardrails (`isHardwareEvent == true`, combat and `InChatMessagingLockdown()` checks, public `SAY`/`YELL`/`CHANNEL` exclusion), falling back cleanly to pre-filling Blizzard's native chat box (preserving any typed draft even on Battle.net or `/w Name-Surname` Forever targets).
- **Approximate Player-Relative World Anchoring on Minimap (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`):**
  - Added a low-overhead (`2.0s` throttled, zero-allocation) player-relative world anchor estimator for nearby non-addon / out-of-party targets using the player's own live position, facing, visible nameplate/soft-target sightline bearing, and measured range (`_estWorldX/Y`, `~` badge, and smooth signal-age opacity fade from `1.0` -> `0.72` -> `0.45`).
  - Minimap target pins now remain anchored in world space and update naturally as the player moves or turns (including fixing the `rotateMinimap` rotation sign), updating even when targets temporarily leave view rather than freezing at the top arc.
  - Added mouse-wheel cycling directly on the floating Target Radar pill to switch between nearby targets cleanly.
- **Automatic Group Peer Discovery, Thread Auto-Merge, Smart Cross-Faction Invites, Toast Quick Actions & LFG Apply Templates (`Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`):**
  - Scoped version discovery cooldowns (`VERSION_MIN_INTERVAL`) per channel (`GUILD` vs `PARTY`/`RAID`/`INSTANCE_CHAT`) and cleared group channel timers cleanly on group leave so new party/raid teammates running SocialCentral are discovered automatically within ~3 seconds (`HasUnverifiedGroupMembers`).
  - Added automatic single-firstName -> `"FirstName Surname"` thread merging (`MergeSingleFirstThreadInto`, `ReconcileSingleFirstThreads`), preserving saved history, unread counts, and muted state; added **"Message in SocialCentral"** directly inside Blizzard right-click unit menus.
  - Upgraded `Compat.Invite` with automatic cross-faction / alternate-character **Battle.net Game Account (`BNInviteFriend`)** fallback when available.
  - Added quick **Mark Read** button + right-click actions (`Open`, `Mark Read`, `Mute`, `Dismiss`) on floating Toasts, a combat-safe `ns:RunAfterCombat` helper, and channel validation preserving typed drafts.
  - Added `lfgAutoApplyDraft` (enabled by default with Settings `?` help) pre-filling Level, Class, and Role when messaging Group Finder (`I`) leaders, plus `[MATCH]` highlights on Global Chat LFG rows matching configured dungeon keywords.

### Changed
- **Full Forever Two-Word Names (`FirstName Surname`) Across All Chat Logs (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):**
  - Updated direct addon messages, location pings, Private Rooms, Cross-Guild Alliance chat (payload cap raised from `25` to `36` chars), mirrored Guild/Party/Raid/Whisper logs, saved whisper history loading, and `/w` action button labels to consistently display both first name and surname (`FirstName Surname`), avoiding ambiguity on WoW: Forever.

## [0.2.12] - 2026-11-16
### Added
- **Social (`O`) Side-Tab & Unread Conversations Identification (`UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Smart routing on the Social (`O`) side tab: clicking the SocialCentral side tab when unread messages exist jumps straight to the **Conversations** tab instead of Watchlist, and hovering it displays a live breakdown of unread senders and counts plus pending room invites.
  - Added an **Unread** filter option (`Unread`) and a quick one-click **Unread (`N`)** toggle button in the Conversations top bar, plus a **Mark Read** button in the active conversation header and automatic mark-read when clicking a conversation row or focusing the reply input.
  - Upgraded conversation rows with a crimson unread pill badge pinned to the right edge (never truncated by long names), a left unread dot and crimson accent bar, and brighter message preview text.
  - Highlighted the `Chat` (`Conversas`) tab counter in crimson-red with an unread sender tooltip so unread messages stand out clearly from total-count tabs like `Watchlist` and `Social`.
- **Persistent Addon Peer Registry & Automatic Login Handshake (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Unified `SocialCentralDB.addonPeers` (60-day TTL, 500 entries) and `clan.peers` into a persistent registry so friends and guildmates who have run SocialCentral remain recognized across `/reload`, relogs, and offline transitions without requiring repeated manual verification clicks before chatting.
  - Added silent Battle.net login auto-discovery (`PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD` after 10s, out-of-combat, strictly over silent `C_BattleNet.SendGameData`, `>= 8s` spacing, cap 25), safe 1-shot incoming `/w` reply verification (`allowWhisperReply`) to unlock in-addon direct chat automatically without chat system errors, and passive Guild P2P gossip digests (up to 8 recent guild peers bundled inside existing probabilistic `VR~` version packets).
  - Unified Battle.net and character `WHISPER` threads (`FindLinkedDirectThread` across opens, incoming `/w`, Battle.net whispers, and addon replies) so conversations with friends switching between Battle.net and character whisper do not split into duplicate rows.
  - Enriched mouseover player tooltips with passive `[SocialCentral · Registered]` indicators and automatic Allied/Enemy Guild tags.
- **Target Radar Coordinate Sharing Upgrade & Optional Blizzard 3D Pin (`Core/Init.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added a one-time profile migration (`spotterSharePosV1Migrated`) enabling guild/friend map coordinate sharing (`spotterSharePosition = true`) on existing profiles automatically.
  - Added an optional **Mark / Clear 3D Waypoint** toggle in the Target Radar right-click context menu using native `C_Map.SetUserWaypoint` + `C_SuperTrack` for targets with map coordinates (throttled `>= 1.5s`, auto-clearing under `8m` or on target dismiss/clear).

### Changed
- **Chat & Peer Detection CPU/Memory Optimization (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`):**
  - Replaced per-render Battle.net friend/account scans (`FindBNetWoWGameAccount*`, `ResolveBNetSender`) with an event-invalidated `O(1)` lookup cache (`EnsureBNetLookupCache`, 4s TTL), added dirty caching to unread thread and invite counters (`GetUnreadSummary`, `GetUnreadDirectCount`), pooled `GetThreadsList` item tables, throttled message deduplication sweeps (`>= 30s`), memoized `GetPeerAddonState` and parsed character targets, eliminated wrapper table allocations in `GetSavedPeerRecord`, pre-compiled `CHAT_MSG_SYSTEM` matching with a 10s active-probe fast-exit, and added `O(1)` Forever full-name resolution prior to Guild Roster scans in `ProcessPeerVersion`.

### Fixed
- **Forever (`16001`) Surname & Shard Whisper Routing (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`):**
  - Fixed `"FirstName Surname"` characters (such as `"Aiden Dell"`) triggering `No player named '<FirstName>'` or `No player named '<FirstName> CAMWOW...Realm...'` system errors when sending addon packets: stripped `-CAMWOW...Realm...` shard suffixes across `BuildForeverTargetCandidates`, `EnsureBNetLookupCache`, `RecordResolvedWhisperTarget`, and `HandleIncomingAddonPacket`, addressed two-word characters via `"FirstName Surname"` / `"FirstName-Surname"`, sent both `WHISPER` and `C_BattleNet.SendGameData` cleanly in `SendDirectPacket`, and matched space-mapped system errors on rejection.
- **Target Radar & Chat Non-Addon Peer Exceptions (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`):**
  - Replaced unsolicited background `DPING` whisper probes and 1.5s auto-open delays with strict exception handling for players without SocialCentral: `ShouldAutoProbe` now probes only previously registered peers, login discovery sends strictly over Battle.net GameData for saved peers, `VerifyBeforeOfficialSend` opens `/w` immediately on user click, the chat composer stays visible with an instant `/w` action/placeholder for non-addon peers, and the Radar `Whisper` button routes directly to `/w` when the target lacks SocialCentral.
  - Separated Target Radar Minimap pins cleanly between exact group/peer coordinates and non-addon proximity targets: coordinate-less targets skip `C_Map`/`GetPlayerFacing()` lookups entirely (500ms idle cadence) and display on a zero-spin fixed upper Minimap proximity arc with a compact `<distance>m` badge.
- **Target Radar Minimap Circle Pin & Action Drawer (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`):**
  - Fixed Minimap target pins spinning around the Minimap when turning the character by locking non-coordinate target headings/positions in world space instead of adding live `GetPlayerFacing()` to relative screen bearings on every tick.
  - Added `Safe.XY`, enabled `spotterSharePosition = true` by default, fixed `UnitPosition` / `C_Map` group token resolution in `GetTargetKinematics`, and resolved Forever first-name guild position broadcasts (`POS~` / `OpenMapLoc`) to full `"FirstName Surname"` keys so nearby/group/guild targets plot accurately on the Minimap and update smoothly (`0.15s` HUD pin cadence).
  - Enlarged Minimap target pins to `16x16` with stance glow halos above Minimap overlays and widened `HUD_WIDTH` (`268px`) so localized drawer buttons (`Sussurrar`, `Convidar`) no longer truncate.
- **Direct Addon Peer Verification & Battle.net/WHISPER Dual Transport (`Providers/Chat.lua`):**
  - Fixed `SendRoomPacket` treating boolean `false` or unconfirmed `C_BattleNet.SendGameData` returns as delivered and skipping `WHISPER` addon routing completely (affecting both Battle.net threads and Character threads linked to a Battle.net friend).
  - Updated `DPING` / `DPONG` handshakes to send via both `WHISPER` Forever target candidates and `C_BattleNet.SendGameData` (until a confirmed `BN_CHAT_MSG_ADDON` reply proves Battle.net GameData delivery), added `msgId` deduplication for incoming `DM`/`DPIN` packets, and kept `FirstName` / `BN_WHISPER` / `WHISPER` thread reconciliation unified so verifying from either tab activates in-addon direct chat reliably.
  - Preserved unread conversation badges when switching to the `Chat` tab instead of silently clearing the active thread's unread count before the user inspects or clicks the conversation.

## [0.2.11] - 2026-11-16
### Added
- **Game-Native Guild Hierarchy, Rank Ladder & Leadership Controls (`Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Captures real in-game guild ranks (`GetGuildRosterInfo` `rankIndex`, `GuildControlGetNumRanks`, `GuildControlGetRankName`) and native WoW permissions (`IsGuildLeader`, `CanEditOfficerNote`, `CanEditGuildInfo`, `CanEditMOTD`, `CanEditPublicNote`, `CanGuildPromote`, `CanGuildDemote`, `CanGuildInvite`, `CanEditGuildEvent`) inside a new **Hierarchy** (`Hierarquia`) view in the Guild window (`J`).
  - Restricted Guild Diplomacy (defining, editing, marking, or removing Allied and Enemy guilds across UI, Target Radar, and incoming `CDIP`/`CDEL` guild packets) **by default strictly to the Guild Leader** (`diplomacyMode = "LEADER"`), while allowing only the Guild Leader to delegate Diplomacy, Guild Info editing, Event Call moderation, and automatic Alliance Chat rank access to Game Officers or any specific real guild rank (`CHIR` guild-isolated sync packet with Guild Master sender validation).
  - Fixed `Clan:IsGuildLeader()` so rank index `1` (Vice-GM/Officer) is no longer mistakenly treated as Guild Master (`rankIndex == 0`).
  - Added native Guild Leadership context menu actions directly on Guild Roster rows (`Promote in Guild`, `Demote in Guild`, `Assign Game Rank` to any selectable in-game rank, and `Edit Guild Public Note`), strictly executed on user clicks outside combat.
  - Fixed Portuguese/Spanish grammar in Diplomacy stance label (`"Guilda Inimiga"` / `"Hermandad Enemiga"`).
- Added `/sc toast [invite|friend|watchlist|buff|queue|clear]` test slash command variants to preview and reposition floating notification toasts directly from chat without opening Settings.
- Added multi-toast vertical stacking in `UI/NotificationToast.lua`: concurrent notifications now stack cleanly on screen simultaneously without replacing each other, automatically sorted so longer-duration items (such as 20s Room Invites) anchor at the top while shorter-duration alerts appear stacked below.
- Centered all layout elements across all notification toasts (centered title, centered body/subtitle, centered Accept and Decline action buttons, and centered symmetric margins).
- Increased Room Invite toast duration to 20 seconds with hover-pause to provide plenty of time to review room details and click Accept or Decline.
- Added unified drag movement across all stacked toast pills: dragging any visible toast smoothly repositions the entire notification stack.
- Added Minimap and World Map target pins in `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`: detected players and position-sharing peers appear with distinct stance-colored circular borders and class emblems (Friend = light blue, Watchlist = gold, Guild = golden-yellow, Ally = green, Enemy = red, Blocked = magenta/dark red), edge-clamping on the Minimap, and full hover tooltips.
- Added opt-in player position sharing (`spotterSharePosition`) in `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua` and `UI/Settings.lua` intercompatible with `OpenMap` (`OpenMapLoc`) and `SocialCentral` (`SCentral1`), broadcasting player coordinates to guild members and friends at low frequency without CPU-intensive nameplate or visual sweeps.
- Added `spotterMapPins` and `spotterSharePosition` configurable checkboxes in Settings with trilingual `[?]` help buttons (`enUS`, `ptBR`, `esES`).
- Added custom category-specific `.tga` notification medallion icons in `Media/` (`Media/toast-mail.tga` gold-filigree sealed parchment envelope for messages, `Media/toast-party.tga` three-hero Warrior/Mage/Archer ornate medallion for group & room invites, `Media/toast-online.tga` glowing emerald crystal orb medallion for Friend/Watchlist online alerts, and `Media/toast-glow.tga` Gaussian radial bloom).
- Added a Left-Click Chooser menu on the Minimap button in `UI/Minimap.lua` (`Open Main Window` / `Open Settings`, with 100%-opaque fallback popover and trilingual localization) so players can pick directly between the unified Main Window and Settings on Left-Click.
### Fixed
- **Conversations Peer Addon Detection & Blizzard Compliance Audit (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Fixed 1-on-1 WoW Forever peer detection (`DPING` / `DPONG`) stopping on space-separated candidate #1 (`"FirstName Surname"`): `Compat.BuildForeverTargetCandidates` now prioritizes the core `FirstName` C-engine character name first, followed by full, hyphenated, and shard-qualified variants, paired with `CHAT_MSG_SYSTEM` missing-player candidate disqualification, per-retry candidate rotation, session-verified `resolvedWhisperTargets` routing, extended `5.0s` ping timeout, and Battle.net `C_BattleNet.SendGameData` cross-shard fast-path lookup on character whisper threads.
  - Strengthened `MarkPeerVerified` Forever surname reconciliation window (`<= 25s` after probe) while preserving the peer's full `"FirstName Surname"` display name instead of overwriting it with `"FirstName"`.
  - Fixed truncated `ChatPanel` compact controls in the Friends (`O`) embedded pane (`Silenciar`, compact official-destination hint, and `Whisper (/w FirstName)` with full hover tooltip).
  - Hardened Blizzard Add-On Policy compliance across `ClanSpotter`, `Settings`, and `Chat`: added full `InCombatLockdown()` / `InChatMessagingLockdown()` / `isStoppedThisSession` / `backoffUntil` + `Enum.SendAddonMessageResult.AddonMessageThrottle` circuit-breaker guards to `OpenMapLoc` / `SCentral1` position broadcasts (`Spotter`) and direct chat sends (`Chat`), registered the `"OpenMapLoc"` prefix explicitly, guarded the delayed official-chat open callback against combat lockdown, and fixed `Spotter:HandleIncomingPosition` storing table keys instead of normalized string `lookupKey`/`displayName`.

### Changed
- **Modernized Blizzard-Grade UI Architecture (`UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Replaced heavy boxy `common-button-list-large` list row borders with clean alternating slate zebra rows, hairline dividers, and soft gold hover highlights, eliminating multi-border visual clutter while remaining ultra-lightweight on low-end PCs.
  - Replaced uniform classic crimson `UIPanelButtonTemplate` buttons on utility controls and navigation menus with clear visual hierarchy: dark charcoal-bronze matte buttons for secondary/filter controls, sleek `"nav"` sidebar tabs with native WoW mini-icons and active gold accent bars, circular gold-ringed `[?]` help badges (`Media/dot.tga`), and embossed crimson-gold styling reserved for true primary Call-to-Action buttons (`+ Invite`, `+ Event Call`, `+ Add Guild`, `+ Clans & Allies LFG`).
  - Upgraded Guild Hub rows (`CreatePeerRow` / `UpdatePeerRow`) with cropped `20x20` class crests, scannable color-coded rank tags, right-aligned zone/time/count columns, and a right-aligned personal rank badge inside `guildHeroBar`.
  - Fixed `peersToolBar` `historyBtn` `[?]` help button argument order overlap, increased `AutoFitButton` padding so `+ Invite` / `History` never truncate, and fixed Portuguese accents (`Configurações`, `Eventos & Calendário`, `Nível`, `há`).
