  - Expanded Guild Roster (`Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`) to include both online and offline guild members (so filtering by any rank such as `#0 Warchief` or searching by rank shows all members even when offline), changed default sort to **Online first -> Character Level descending (`Lv max -> min`) -> Rank**, and added rich `Filters` dropdown controls (Presence `Online`/`Offline`/`[SC]`/`Active Status`, Guild Rank `#0..#N`, Class, and Sort Order).
  - Polished the Friends/Social (`O`) window and unified SocialCentral's Obsidian-Bronze & Gold identity across all screens: replaced cramped scroll corner arrow buttons with a slim bronze-gold scrollbar, added `20x20` class crests to Watchlist/Social/Nearby rows, replaced truncated inline row action labels (`Sussur...`/`Convi...`) in compact mode with crisp letter/invite icon buttons, highlighted primary Add buttons (`+ Add`, `+ Friend`), and increased compact toolbar button padding so labels never clip.
- Optimized all visual & UI enhancements for low-end / basic PCs across `Media/`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, and `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`:
  - Downsized custom toast medallions (`toast-mail.tga`, `toast-party.tga`, `toast-online.tga`) to `64x64` and radial bloom (`toast-glow.tga`) to `32x32` (~78% VRAM reduction), and added a lightweight `16x16` `Media/dot.tga` circle texture so status dots and map pin backdrops/rings render directly without extra GPU `MaskTexture` shader passes.
  - Restricted `groupfinder-background` carved stone backdrop layers in `Theme:SyncDepthPolish` to larger structural containers (`>= 120x80` and not `_scNoStone`, skipping Alts' 144 pooled inventory buttons), skipped sheen/bevel overlays on pooled list rows and micro-buttons (`< 32px`), reused shared `ColorMixin` objects across gradients (`ApplySafeGradient` / `ApplyTexGradient`) to eliminate GC allocations, and dirty-checked toast card gradient updates so only the timer bar updates each tick.
  - Eliminated per-tick table and `Vector2D` allocations in `ClanSpotterHUD` map pin projection (`PrepareMinimapContext`, reusable pin/peer/vector pools), skipped pin updates when Minimap/WorldMap are hidden, dirty-checked pin anchors/colors/icons/macros, and throttled compass-tick map pin refreshes (`>= 1.5s`).
- Upgraded UI visual depth and RPG cohesion across `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, and `UI/Settings.lua`: added carved stone inset textures (`groupfinder-background`), subtle vertical ambient depth gradients and top metallic bevels across headers/surfaces/buttons, tapered gold section header rules in Settings and modal dialogs, circular status orbs + left status bars on list/group/guild/diplomacy/conversation rows, amber accent stripes on Guild Events/Calendar rows, item slot hover highlights in Alts 2D inventory grid, framed active World Buff icons, Gaussian stance glow on Target Radar pill + drawer divider, active header shelf & tab strip dividers in `SocialCentralFrame`, and seamless full-canvas stone framing on single-tab embedded panels (`C`, `J`, `M`).
- Upgraded `UI/NotificationToast.lua` to an epic Blizzard-styled card layout (`TOAST_WIDTH = 396`, carved stone background texture + smooth horizontal ambient light wash, Gaussian medallion halo, tapered center title flourish divider, recessed gradient timer bar, symmetric `64px` horizontal text insets, wrapped auto-expanding body height, and zero overlap against left medallions or `[X]` close buttons).
- Removed rotating compass arrow and bearing text from Target Radar (`ClanSpotterHUD`) in favor of a clean circular class/stance badge and pure distance calculator (`~12m`, etc.) color-coded by proximity tier, reducing ticker frequency and eliminating heavy animation CPU/RAM overhead.

## [0.2.10] - 2026-11-15
### Added
- **Per-Player Notification Bell Toggle & Unified Watchlist/Friend Login Toasts (`Media/bell-on.tga`, `Media/bell-off.tga`, `Core/Init.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added clickable **Bell (`bell-on.tga`) / Slashed Bell (`bell-off.tga`)** notification icon right beside each character name in window `O` (`Watchlist` & `Social` lists) plus right-click menu mute toggles so players can silence or enable login Toasts per person in one click (and opt in per-guildmate without guild-wide spam).
  - Upgraded `Store:NotifyWatchlistOnline` to emit floating top-of-screen `WATCHLIST_ONLINE` Toasts by default (`alerts.toast = true`), and upgraded default `friendOnlineAlertMode` from `"favorites"` to `"all"` (`friendAlertAllMigrated` one-time migration) so Friends, Battle.net, and Watchlist login Toasts appear automatically unless manually muted or disabled.
### Fixed
- Fixed Watchlist `NotifyWatchlistOnline` suppressing `FRIEND_ONLINE` top-of-screen Toasts when the same player existed in both Friends and Watchlist, fixed Battle.net disconnect status reset on Watchlist entries (`prevBNetWatchlistOnline`), initialized newly added Watchlist entries cleanly from `Social:GetDirectoryEntry`, and coordinated `CheckFriendOnlineTransitions` + `SyncWatchlistObservation` to eliminate duplicate alerts and `"unknown"` transition misses.

## [0.2.9] - 2026-11-15
### Added
- **Unified Friends Directory, Online Alerts, Native Notes & Favorites Overhaul (`Core/Init.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added unified `Store.socialDirectory` in `Providers/Social.lua` uniting online & offline WoW Character Friends, Battle.net Friends (WoW Forever, other realms/games, Launcher/Mobile App, and Offline), and online Guildmates with multi-signal deduplication (`guid` / `bnetAccountID` / `lookupKey`), preserved `Store.social` strictly-online compatibility, multi-game-account prioritization (`PickBestBNetGameAccount`), separate `guildNote`/`friendNote`/`bnetNote` tracking, `GetDirectoryStats()`, `GetDirectoryEntry()`, and `GetDirectoryList(opts)` (`smart`, `name`, `level`, `lastSeen` sorting).
  - Added configurable **Friend Online Alerts** (`friendOnlineAlertMode`: Favorites Only / All Friends / Off, `friendOnlineAlertToast`, `friendOnlineAlertChat`, `friendOnlineAlertSound`) with 8-second post-login warmup grace, 120-second per-friend anti-spam cooldown, `FRIEND_ONLINE` toast routing + `addon:SCentral:social:<who>` clickable chat links, and full Settings controls with trilingual `[?]` help buttons.
  - Added **Favorites (`*`)**, privacy-safe `friendPresence` (`lastSeen` stored strictly by `lookupKey` with 250-entry cap & 180-day TTL — never persisting BattleTag or Real ID), `Store:GetLinkedAltsForCharacter`, `Store:MigrateSocialSurnameRecords`, and dynamic Social Folder presets in `SocialPanels:OpenSocialMetaModal`.
  - Added combat-guarded native Blizzard Character Friend & Battle.net API wrappers in `Core/Compat.lua` (`AddCharacterFriend`, `RemoveCharacterFriend`, `SetCharacterFriendNote`, `SetBNetFriendNote`, `SetBNetFriendFavorite`, `GetPendingBNetInvites`, `RespondBNetInvite`) powering Social tab `+ Friend`, native Blizzard/BNet Friend Note editing, Remove Friend confirmation modal, Pending Battle.net Invite Accept/Decline banner, extended status dot colors (`away`, `busy`, `app`), Source/Folder/Offline filters, Sort selector, and trilingual `enUS`/`ptBR`/`esES` locales.
- **Multi-Layer Addon Notification System & Messaging Reliability (`Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/WorldBuffs.lua`, `UI/NotificationToast.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added compact, 100%-opaque, movable `UI/NotificationToast.lua` floating notification pill (`ADDON_MESSAGE_NOTIFY` with queue/coalescing, hover-pause auto-dismiss, category accent strip, 1-click navigation deferred safely after combat, inline `Accept`/`Decline` buttons for Room Invites, and `FlashClientIcon()` taskbar pulse).
  - Added clickable `[SC ...]` chat-frame summaries with `SetItemRef` hyperlinks (`thread:`, `room:`, `clanevt:`, `buffs`, `spotter`), numeric unread counter badges on the Minimap button and Social (`O`) / Guild (`J`) native side-tabs, `[off]` NACK feedback on direct messages when recipients disable in-addon direct chat, plus opt-in Guild Event (`EVT`) and organizer Event Signup (`SIG`) notifications.
  - Added granular Settings checkboxes with `[?]` help buttons (`Addon Message Notifications`, `Test Notification Toast`, `Play chime on Alliance Chat messages`, `Notify when guildmates post a new Guild Event`, `Notify me when guildmates sign up to my Events`) and complete `enUS`/`ptBR`/`esES` translations.
  - Added `[SC*]` recent-session addon badge indicator via `Chat:GetLookupKeyAddonBadge` across Watchlist, Social, Nearby, and Guild lists while keeping `[SC]` for live session-verified peers (including `ALM` cross-guild packets).
### Fixed
- Fixed `Store:GetResolvedSocialMeta` inheritance so direct `flag` tags no longer short-circuit inheriting `folder`/`note`/`favorite` from linked `mainMeta`, added `EnsureModal` button/input reset hygiene in `UI/MainFrame.lua`, and preserved Battle.net privacy by skipping `SavedVariables` persistence on Battle.net-only friends lacking a WoW character name.
- Fixed `AppendMessage` active-thread visibility detection (`UI:IsChatThreadVisible`) so switching to other SocialCentral tabs no longer suppresses unread badges or sound chimes, and automatically marks the open thread read when opening Conversations.
- Fixed WoW Forever two-word `isMe` false-unread detection in `Chat:ReceiveAllianceMessage`, enabled category-distinct sound chimes with 2s/2.5s anti-spam cooldowns (`ALLIANCE` sound support included), respected `spotterSound` on `SCT` Scout alerts, added rate-limited cross-guild `ALM` delivery with `msgId` deduplication to authorized Allied contacts, and prevented notification jumps from toggling shut already-open embedded `O`/`J` panels (`forceKeepOpen`).

## [0.2.8] - 2026-11-15
### Fixed
- **Conversations Peer Addon Detection & Blizzard Compliance Audit (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Fixed 1-on-1 WoW Forever peer detection (`DPING` / `DPONG`) stopping on space-separated candidate #1 (`"FirstName Surname"`): `Compat.BuildForeverTargetCandidates` now prioritizes the core `FirstName` C-engine character name first, followed by full, hyphenated, and shard-qualified variants, paired with `CHAT_MSG_SYSTEM` missing-player candidate disqualification, per-retry candidate rotation, session-verified `resolvedWhisperTargets` routing, extended `5.0s` ping timeout, and Battle.net `C_BattleNet.SendGameData` cross-shard fast-path lookup on character whisper threads.
  - Strengthened `MarkPeerVerified` Forever surname reconciliation window (`<= 25s` after probe) while preserving the peer's full `"FirstName Surname"` display name instead of overwriting it with `"FirstName"`.
  - Fixed truncated `ChatPanel` compact controls in the Friends (`O`) embedded pane (`Silenciar`, compact official-destination hint, and `Whisper (/w FirstName)` with full hover tooltip).
  - Hardened Blizzard Add-On Policy compliance across `ClanSpotter`, `Settings`, and `Chat`: added full `InCombatLockdown()` / `InChatMessagingLockdown()` / `isStoppedThisSession` / `backoffUntil` + `Enum.SendAddonMessageResult.AddonMessageThrottle` circuit-breaker guards to `OpenMapLoc` / `SCentral1` position broadcasts (`Spotter`) and direct chat sends (`Chat`), registered the `"OpenMapLoc"` prefix explicitly, guarded the delayed official-chat open callback against combat lockdown, and fixed `Spotter:HandleIncomingPosition` storing table keys instead of normalized string `lookupKey`/`displayName`.

### Changed
- **Pass 4 Safe Reliability, Zero-Taint Targeting & UI Allocation Reduction (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/*.lua`, `UI/*.lua`):**
  - Replaced `SafeGetGuildRosterNameAndGuid` double-`pcall` + temp-table allocations with single-call vararg `ExtractRosterNameAndGuid`, preserved `Alts` `prev.zone` / `money >= 0` across loading screens, wrapped `C_Map.GetBestMapForUnit`, `GetNumGuildMembers()`, `GetFriendInfo` status, and `UnitLevel("player")` through `Safe.*`, fixed `ClanSpotter` `HasGuildChatLFG` lookups (`guildChatLFG` array bug), and deferred Watchlist `/who` surname rekeying (`toRekey` + index invalidation).
  - Unified two-word Forever self-identity resolution across `Clan`, `Chat`, and `MainFrame` `UpdateEventRow`, fixed `Chat:EnsureThread` surname upgrades, replaced `Chat:GetTotalUnread(true)` sorting with `CountUnreadThreadsAndInvites`, fixed `SocialPanels` direct-chat `+ Target` `UnitFullName` multi-assignment precedence, and pruned bounded cooldown/delivery/chat maps (`alertCooldowns`, `lfgAlertCooldowns`, `deliveryIndex`, `probeSentAt`/`probeFailures`, `PruneAndBoundByTimestamp`).
  - Removed protected `TargetByName` context-menu actions from `GroupPanel` and `MainFrame` `OpenRowMenu` (relying safely on `"Track on Radar"`), eliminated per-row table cloning in `MainFrame` `BuildFilteredRows` / `UpdateClanPanel` / `OpenRowMenu`, hoisted `CreateMemberRow`/`UpdateEventRow` click & layout guards, memoized `LFGFrameTab` `ProjectWorldMapCoord`, reused `Theme:HasAtlas` & `Compat.PlayerFaction`, merged duplicate `LFGFrameTab` `PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD` listeners, and deduplicated `HasSelfActiveListing()` inside `UpdateViewLayout` -> `UpdateBottomButtons`.
  - Debounced `MainFrame` (`searchBox`, `peerSearch`, `profSearch`) and `SocialPanels` (`roomInviteModal`, `AltsPanel` search/resize) inputs (`clearButton = true` on filter boxes), attached `Theme:CreateScrollList` thumb `OnUpdate` strictly during active drag, cached `Theme:ApplyBackdrop` / `SpotterHUD` macro & theme state, hid `allianceModalFrame` on main close, re-anchored `BuffsPanel` text when `btnReport` is hidden, guarded hidden `GroupPanel`/`clanPanel` updates, and eliminated duplicate synchronous `ShowHub`/`OpenTab`/`OpenEmbedded` refreshes.
- **Deep Pass 2 Target Radar, HUD 15 Hz & Minimap Zero-Allocation Optimizations (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`):**
  - Added allocation-free `Compat.ParseCharacterFields(raw)` over the memoized parse cache and replaced table-allocating `ParseCharacterName` calls across `ClanSpotter` (`AddPositiveCandidate`, `RebuildPositiveIndex`, `RebuildGroupMemberCache`, `UnitMatchesEntry`, `InspectUnit`, `LookupKnownPlayerGuildOrMembership`) and `ClanSpotterHUD` (`ReadCurrentTargetIdentity`).
  - Prevented synchronous `Compat.SyncRosterForeverNames()` full-guild scans on passive `NAME_PLATE_UNIT_ADDED` FirstName collisions (`useFastOnly = true` for passive nameplates), reused `Store.socialByGuid`/`Store.socialByLookup` directly in `RebuildPositiveIndex`, cached `Spotter._cachedPlayerGuildNorm`, short-circuited `TrimExcessTargets()` when `<= MAX_TARGETS`, pulled `target`/`focus`/`mouseover` reads outside `CleanupAbsentTargets`'s entry loop, used fast `entry.guid` checks + adaptive `0.22s`/`0.40s` `MeasureUnitDistance` intervals in `GetTargetKinematics`, and unified `RecordGateMissCooldown` inside `RefreshVisibleNameplates`.
  - Moved primitive dirty-state checks ahead of string concatenation and `string.format` in `ApplyCompassBarVisuals` (eliminating `~75/s` transient strings at `15 Hz`/`4 Hz` idle tracking), cached `SyncTickerState` `OnUpdate` closures, added layout/text dirty checks in `SpotterHUD:Refresh`, and switched `Minimap:PopulateTooltip` to `O(1)` `Social:GetCount`/`Nearby:GetCount`.
- **Raid/Crowd CPU & Distance Binary-Partitioning Optimizations (`Providers/Nearby.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`):**
  - Eliminated `Nearby`'s 400-entry linear upgrade/eviction loops (`~2000` table iterations per unseen sighting at cap) using `O(1)` `nearbyByGuid` lookup, incremental `nearbyCount`, single-pass eviction with an 8-entry cushion, debounced `GROUP_ROSTER_UPDATE` scans, combat/hidden-raid `NAME_PLATE_UNIT_ADDED` & `ScanGroup` throttling (skipping `raid1target..raid40target` on background ticks and closed UI), and `cacheOnly` tooltip guild reads on background tokens.
  - Switched `ClanSpotter` `MeasureUnitDistance` from a linear `O(9)` `IsItemInRange` loop to domain-partitioned binary probing (`d1` / `UnitInRange` narrowing down to `1–3` probes per update), isolated `GROUP_ROSTER_UPDATE` to `O(active)` `ReconcileGroupEntries()` instead of rebuilding the full positive index/plates on raid swaps, and throttled `FindLiveUnitForEntry` `nameplate..target` sweeps out of Crowd Mode.
  - Eliminated `15 Hz` per-frame `tostring` concatenations (`UpdateCompassVisuals` scalar signature checks), redundant `PLAYER_TARGET_CHANGED` full-list sorts (`O(1)` `spotter.entries` lookup + cached `_isTargetedBackdrop`), duplicate `ApplySecureMacroAttributes` calls in `Refresh`, and needless dirty-state resets.
- **Strict 6-Category Target Radar Scope, Blocked Player Alerts & Crowd/Raid O(1) Pre-Filtering (`Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Restricted Target Radar detection strictly to the 6 authorized categories (plus explicit click-to-track): **Friends** (`WoW` & `Battle.net`), **Watchlist**, **Blocked / Avoid** players (`SocialCentral` `AVOID` red flag and native `WoW` `/ignore` list via `C_FriendList`), **Own Guild** (`spotterNotifySameClan`), and **Ally / Enemy Diplomacy Guilds**, adding full `"BLOCKED"` HUD stance styling, drawer `Dismiss Target` quick button, context menu guards, `spotterNotifyBlocked` setting checkbox with trilingual `[?]` help, and `GetCounts()` integration.
  - Eliminated repeated 1-by-1 stranger and raid-member checks in crowds using an **Inverted Positive-Set Index** (`posGuidSet`, `posFirstSet`, `posKeySet`, memoized `posGuildRawCache` — deterministic zero-false-negative Bloom-filter principle with zero Lua table allocations on rejection), `O(1)` party/raid early short-circuit at `InspectUnit` entry when `spotterIgnoreGroup` is active, crowd-adaptive cooldown spacing (`25s` normal / `60s` raid or `>= 18` plates, `3.0s` prune loop, `12s` bounded chat cooldown, `0.25s` mouseover throttle), and a time-sliced work-budget inspection queue (`INSPECT_BATCH_LIMIT = 6` per `0.15s` slice) so cities, raids, and large groups never stutter.
- **Target Radar Re-Architected for Zero-Idle CPU & WoW Forever (`16001`) Reliability (`Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`):**
  - Replaced continuous `ClanSpotter` background `0.75s` `OnUpdate` scanning and unfiltered global `UNIT_TARGET` / `UNIT_NAME_UPDATE` / `UNIT_FACTION` event storms with a strictly event-driven architecture (`NAME_PLATE_UNIT_ADDED`, `FORBIDDEN_NAME_PLATE_UNIT_ADDED`, `NAME_PLATE_UNIT_REMOVED`, `PLAYER_TARGET_CHANGED`, `PLAYER_FOCUS_CHANGED`, `UPDATE_MOUSEOVER_UNIT`, `PLAYER_SOFT_*_CHANGED`, engine-filtered `RegisterUnitEvent("UNIT_TARGET", "player", "target", "focus")`, debounced `GUILD_ROSTER_UPDATE`, `CHAT_MSG_SAY/YELL/EMOTE`, and `CLAN_DIPLOMACY_UPDATED` / `WATCHLIST_UPDATED` / `SOCIAL_UPDATED`).
  - Added an O(1) active nameplate map (`activePlatesByUnit`, `activePlateUnitByGuid`), O(1) `groupMemberByGuid` / `groupMemberByKey` roster cache (eliminating 40-slot group loops per inspect), frame-coalesced `QueueBatchEmit` (`C_Timer.After(0)`), early bounded `bystanderCooldown` short-circuiting (`400`-entry cap), `Unknown`-name deferred retries, cached `UIParent` viewport metrics, bidirectional `CheckInteractDistance` + `IsItemInRange` distance calibration (`MeasureUnitDistance` updating smoothly both moving away and coming closer without uncached/nil item blacklisting), `IsInInstance()` + group-unit guards around `UnitPosition`/`C_Map`/`UnitDistanceSquared`, `IsPlateAnchorSafe` (`IsForbidden` + `IsAnchoringRestricted`) protection, zero-allocation `UpdateEntryLocation`, and an auto-stopping prune loop that consumes `0.00ms` when no targets are active or when Target Radar is disabled.
  - Replaced `SpotterHUD`'s unconditional `20 Hz` `OnUpdate` loops with lazy frame creation plus demand-driven adaptive ticking (`SyncTickerState`: completely detached when hidden or empty, `1.0s` when target is off-unit, `0.25s` when live without directional vector, and `0.066s` only while actively animating a directional compass vector), replaced the solid-black `UI-Minimap-Background` fallback circle with a shared `CreateRadarArrowBox` circular-masked class/radar emblem + stance ring, and added `spotterV2Migrated` one-time migration so previously deactivated `0.2.7` profiles re-enable cleanly while preserving explicit user toggles.
### Fixed
- **Guild Roster Multi-Return Index & Cold Guild Query Grace Window (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`):**
  - Fixed `RebuildPositiveIndex` passing `GetGuildRosterInfo` through 3-return `Safe.Call` (which dropped `gOnline` index 9 and `gGuid` index 17 as `nil`), switching to `pcall` + `Safe.Value`/`Safe.String` with a dedicated cached online-guild sub-index (`guildPosGuidSet`/`guildPosKeySet`/`guildPosFirstSet`) updated only on guild changes.
  - Bounded `posGuildRawCache` (`400`-entry cap) and added a short one-time cold-guild grace retry (`4s` solo / `8s` crowd via `coldGuildRetryUsed`) when `hasAnyPosGuilds` is enabled and `GetGuildInfo` returns `nil` on initial unit spawn, preventing missed enemy/ally guild detections while keeping confirmed strangers on `25s`/`60s` cooldown.
- **Lua Heap Growth, GC Allocation Churn & Event-Burst Optimization (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Nearby.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`):**
  - Bounded Forever GUID/guild/miss memoization caches (`foreverNameByGuid`, `foreverNameByFirst`, `unitGuildByGuid`, `foreverLookupMissAt`) in `Core/Compat.lua`, removed per-call table cloning inside internal read-only `Compat` lookups (`ParseCharInternal` / `ParseForeverInternal`), and stopped mutating cached parser tables.
  - Fixed `Nearby` eviction cleanup so `nearbyByGuid` and expired `nearbyInspectCooldown` keys never leak past `MAX_NEARBY_ENTRIES`, enforced per-GUID inspection cooldowns on passive world/chat events, removed dead `ResolveUnitFullName(nil, ...)` work in `Nearby:OnChat`, and gated non-core `UNIT_TARGET` inspection to open windows.
  - Added debounced `QueueClanEmit` coalescing for passive `SCentral1` addon/chat packets, suppressed background `CLAN_UPDATED` emissions from public trade/LFG channel chatter when SocialCentral/LFG frames are hidden, bounded `Clan.passiveChatProfs` with TTL + size caps, and eliminated `Social:GetList("all")` sorting inside `Clan:GetClanLFGList`.
  - Added allocation-free O(1)/zero-sort count helpers (`Chat:GetUnreadDirectCount`, `Store:GetAltsCount`, `Social:GetCount`, `Nearby:GetCount`, `Clan:GetOpenEventsCount`, `Clan:GetClanBadgeLFGCount`), switched `Minimap:UpdateBadge` off full `GetUnreadSummary()` table sorts and unrelated background events, cached `SetBackdrop` spec state in `UI/Theme.lua`, coalesced `UI:Refresh` bursts via `UI:QueueRefresh`, avoided duplicate `GetMergedGuildMembers` builds in `UpdateClanPanel`, and reduced the `MainFrame` `OnShow` scroll-list re-render timer from `1s` to `5s` only on live-timestamp tabs (`watchlist`/`nearby`).
- **Pass 2 O(1) Social Indexing, Companion Sort Elimination, Incremental Alt Item Index & UI Redraw Reduction (`Core/Store.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Group.lua`, `Providers/Alts.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Nearby.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`):**
  - Indexed `Store.socialByLookup` and `Store.socialByGuid` once inside `Social:Scan()`, switching `Compat.ClassFileFromGuid`, `Nearby:RecordSighting`, `Chat` lookups (`LookupClassFromCache`), `Clan:GetPlayerRelationshipFlags`, and `Clan:GetClanLFGList` from repeated full `Store.social` iterations/copies to O(1) hash lookups.
  - Eliminated the `O(N^2)` `prevByGuid` lookup and repeated companion `RecordCompanion` bursts inside `Group:Scan()` (pre-indexing previous members once and throttling unchanged groupmates to `120s` with a bounded map), throttled `Store:GetRecentCompanionsTable` TTL sweeps to `60s`, and replaced per-insert `table.sort` trims inside `Store:RecordCompanion` with direct oldest-entry eviction.
  - Added per-alt bucket caching (`Alts._altBuckets`) in `Alts:RebuildItemIndex()` so inventory updates only re-index the logged-in character while reusing untouched alt buckets, and hoisted comparator closures + skipped sorting single-owner items (`#owners <= 1`).
  - Added `Watchlist` O(1) cached lowercase/first-name fallback indexing (`EnsureWatchlistFastIndex`) invalidated only on `Add`/`Remove` to avoid full watchlist scans on tooltip/list misses.
  - Replaced per-button `_measureProbe` hidden FontStrings in `UI/Theme.lua` with a single `sharedMeasureProbe` plus `(text, font)` memoization and dirty-state early returns in `SetButtonText` / `SetButtonActive`, eliminated redundant full `RefreshTheme()` + double `scroll:Render()` + `msgFrame:Clear()` message rebuilds on unrelated updates in `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, and coalesced `LFGFrameTab` World Map buff refreshes, host-tab visibility syncs, `TryAttachAll` frame hooks, and completed `HarvestLfgListingArt` scans.
- **Pass 3 Startup Lazy UI, Hot-Path Caching, Event Filtering & Compact SavedVariables (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Providers/WorldBuffs.lua`, `Providers/Alts.lua`, `Providers/Nearby.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`):**
  - Added session and hot-path caches in `Core/Compat.lua` (`realmPoolCache`, `PlayerFaction`, `CharacterKey`, `IsMe`, `ClassHex`, and `1.0s` `CurrentZoneName` TTL) to avoid repeated `C_Map.GetMapInfo` and string/GUID work during group/nearby sweeps.
  - Optimized `Store:GetClanScopeData(forcePrune)` (cached scope string, weak-keyed `normalizedClanBuckets`, and `20s` TTL sweep throttling) and switched `Store:SyncWatchlistObservation` to early empty-table exit plus O(1) lookup via cached `_wlIdMatches` / `_wlNoSurnameByFirst` invalidated by `Watchlist:Add`/`Remove`.
  - Filtered `WorldBuffs` `UNIT_AURA` at the engine level using `RegisterUnitEvent("UNIT_AURA", "player")`, short-circuited `Alts` `ApplyAuthorSurname` once `mySurnameConfirmed` locks the player's surname, and skipped `Compat.LookupForeverFullName` in `Nearby:OnChat` when the sender already carries a space-separated surname.
  - In `Providers/Clan.lua`, queried `Store.social` directly in `Clan:GetMergedGuildMembers` without sorting/copying full lists, pre-scanned `LFG_PATTERNS` before parsing sender names in `OnExternalChatPassive`, packed `rosterSnapshot.members` into compact `"displayName\trank"` strings with backward-compatible `UnpackSnapshotMember`, and coalesced group version check timers (`Clan._groupVerPending`).
  - Coalesced background public chat emits (`QueueChatEmit(0.30)`) and bounded `Chat.seenOnlineChars` (`400` entries, `7200s` TTL) in `Providers/Chat.lua`.
  - Deferred `UI/Settings.lua` widget tree construction (`BuildPanel` on `OnShow`/`Open`) and `UI/MainFrame.lua` subpanel creation (`groupPanel`/`chatPanel`/`buffsPanel`/`altsPanel` on first `UI:Refresh` tab visit), and cached `row._scRenderedY` + visibility guards in `Theme.lua` `ScrollList:Render()`.
- **Guild & Communities (`J` / `CommunitiesFrame`) Native Tab Sync & Overlap Fix (`UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`):**
  - Fixed Blizzard's Forever `16001` `GuildPreferredPlaySettingsTab` ("Preferências de jogo") remaining checked when activating SocialCentral's side tab (`SyncAllEmbeddedTabStates`), dynamically hooked guild-only native tabs revealed after switching from a Community to a Guild (`EnsureGuildSideTab` + `OnClubSelected`), and updated `IsHostChromeOrTabChild` / `RestoreEmbeddedHostUI` so switching away from SocialCentral never re-shows stale `Chat` or `MemberList` frames overlapping `Preferências de jogo` or leaves `CommunitiesList` hidden.
- **Guild Window Layout Polish (`UI/MainFrame.lua`):**
  - Fixed empty `[ / ]` profession rendering (`Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`) caused by `""` vs `"NONE"` checks, fixed truncated `"Frost Wolf..."` guild header by dynamically releasing the right anchor when `motdEditBtn` is hidden, hid the deactivated `Target Radar` footer button when `spotterEnabled` is false so `Clan Info` ("Info da Guilda") and MOTD fit without ellipsis, and widened the top header right inset to prevent overlap with the embedded `Settings` button.
- **Reliability, SavedVariables Bounds, O(1) Social Lookups & Search/Modal UX (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `Providers/WorldBuffs.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Alts.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`):**
  - Fixed `ClanSpotter` `UnitPosition` 4th-return (`mapID`) truncation via `pcall`, filtered `WorldBuffs:OnMonsterMessage` by `Compat.PlayerFaction()` so Alliance Rend Blackhand announcements match `stormwind` (`Might of Stormwind`) instead of `warchief`, set `online = true` + `pcall`-guarded `GetGuildRosterInfo` / legacy `GetFriendInfo` in `Social.lua`, passed `rawGuid` into `Compat.IsMe` + wrapped `GetNumWhoResults`/`GetWhoInfo` in `pcall` inside `Watchlist.lua`, fixed `SocialPanels` "Pick Online Friend" payload fields (`displayName` / `sources.bnet` / `bnetName`), and fixed `Alts:RebuildItemIndex` cache freshness timestamp (`alt.lastActive or alt.updatedAt or 0`).
  - Eliminated remaining `Social:GetList` table copies/sorts and fixed `row.sources.friend` matching in `Clan.lua` (`CollectExternalLFGTargets`, `GetGuildMembersWithAllianceStatus`, `GetPeersList`, `GetProfessionsList`) plus `pcall`-guarded `GetGuildRosterInfo` in `PublishProfessionsToGuildNote`, added 30-day TTL and size caps (`80` threads / `200` peers) to persisted `chatHistory` and `addonPeers` in `Chat.lua`, added optional search clear (`x`) buttons in `Theme:CreateEditBox` (enabled on Guild Roster, Professions, Bags, and Room Invite inputs), added live search filtering in `roomInviteModal`, registered `SocialCentralModal` in `UISpecialFrames` for `Esc` closing, and gated `MainFrame` `OnShow` 1s `UpdateWhoButtons()` calls to actual state changes.
### Added
- **Multi-Source Guild Professions Directory & Account-Wide Lockouts Tracker (`Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Alts.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Added localization-agnostic profession auto-detection (`Clan:AutoDetectMyProfessions`) pulling live primary & secondary professions and exact skill ranks (`e.g. 122/150`), passive **Guild Roster Public Note** profession parsing (`Clan:ParseProfessionsFromNote`), passive `/g` official `|Htrade:` link & craft-offer capture, backward-compatible `PRF` skill-rank sync (`SCentral1`), same-pool **Alt** profession discovery, and an explicit 1-click **Publish Note** button in `OpenProfessionModal`.
  - Upgraded the `Professions` toolbar with live-count **Profession Filter** and **Source Filter** dropdowns (`All`, `Online Only`, `Guild`, `My Alts`, `SC Sync Only`), accent-insensitive search, `[SC]` / `[Note]` / `[/g]` / `[Alt]` source badges, detailed hover tooltips, and row click shortcuts (`Whisper Craft Request`, `Open in SocialCentral Chat`, `Invite`, `Watchlist`, `Copy Name`).
  - Upgraded `Alts:Snapshot` and `Locks` (`Clan:GetAllLockouts`, `Clan:RequestLockoutsRefresh`) to track active Dungeon & Raid lockouts across the logged-in character and Realm Pool Alts with boss progress, `[Raid]`/`[Alt]` badges, `Extended` state, and smart reset countdowns (`Xd Yh` / `Xh Ym`).

## [0.2.7] - 2026-11-15
### Fixed
- **Target Radar Deactivation & Main-Thread CPU Stutter Elimination (`Core/Init.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Providers/Alts.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Nearby.lua`, `Providers/WorldBuffs.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`):**
  - Disabled Target Radar (`ClanSpotter` & `ClanSpotterHUD`) runtime scanning, event registrations, and `OnUpdate` polling loops (`spotterEnabled = false`, `spotterAutoShowHUD = false`, `spotterPromptAnswered = true`, no-op `Init()`) while preserving clean stub APIs.
  - Fixed `Watchlist` tooltip & context-menu (`AppendPlayerTooltipLines`, `InjectMenu`) wrong destructuring of `Compat.ResolveUnitFullName` that triggered recurring mouseover Lua error storms, removed synchronous C++ tooltip rebuilds (`C_TooltipInfo.GetUnit` / `SocialCentralUnitScanTooltip:SetUnit`) from `Core/Compat.lua`, added bounded `parseCharCache` / `parseForeverCache` memoization, and throttled `Compat.SyncRosterForeverNames` to `15s`.
  - Decoupled `Alts:Snapshot` from guild/friend/XP events via O(1) `Alts:UpdateScalars()`, debounced `Alts:QueueSnapshot`, `UI:IsAltsVisible()` gating, and `0.05s` same-frame deduplication in `Alts:AppendAltItemTooltip`.
  - Throttled background roster and aura work across `Social` (`QueueScan` debounce, `60s` ticker, gated Watchlist observation), `Clan` (`60s` `UpdateGuildRosterHistory` cooldown, `1.5s` `CLAN_UPDATED` debounce), `Nearby` (skipping `nameplate` loops during combat or when `SocialCentralFrame` is hidden, `8.0s` ticker), `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua` (removed redundant `UNIT_AURA` hook and early-returned `RefreshWorldMapBuffs` when `WorldMapFrame` is hidden), and `WorldBuffs` (`2.0s` `UNIT_AURA` throttle emitting only on state changes or visible World Map / SocialCentral frames).
- **Target Radar Frame-Time / Stutter Elimination (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `Providers/Nearby.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`):**
  - Removed un-throttled tooltip scans (`ReadUnitTooltipTitle`, `ReadUnitTooltipGuild`) and linear full-guild/friend roster scans (`LookupForeverFullName`) from `UnitMatchesEntry` and background loops; added short-TTL negative memoization caches (`tooltipTitleMissAt`, `unitGuildMissAt`, `foreverLookupMissAt`), zero-allocation roster field unpacking (`SafeGetGuildRosterNameAndGuid`), and an optional `fastOnly` resolution path.
  - Indexed `Store.social` and `Store.nearby` GUID maps (`socialByGuid`, `socialByKey`, `nearbyByGuid`), collapsed `Nearby:RecordSighting` from 3 full social table passes down to 1, eliminated redundant `1..40` `"nameplate"`/`"nameplateXtarget"` fallback scans when `C_NamePlate.GetNamePlates()` is active, raised `ClanSpotter` background sweep interval from `0.25s` -> `0.75s` (bypassing completely when disabled) and `Nearby` ticker from `1.5s` -> `2.0s` with per-GUID bystander cooldowns.
  - Throttled `GetTargetKinematics` unit re-discovery (`0.35s`), item range telemetry (`0.25s`), and zone snapshots (`0.50s`), reused `entry._kinematics` tables without per-frame allocations, skipped `GetNamePlates()` fallback scans when `GetNamePlateForUnit(unit)` has already resolved a unit's plate state, and added UI dirty checks + macro signature caching in `ClanSpotterHUD` (`ApplyCompassBarVisuals` / `UpdateCompassVisuals` at `0.05s` cadence) so unchanged frames emit zero redundant text, color, or macro-string rebuilds.
- **Target Radar Honest Directional Bearing (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):**
  - Removed the false-positive `angle = 0` (`"Ahead"`) soft-target assumption (`softfriend`/`softenemy`/`softinteract`) which mirrored `"target"` even when the target stood behind or to the side without an active nameplate, showing a stance-colored beacon dot (`noSignalDot`) with real item-range distance (`~13m · Nearby`) and a localized nameplate/group tooltip hint instead of a misleading fixed-upward green arrow.
  - Improved `ComputeScreenAngleAndRadial` viewport-bounds checking and vertical depth transition when visible nameplates sit below character height (side/rear perspective) while prioritizing hard `target`/`focus`/`mouseover` tokens before `soft*` tokens in `FindLiveUnitForEntry`.

## [0.2.6] - 2026-11-15
### Fixed
- **Eliminated Repeated In-Game Lua Error Sounds & Blizzard Throttling Popups (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Providers/WorldBuffs.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`):**
  - Added `Safe.Bool()` in `Core/Compat.lua`, fixing repeated nil-call errors in `Watchlist` unit mouseover tooltips (`Watchlist.lua:789`, `919`, `922`).
  - Replaced unsupported Lua `os.date` / `os.time` globals with native WoW `date` / `time` functions in `Providers/WorldBuffs.lua` (`GetDarkmoonFaireStatus`, `RecordPeakHourSample`, `BuildCooldownEntry`).
  - Fixed malformed Lua frontier patterns (`%flfm%f` -> `%f[%w]lfm%f[%W]`, etc.) in `Providers/Clan.lua` chat LFG/keyword scanners (`DUNGEON_KEYWORDS`, `LFG_PATTERNS`, and role/mode classifiers).
  - Hardened `UI/Minimap.lua` `UpdateBadge()` against non-table `GetUnreadSummary()` payloads and removed addon-context `ToggleCharacter` calls in `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua` (`OpenOfficialModule("alts")`) to prevent `Blizzard_TextStatusBar` secret-number taint on `CharacterFrame`.
- **Target Radar & Directional Arrow Overhaul (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`):**
  - Removed speculative stale-anchor world dead-reckoning (`_anchorMapX`/`_anchorMapY`) and camera-corrupted `_lastWorldHeading` fallbacks so the HUD never spins backwards toward old ground anchors when tracking enemies or ungrouped guildmates off-plate.
  - Fixed visible nameplate screen bearing (`ComputeScreenAngleAndRadial`) using the underlying `plate.UnitFrame` / `plate` center with a forward-hemisphere floor so players visibly in front never invert into `"Behind"`.
  - Replaced the misleading fixed-upward `0°` arrow on `"Nearby"` targets without live directional vectors (`not kin.hasAngle`) with a clean stance-colored radar orb (`noSignalDot`) while reserving the rotating 360° `Media/arrow.tga` needle strictly for verified bearings (live `UnitPosition` / `C_Map` group vectors, visible nameplates, or soft-target reticles).
  - Hardened click-to-target secure macros (`BuildTargetMacroText` + `ApplySecureMacroAttributes`) to emit `/target [@liveUnit,exists]` only for freshly verified live units, stopped Right-click from misfiring target macros while opening the context menu, prevented WoW: Forever `"FirstName Surname"` collisions in `UnitMatchesEntry` and `IsEntryCurrentlyTargeted`, synchronized combat fallback target selection (`focusedKey` / current target), and wired `SpotterHUD:TrackEntry` for unit right-click `"Track on Radar"` menus.

## [0.2.5] - 2026-11-15
### Added
- **World Buffs Opt-In Guild Sync (`SCentral1 WB`), Separated Onyxia/Nefarian Cooldowns & Peak Drop Analytics (`Providers/WorldBuffs.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):** Switched drop timing to `GetServerTime()`, split Dragonslayer into independent **Onyxia (6h)** and **Nefarian (8h)** head-turn-in cooldowns, updated **Spirit of Zandalar (Hakkar)** to reflect zero server cooldown while still recording last observed turn-in time, tracked **Songflower Serenade (~25m Felwood respawn)** and **Darkmoon Faire calendar schedule + Sayge 4h `/played` personal cooldown**, added per-realm peak drop hour analytics (`worldBuffsHistory`), and added strictly opt-in (`clanSync == true`) guild buff sync.
- **Account-Wide Alt Item Counts & Player Social Tooltip Integration (`Providers/Alts.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`):** Added a cached multi-alt inventory index (`Alts:RebuildItemIndex`) that appends Equipped, Bags, Bank, and Account Total counts onto `GameTooltip`/`ItemRefTooltip` (`tooltipItemAlts`), plus safe player hover tooltips (`tooltipPlayerNotes`) showing Watchlist notes, Social Card notes/linked Mains, **Good Partner (`+`) / Flag: Avoid (`!`)** indicators, and recent dungeon companions.
- **Safe Unit Right-Click Context Menus & Companion Reputation Flags (`Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`):** Integrated `Menu.ModifyMenu` right-click shortcuts onto player frames, party/raid frames, guild roster, friends list, and chat names (`+ Watchlist`, `Edit Social Card`, `Good Partner (+)`, `Flag: Avoid`, `Track on Radar`), with quick Flag toggles inside the Group/Recent Companions panel, Social Card modal, and LFG rows.
- **Global Chat LFG Bulletin Filter, Chat Log Copy & Guild Roster History (`Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`):** Added passive public channel LFG/LFM bulletin capture (`globalChatLfgScan`) under the dedicated `Filter: Global Chat LFG` dropdown option inside Group Finder (`I`) (kept strictly separate from Guild/Ally/Friends sources), added a one-click **Copy** button in Conversations (`OpenCopyModal`), and added automatic **Guild Roster History** diffs (`JOINED`, `LEFT`, `RANK` changes accessible via the `History` button in Guild Roster).
- **Split 2D Items & Right-Side Character Dossier (`Providers/Alts.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):** Redesigned the Character (`C` / `Alts`) window into a clear two-column split layout with **2D Equipment, Bags & Bank items** on the left and a dedicated, scrollable **100%-opaque Character Info & Dossier column** on the right displaying Level, Race/Class, Faction, Guild, Item Level, Experience/Rested status, Zone, Hearthstone bind, accurate Equipped count (`CountEquippedSlots`), Bags, Bank, Last Seen, Character Gold, Total Server Gold, and **all primary + secondary professions** (fixing `Safe.Call` 3-return truncation of `GetProfessions()` slots 4–6 in `SnapshotProfessions` plus `GetNumSkillLines` supplementation). Moved the equipment/container filter (`[All / Equipped / Bags / Bank]` + `[?]`) immediately to the right of the `"Search items..."` box.
- **Minimalist Integrated World Map (`M`) World Buffs Pins & Mouseover Badge (`UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):** Replaced the full-screen opaque World Map (`M`) overlay with a lightweight, native-feeling integration directly over the live World Map: interactive framed spell pins at Classic/Forever drop capitals and open-world sources (`Stormwind`, `Orgrimmar`, `The Barrens`, `Stranglethorn Vale`, `Felwood`, `Feralas Dire Maul`, `Elwynn Forest`, `Mulgore`, `Silithus`, plus continent/Azeroth fallback projection) with state borders and full mouseover tooltips (Left-click to pre-fill chat, Right-click on continent/world views to zoom to the zone), plus a 100%-opaque compact top-right corner summary badge (`SocialCentralWorldMapBuffsPill`) displaying active buff count, mini spell icons, `[?]` help button, hover breakdown tooltip, and `worldBuffsMapPins` Settings toggle.
- **LFG (`I`) Category Banner Artwork Restoration (`UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`):** Fixed solid `common-button-list-large` button chrome painting over harvested `groupfinder-button-*` artwork and mis-scoring button textures during `HarvestLfgListingArt`, restoring official illustrated backgrounds on Dungeons, Raids, Quests & Zones, Battlegrounds, and Custom/Guild cards, and fixed `CreateBuffsPanel` missing `scroll.container` anchors so the `/sc hub` Buffs list renders properly.
- **Epic Character Dossier & Professions Snapshot (`Providers/Alts.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):** Replaced top-bar Level/Gold clutter in Character (`C`) with a clean character-name-only selector (`charHelpBtn`) and 100%-opaque Character Dossier showcase.
- **Epic Guild (`J`) Hub Overview, Member Search & Invite Controls (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):** Added a 2-line **Guild Hero Overview Card** inside Guild (`J`) (live online ratio, `[SC]` addon users, open events, allies/enemies, Alliance tag, shared focus/schedule & online class distribution + rich hover tooltip), Roster live search (`Search guild members...`), localized profession crafter search (`Clan:GetProfessionsList`), auto-prefill of learned primary professions inside `OpenProfessionModal`, and an explicit officer/member-permitted `+ Invite` modal (`OpenGuildInviteModal`).
- **2D Character Equipment, Bags & Bank Snapshots (`Providers/Alts.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):** Upgraded the Character (`C`) Alts side-tab into a minimalist, organized items-only inspector without 3D model rendering:
  - **Organized Equipped & Bags 2D Grid + Server Alts Selector:** Fixed the missing `Compat.Trim` runtime helper (which prevented item stats and the item grid from rendering) and the double `|cff` prefix inside the character selector; added explicit **Equipped**, **Bags**, and **Bank** section headers with live counts inside `bagGridScroll`; strengthened Forever `16001` equipment/container item visual resolution (`GetItemInfoInstant`/`GetItemInfo` fallbacks and delayed login/reload re-snapshot); scoped the character dropdown cleanly to all saved alts on the current server pool (defaulting to the active character each time `C` opens); and removed the obsolete `Set Forever Surname` and `Remove Alt` UI actions.
  - **Group Finder (`I`) Native Chrome, Borders & Large Role Icons (`UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`):** Restored `PortraitFrameTemplateNoCloseButton` (keeping `NineSlice` outer gold borders, brown stone `TitleBg`, and `"Looking for Group"` title strictly visible while elevating `LFGParentFrame` Eye Portrait and `[X]` cleanly above `560+`), enlarged the 4 role circles to `52x52` with bold `40x40` Shield/Cross/Sword icons (supporting Forever `CheckButton` role controls + tighter `UI-LFG-ICON-PORTRAITROLES` sprite crop), deepened banner scanning (`collectRegions`), and removed `SocialCentralChatButton` above official Social.
  - **Native 4-Role Circular Header Strip:** Added `Tank` (Shield), `Healer` (Cross), `DPS` (Sword), divider, and `Dungeon Guide` (Green Flag with newcomer Murloc badge) circular role toggles with auto-detected spec default and bottom-left checkboxes.
  - **5 Illustrated Category Selection Banners:** Created full-width `common-button-list-large` category cards for **Dungeons**, **Raids** (explicitly included alongside Dungeons), **Quests & Zones**, **Battlegrounds & PvP**, and **Clan, Alliance & Custom** (Clan Events, Cross-Guild Alliance, Friends & Watchlist Groups, World Buffs Coordination, Professions) matching `LFGListingFrame`.
  - **Activity Checklist, Playstyle & Broadcast Workflow:** Clicking any category banner opens the native activity checklist with Playstyle selector (`LFG`/`LFM`/`Normal`/`Fast`/`Full Clear`), `"All Level Ranges"` toggle, curated Classic & Forever dungeons (*Hall of Thanes*, *Ruins of Lordaeron*, etc.), multi-line group notes, and Broadcast target selector (`Clan & Allies`, `+ /g`, `My Clan Only`), plus `[Voltar]`, `[Remover]`, `[Listar-se]`, `[Procurar grupos]`, and `[Criar grupo]`.
  - **Native Browse Groups View:** Replicated `LFGBrowseFrame`'s stone header (`CategoryDropdown`, `FilterDropdown`, `30x30` square `UI-RefreshButton`, `[?]`), transparent cliff scroll viewport, `52px` `groupfinder-highlightbar` rows, and bottom bar (**Enviar mensagem**, **Criar grupo**, **Convidar**).
- **Full Native Integrations Across Official WoW: Forever Windows (`UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`):** Docked SocialCentral modules via native side-tabs and toolbar buttons into official Blizzard frames (`SocialUIFrame` / `FriendsFrame` (`O`) -> Watchlist, Social & Nearby; `CommunitiesFrame` / `GuildFrame` (`J`) -> Clan Hub & Diplomacy; `CharacterFrame` (`C`) -> My Alts; `ChatFrame1` -> Private Conversations & Alliance Chat with unread badge; `WorldMapFrame` (`M`) -> World Buffs) backed by combat-safe `UI:OpenEmbedded()`.
- **Settings-First Minimap, Addon Compartment & `/sc` Entry Point (`UI/Minimap.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`):** Updated Left-Click on the Minimap button, Addon Compartment click, and default `/sc` to toggle SocialCentral Settings (with quick-jump shortcuts to every official game window), reserving `/sc hub` for the standalone unified frame.
- **Cross-Clan, Friends & Watchlist Group Filtering (`Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`):** Added opt-in rate-limited `ALFG`/`ALFX` cross-guild exchange, passive Friends/Watchlist/Allied LFG chat capture, group-signup detection for Friends and Watchlist members (`hasFriendOrWatchlistSignup`), and source filter controls (`All`, `Same Clan`, `Allied Clans`, `Friends / Watchlist`).
- **Watchlist & Friends Target Radar + On-Demand Tracking (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`):** Added `WATCHLIST` and `FRIEND` priority stances to Target Radar (`spotterNotifyWatchlist`, `spotterNotifyFriends`, `spotterIgnoreGroup`), plus right-click `"Track on Radar"` (`Spotter:TrackPlayer`) in Watchlist, Social, Nearby, Clan, and Integrated LFG rows.

### Improved
- **Watchlist Automation, Full-Name `/who`, Suppress `Procurando grupo` Popups & Automated Nearby Detection (`Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Nearby.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`):** Removed manual `"Check"` row buttons and `"Check Next"` toolbar button from Watchlist, synced both online and offline character observations automatically from Guild/Friends rosters, fixed `/who` queries to search full `"FirstName Surname"` (eliminating `50+` false positives on spaced Forever names like `Zul Moletom`), prevented `LFGParentFrame` (`Procurando grupo`) / `WhoFrame` from popping open during `/who` checks, wired hardware-event auto-checks across Watchlist clicks, and expanded `Nearby` passive scanning (nameplates, soft-targets, party/raid targets, and 1.5s ambient ticker) so friendly players appear automatically without clicking or hovering.
- **Native Fidelity Refinements Across Character (`C`), Guild (`J`), LFG (`I`) & Global `"Guild"` Terminology (`UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):** Fixed Character (`C`) `Alts` responsive header/search layout (`RelayoutAltsPanel`), wrapped the item grid inside an inset card, and defaulted selection to the active character; elevated embedded `CommunitiesFrame` (`J`) portrait overlays above interior frames, cleared the top-left lute portrait, hid native interior footer controls, and disabled guild-dependent actions when the player is not in a guild; unclipped `LFGParentFrame` (`I`) eye portrait artwork, aligned the 4 role circles (`Tank`, `Healer`, `DPS`, divider, `Guide`) and activity font sizes (`GameFontNormal`) 1:1 with `LFGListingFrame`, and replaced all user-facing `"Clan"`/`"Clã"` references with `"Guild"`/`"Guilda"`/`"Hermandad"` across all 3 languages.
- **Minimum-Button Interface Consolidation (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):** Streamlined every integrated window and sub-panel to minimize visible button clutter while keeping 100% of existing features one click away inside dropdown/row context menus:
  - **Character (`C`):** Removed 3D model controls and sub-tab bars in favor of 2 top dropdowns (`[Character v]`, `[Filter v]`), search bar, and unified 2D item grid.
  - **Watchlist / Social / Nearby (`O`):** Consolidated Social's 5 source/folder buttons into 1 filter dropdown (`socialFilterBtn`), consolidated Watchlist's 4 input buttons into 2 smart buttons (`+ Add` targeting typed text or current target, and `Check Next` running `/who`), and reduced per-row action buttons from 3 (`Checar` / `Sussurrar` / `Convidar`) down to 1 primary button with full row-click context menus.
  - **Conversations, Group & World Buffs (`O` / `M`):** Consolidated Chat's 9 header buttons into 2 dropdowns (`[Filter: %s v]` and `[+ New v]`), Buffs' 3 view buttons into 1 view selector + `Post in Chat`, and Group's header/row buttons into `[View v]` + 1 contextual action per row.
  - **Clan (`J`):** Simplified sidebar to 4 views + `+ Clans & Allies LFG`, moved `Clan Sync` / `Alliance` into dropdown menus, reduced Events (6 -> 2), Diplomacy (3 -> 2), and Professions/Locks (3 -> 2) toolbars to dropdown + primary action, removed per-row `"W"`/`"I"` buttons in favor of row-click context menus, and trimmed the bottom bar to `Clan Info` and `Target Radar`.
- **`SocialUIFrame` (`O`) Clean Close & Reparent Fix (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`):** Fixed closing or pressing `O` after viewing SocialCentral's 5th side-tab leaving a standalone `SocialCentral` window visible behind `SocialUIFrame` by ensuring `frame:HookScript("OnHide")` calls `frame:Hide()` before deferring safe re-parenting to `UIParent`, making `UI:CloseEmbeddedIfHost()` idempotent when parent hides fire `OnHide` first, and tightening right-side tab discovery to `pRight - 14`.
- **WoW: Forever Native `Guilda e comunidades` (`J`) Layout & Embedded Top Header Chrome Cleanup (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):** Redesigned `CreateClanPanel` into Forever's native sidebar navigation (`Roster`, `Events & Calendar`, `Diplomacy`, `Professions & Locks`, `Clan LFG Posts`, plus bottom `+ Clans & Allies LFG` button), a clean top status/sync strip, contextual right toolbar, and bottom MOTD/utility bar (`Sync`, `Clan Info`, `Alliance`, `Target Radar`, `WoW Calendar`); eliminated top native header bleeding on `CommunitiesFrame` (`J`) and `CharacterFrame` (`C`) via `topPad = 21`, `IsSingleTabEmbedded()` single-tab strip suppression, and automatic hiding/restoration of native `CharacterFrame` paperdoll subframes.
- **`SocialUIFrame` (`O`) 5th Side-Tab Activation & Frame-Level Layering Fix (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`):** Fixed clicking SocialCentral's 5th right side-tab on WoW: Forever's Friends/Social window (`O`) doing nothing visually: parented embedded `SocialCentralFrame` directly to `hostFrame` (`SocialUIFrame` / `CommunitiesFrame` / `CharacterFrame`) at `FrameLevel 520+` above `Inset`/`ScrollBox` containers while elevating host outer chrome (`[X]`, portrait, title, `NineSlice`, and right side-tabs) cleanly above `560+`, prevented `Theme:RestoreWindowPosition()` on `OnShow` from clearing embedded anchors while `_scLocked == true`, and restricted `DiscoverRightSideTabs` named keys to right-edge tabs so bottom horizontal tabs cannot corrupt side-tab sync or positioning.
- **True In-Window Interior Embedding & Conversations/History inside `SocialUIFrame` (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/LFGFrameTab.lua`):** Embedded `SocialCentralFrame` strictly inside the interior client bounds of official WoW: Forever frames (`SocialUIFrame`, `CommunitiesFrame`/`GuildFrame`, `CharacterFrame`, `LFGParentFrame`, `WorldMapFrame`) keeping native title bars, portraits, `NineSlice` borders, `[X]` close buttons, and side-tabs clickable without spawning a second floating window beside them; consolidated `"chat"` (Conversations & History) into `SocialUIFrame`'s `{ "watchlist", "social", "nearby", "chat" }` tab set (`SocialCentralChatButton` opens `SocialUIFrame` directly on `"chat"`), added responsive vertical stacking + multi-row compact toolbar wrapping `< 540px`, synced native side-tab checked states while embedded, and guarded `LFGFrameTab:ShowSubPanel()` against `OpenEmbedded` collisions.
- **WoW: Forever (`16001`) Native Visual Standard (`UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`):** Replaced Classic marble/desaturated-pewter styling on `"blizzard"` theme with Forever native `UIPanelButtonTemplate` red/gold buttons, `common-button-list-large` row cards with bronze-gold selection highlights, and `groupfinder-Stat-StoneBG` / `groupfinder-background` / `common-insideframe` backing surfaces while preserving strict 100% opaque `_solidOpaqueBg` (`alpha = 1.0`).
- **Target Radar Declutter & Dismiss Reliability (`Core/Init.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`):** Fixed the Dismiss `[X]` button so dismissed players stay muted for 180s instead of reappearing on the next nameplate tick; excluded current Party/Raid members by default (`spotterIgnoreGroup = true`); defaulted noisy Same-Clan alerts, Nameplate Badges, and unsolicited first-time prompts to `false`/`true` respectively (with one-time `spotterDeclutterMigrated` transition for existing profiles); suppressed redundant chat alerts when the HUD is visible for friendly stances; lengthened alert cooldowns (`120s`/`180s`); and compacted the floating radar pill to `248x48` without nested double borders.

## [0.2.4] - 2026-11-14
### Added
- **Classic World Buffs Tracker & Inform Module (`Providers/WorldBuffs.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`, `SocialCentral.toc`):** Added a dedicated Classic World Buffs tracking, notification, and reporting system tailored for World of Warcraft: Forever (`16001` / `wowf`):
  - **Live Player Buff Detection:** Automatically detects active classic world auras on the character via `C_UnitAuras.GetPlayerAuraBySpellID`, tracking real-time expiration, formatted time remaining (`1h 45m`, `23m`), spell icons, and applications without using prohibited legacy APIs.
  - **Complete Classic & Forever Buff Directory:** Curated and documented all functioning classic world buffs with their exact status on WoW Forever (active in Open World & 5-man Dungeons; suppressed with explicit *"No effect while in a raid instance"* note):
    - *Rallying Cry of the Dragonslayer* (Onyxia & Nefarian head turn-in, +5% spell crit, +10% melee/ranged crit, +140 AP, 2h).
    - *Warchief's Blessing* (Horde - Rend Blackhand head turn-in, +300 HP, 15% melee/ranged/spell haste, 10 MP5, persists through death).
    - *Might of Stormwind* (Alliance - Rend Blackhand head turn-in, WoW Forever exclusive counterpart to Warchief's Blessing, +300 HP, 15% melee/ranged/spell haste, 10 MP5, persists through death).
    - *Spirit of Zandalar* (Heart of Hakkar turn-in, +15% all attributes, +10% movement speed, 2h).
    - *Songflower Serenade* (Cleansed Felwood Songflower, +15 all stats, +5% crit, 1h).
    - *Dire Maul North Tribute Buffs* (Slip'kik's Savvy +3% spell crit, Fengus' Ferocity +200 AP, Mol'dar's Moxie +15% stamina, 2h).
    - *Darkmoon Faire (Sayge's Fortune)* (+10% damage, +10% armor, +25 resists, or +10% stats, 2h).
    - *Traces of Silithyst* (Silithus geysers, +5% damage, 30m).
  - **Real-Time Capital Drop Announcements & Countdown Alerts:** Passively monitors `CHAT_MSG_MONSTER_YELL` and `CHAT_MSG_MONSTER_EMOTE` in capital cities and drop hubs for Onyxia, Nefarian, Rend Blackhand (Orgrimmar & Stormwind / Goldshire / Lakeshire / Sentinel Hill), and Hakkar. Displays a live drop countdown banner in the UI, prints local chat alerts (~6-15 seconds before the buff lands), plays an audio chime, and records drop timestamps in `SocialCentralDB.worldBuffsDrops`.
  - **1-Click Pre-Filled Chat Reporting ("Informar no Chat"):** Added Blizzard-policy-compliant reporting via `ChatFrameUtil.OpenChat` / `ChatFrame_OpenChat` that drafts an organized summary of active world buffs (or individual buffs) targeting Raid (`/ra`), Party (`/p`), Guild (`/g`), or Say (`/s`) without automated chat spam.
  - **World Buff Drop Predictive Cooldown Countdown Engine (`Providers/WorldBuffs.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):** Added a predictive cooldown status calculation for capital NPC drops (Dragonslayer 2h, Rend Blackhand / Might of Stormwind 3h, Songflower 25m). Displays real-time cooldown countdowns (e.g. `[Recarga: ~1h 24m restantes (Pronto às 19:42)]`) when on cooldown, switching to `[PRONTO / DISPONÍVEL: Pode cair a qualquer momento!]` once the timer elapses.
  - **Dedicated Guild and Group/Raid Chat Filters (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):** Separated the Chat panel's unified stream into dedicated filter buttons: `[Guild]` (specifically for `/g` Guild and `/o` Officer channels) and `[Group]` (specifically for `/p` Party, `/ra` Raid, and `/i` Instance channels), with auto-population of channel threads upon addon initialization.
  - **Cross-Guild Alliance Hub & Governance System (`Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):**
    - **Private Alliance Chat (`ch:alliance`):** Added an encrypted-free in-addon chat thread for allied guilds with direct in-addon message composer and guild-prefixed message badges (`<GuildName> CharacterName: Message`).
    - **Strict Guild Leader Governance:** The `[Alliance]` configuration button in Clan Management is visible strictly to the Guild Leader (`IsGuildLeader()`) and appointed Alliance Managers, eliminating UI clutter for standard members.
    - **Manual Member Whitelist Modal (`SocialCentralAllianceModal`):** 100% opaque modal allowing Leaders to toggle access modes (`ALL` vs `WHITELIST`), edit Alliance Name and shared secret Token, and grant or revoke individual member access and managerial rights with 1 click.
  - **Real-Time Scout & Bounty Radar Alert (`UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`):** Added a 1-click `[Scout Alert]` button to the minimal Target Radar Interaction Drawer when an enemy is spotted. Immediately broadcasts character name, foreign guild, level, class, zone, and map coordinates to guild and chat channels with audio alert.
  - **Main Frame Buffs Tab & Slash Commands:** Added a responsive `"Buffs"` navigation tab in `UI/MainFrame.lua` with filter views (`All Buffs`, `My Buffs`, `Drop History`), wired into `ReflowTabStrip` and `/sc buffs` / `/sc buff [report]`.
  - **Configurable Settings with '?' Help Buttons:** Added World Buff Drop Alerts and Audio Chime toggles with standard `[?]` help buttons (`HelpBtn`) and an `[Open World Buffs Tracker]` shortcut in `UI/Settings.lua`.
  - **100% Trilingual Parity (`Locales/Locale.lua`):** Complete translations and exact placeholder matching (`%s`, `%d`) across English, Portuguese (`ptBR`/`ptPT`), and Spanish (`esES`/`esMX`).

### Fixed
- **CurseForge Archive Compliance & Executable Blacklist Safeguards (`.pkgmeta`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`, `AGENTS.md`):** Excluded `Instalar-SocialCentral.bat`, `update-socialcentral.ps1`, and all `*.bat`, `*.ps1`, `*.cmd`, `*.sh`, `*.exe` scripts from CurseForge zip archives in `.pkgmeta`, preventing CurseForge rejection (`Archive contains blacklisted file extensions`), with automated pre-commit and pre-deploy compliance validation.
- **Clan Member Spotter Detection, On-Screen Alert & Live Location/Direction Radar (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Alts.lua`):**
  - **Fixed Event Callback Argument Offset (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`):** Fixed `NAME_PLATE_UNIT_ADDED`, `NAME_PLATE_UNIT_REMOVED`, `UNIT_NAME_UPDATE`, and `PLAYER_GUILD_UPDATE` handlers to unpack `function(_, _, unit)` matching `Init.lua`'s `pcall(fn, owner, event, ...)` dispatch signature, restoring real-time nameplate and unit-name detection.
  - **Fixed Lexical Scope Forward-Declaration Bug on `NormalizeAngleRad` (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`):** Moved `local function NormalizeAngleRad(a)` above `Spotter:InspectUnit` so hovering over a unit without a nameplate (`unit == "mouseover"`) no longer throws a nil-call error before `Spotter:MaybeTriggerAlert` and `SPOTTER_UPDATED` execute.
  - **Fixed Scope Hydration Wiping Active Targets (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`):** Updated `Spotter:OnGuildChanged()` so initial scope hydration (`"" -> scopeToken`) and `CLAN_UPDATED` events reclassify and preserve active targets instead of wiping `Spotter.entries`.
  - **Multi-Source Guild & Same-Clan Member Resolution (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `Providers/Alts.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`):** Added `Compat.ReadUnitTooltipGuild(unit, guid)` to extract `<Guild Name>` from unit tooltips (`C_TooltipInfo` / `SocialCentralUnitScanTooltip`) when `GetGuildInfo(unit)` returns `nil` on WoW Forever (`16001`). Eliminated false-positive same-clan matches when non-guild players share a first name (`FirstName`) with a guild member: indexed `Compat.CacheGuildMember` strictly by canonical `lookupKey` (`firstname-surname`) and `GUID`, rejected non-matching `GUID`s in `Compat.IsGuildMember`, `Compat.IsMe`, `UnitMatchesEntry`, and `IsEntryCurrentlyTargeted`, guarded `foreverNameByFirst` against multi-character surname collisions, prevented `Spotter:InspectUnit` and `Spotter:ReclassifyActiveEntries` from overwriting a known foreign guild with the player's own guild, and automatically purged stale `Spotter.entries` when a unit's guild resolves as neutral.
  - **Expanded Unit, Visible Group & Proximity Chat Scanning (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`):** Extended `RefreshVisibleNameplates()` to scan `nameplate1`..`nameplate40`, `targettarget`, `focustarget`, `mouseovertarget`, and visible group members (`party1..4`, `raid1..40` where `UnitIsVisible` is true), plus `UNIT_FACTION`, `UNIT_TARGET`, and local proximity chat (`CHAT_MSG_SAY`, `CHAT_MSG_YELL`, `CHAT_MSG_EMOTE`, `CHAT_MSG_TEXT_EMOTE`) so clanmates are detected even when friendly nameplates (`Shift+V`) are disabled.
  - **Real-Time Location, Map Coordinates, Distance & Directional Bearing ("Onde ele está") (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`):** Captured zone, subzone, and map coordinates `(X, Y)` via `C_Map.GetBestMapForUnit` / `C_Map.GetPlayerMapPosition` on every sighting. Guarded `UnitPosition` with `Safe.Call` / `Safe.Number`, validated `UnitMatchesEntry` before trusting cached `unitToken`, added map-coordinate bearing vectors for party/raid members, and displayed full location + distance + directional bearing (`Ahead`, `To your left`, `To your right`, `Behind`) across chat alerts, center-screen `RaidWarningFrame` / `UIErrorsFrame` notifications, the Target Radar alert banner, the Compass card, and target slot rows.
  - **Minimalist Arrow Pill Redesign, Hardware-Click Target Selection & On-Click Interaction Drawer (`UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`):**
    - Replaced the bulky multi-section Target Radar window (header bar, alert banner, filter bar, and duplicate target slot list) and suppressed overlapping `RaidWarningFrame` text when the HUD is active in favor of a sleek **Minimalist Arrow Pill (`304 x 66`)** showing only the rotating directional arrow (`Media/arrow.tga`), player name + stance badge, live distance + direction, and a compact `[X]` dismiss button (plus a `[1/N]` cycle button only when >1 target is nearby).
    - Fixed click-to-target in WoW Forever (`16001`) by configuring `SecureActionButtonTemplate` with `RegisterForClicks("AnyUp")` and `SetAttribute("useOnKeyDown", false)`, passing `arrowBox:EnableMouse(false)` so clicks directly on the arrow reach the secure button, and dynamically building the target macro with live unit tokens (`/target [@nameplateX,exists]`), raw `UnitName(unit)`, and all Forever surname variants (`/targetexact`).
    - Added an expandable **On-Click Interaction Drawer (`actionPanel`)** right below the Arrow Pill that opens when the user clicks the arrow (right after selecting the target on screen), presenting direct 1-click interaction buttons (`[Whisper]`, `[/w]`, `[Invite]` / `[Diplomacy]`, `[+ Watchlist]`).
    - Fixed the Lua multiple-assignment `GetCounts()` precedence bug, guarded `SetClipsChildren` calls, added a combat-safe floating arrow fallback (`SocialCentralSpotterCombatArrow`), and upgraded 2D screen-space direction (`ComputeScreenAngleAndRadial`) and meter distance estimation (`~12m`–`~40m`).
    - **16001-Compliant Proximity Detection & Forever-Calibrated Kinematics (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/Nearby.lua`):** Replaced `COMBAT_LOG_EVENT_UNFILTERED`, protected nameplate CVar tampering (`SetCVar`), and `GameTooltip:SetHyperlink` (which trigger `ADDON_ACTION_FORBIDDEN` in Forever `16001`) with Blizzard-safe multi-source unit token detection (`nameplate1..40`, `nameplate1target..40target`, `softfriend`, `softenemy`, `softinteract`, group units, and local chat) plus out-of-combat `C_TooltipInfo.GetHyperlink` guild resolution. Added Forever-calibrated `C_Item.IsItemInRange` distance estimation (`< 8m` to `> 48m`, guarded for friendly targets in combat), off-screen/no-nameplate world-coordinate dead-reckoning anchored only on direct position reads, non-interrupting `SPOTTER_UPDATED` smoothing, and real-time HUD/drawer updates without requiring `mouseover`.
    - **Dedicated Guild & Group Chat Sub-Tabs (`UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`):** Separated the Chat panel top filter into distinct `[Guild]` (Guild & Officer channels) and `[Group]` (Party, Raid & Instance channels) sub-tabs alongside `[Whispers]`, `[Rooms]`, and `[Channels]`. Enabled native channel mirroring (`chatMirrorChannels = true`) by default and pre-initialized channel threads so guild and party conversations appear with full class coloring, unread counters, and one-click `/g` and `/p` pre-fill without configuration.
    - **World Buffs Cooldown & Next-Drop Discovery Engine (`Providers/WorldBuffs.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):** Integrated internal server cooldown tracking (`7200s` for Dragonslayer/DM, `10800s` for Warchief/Stormwind, `1500s` for Songflower) and added `GetDropCooldownStatus` to calculate and display real-time cooldown countdowns (`[Cooldown: ~Xh Ym left (Reset at HH:MM)]`) and instant `[RESET / READY: Can drop at any time!]` availability statuses in the World Buffs panel for every tracked world buff.
- **UX/UI Layout Streamline & Contextual Toolbar Reorganization (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):**
  - **Main Window Header Declutter:** Hidden the redundant inline theme-cycling button (`themeBtn`) and idle `[Update]` button from the top header bar (keeping `[Update vX.Y.Z]` auto-visible whenever a newer peer version is detected), leaving only `[?]`, `[Settings]`, and `[X]` in the top-right header.
  - **Logical Top Tab Order:** Grouped top navigation tabs by player workflow: `Watchlist`, `Social`, `Chat`, `Clan`, `Group`, `Nearby`, `Buffs`, `Alts`.
  - **Contextual Clan Hub Toolbars:** Reduced the top Clan toolbar (`controlsBar`) from 9 multi-row buttons to 5 global controls (`Clan Sync`, `My Status`, `Target Radar`, `Clan Info`, `Sync`) on a single clean row. Moved contextual action buttons directly into their respective sub-panels: `[+ Event Call]` into the `Events & Calendar` card header, `[+ Clan LFG]` and `[LFG Alerts]` into the `Clan LFG` sub-toolbar (`lfgToolBar`), and `[+ Prof]` into a dedicated `Professions` sub-toolbar (`profToolBar`), with `FlowButtonGroup` responsive layout and preserved `[?]` help buttons.
  - **Cleaner List Rows (`Watchlist` & `Social`):** Removed the inline red `[X]` delete button from `Watchlist` rows (preventing accidental deletions next to `Invite`; `Remove` remains in the right-click menu) and removed the redundant inline `[+ Watchlist]` button from `Social` rows (keeping `[Whisper]` and `[Invite]` inline and `Add to Watchlist` in the right-click menu, while retaining inline `[+ Watchlist]` on `Nearby` rows).
  - **Single-Row Chat Toolbar:** Removed redundant global `Clear` and `Mark Read` buttons from the top Chat bar (already available in the conversation right-click menu) so the Chat top bar remains on a clean single 34px row.

## [0.2.3] - 2026-11-13
### Added
- **1-Click Antivirus-Clean Windows Installer (`Instalar-SocialCentral.bat`, `scripts/Instalar-SocialCentral.bat`):** Added a native double-clickable Windows `.bat` hybrid installer that runs with 1 click without triggering antivirus heuristics or SmartScreen blocks. Automatically discovers World of Warcraft Forever across all local drives and directories, handles administrative permissions gracefully via standard UAC elevation when necessary, supports both instant local deployment and automated GitHub release downloads, and cleanly installs or updates `SocialCentral` into `_forever_\Interface\AddOns`.
- **Public Release Asset Integration (`scripts/publish-public-release.ps1`):** Integrated `Instalar-SocialCentral.bat` into public GitHub release workflows, bundling the batch installer directly into `SocialCentral-Releases` root and uploading it as a standalone downloadable release asset.
- **Alts Visual Streamline & Clean Layout (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):** Removed redundant `[Set Surname]` buttons from the Alts top header bar (`altsBar`) and character rows, freeing the full row width for current character information (zone, guild, item level, rested XP, gold) and displaying only a subtle red `[X]` prune button on non-current alts. Preserved manual surname editing and alt removal via right-click context menu (`OpenRowMenu`). Updated Alts help text across `enUS`, `ptBR`, and `esES` with 100% trilingual parity.
- **WoW Forever Universal Group Compatibility Layer (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/Group.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Nearby.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`):** Added `Compat.IsInGroup()`, `Compat.IsInRaid()`, `Compat.GetNumGroupMembers()`, `Compat.IsGroupLeader()`, `Compat.IsGroupAssistant()`, and `Compat.LeaveGroup()` with seamless fallback to classic 1.12 group APIs (`GetNumPartyMembers`, `GetNumRaidMembers`, `IsPartyLeader`, `IsRaidLeader`, `LeaveParty`). Registered `"PARTY_MEMBERS_CHANGED"` and `"RAID_ROSTER_UPDATE"` across Group, Clan, and Nearby modules to ensure instant event responsiveness on WoW Forever (`_forever_`, 16001).
- **Roster, Friends & Watchlist Engine Fallbacks (`Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`):** Added 1.12 online status checks `(online == 1 or online == true)` and localized class fallback in `ScanGuild()`. Added classic `GetNumFriends()` and `GetFriendInfo(i)` scanner in `ScanCharacterFriends()`. In `Watchlist:AddTarget()`, targeting an already watched player now refreshes their live observation (zone, guild, class, level, online status) instead of erroring with duplicate. Added classic `GetNumWhoResults()` and `GetWhoInfo(i)` fallback in `OnWhoListUpdate()` and global `AddFriend` fallback in `PinToFriends()`.
- **Addon Message Prefix Registration & Send Fallbacks (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`):** Registered addon message prefix `"SCentral1"` in `Chat:Init()` with fallback between `C_ChatInfo.RegisterAddonMessagePrefix` and global `RegisterAddonMessagePrefix`. Supported global `SendAddonMessage` fallbacks across Chat and Clan modules. Added classic sound string fallbacks (`"RaidWarning"`, `"ReadyCheck"`, `"TellMessage"`) in `ClanSpotter.lua` when `SOUNDKIT` is unavailable.
- **Clan Tab Dynamic Target Radar Indicator, Live Count Badge & Hover Tooltips (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):** Wired `clanPanel.radarBtn` in `UpdateClanPanel()` to dynamically display live spotted target counts (`Target Radar (N)`) with stance-responsive styling (`danger` red if enemies spotted, `primary` accent if allies/clanmates), resetting to `default` when clear. Added hover tooltip showing detailed stance tallies (`Enemy: X  Ally: Y  Clan: Z`) and toggle shortcuts. Added dynamic count badge to the Clan tab strip (`Clan (N)`) tracking open clan events and active LFG calls.
- **Clan Event Row Role Toggle, Status Accents & Officer-Scoped Controls (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):** Enhanced event call rows (`T` / `H` / `D`) so clicking an already signed-up role toggles it off (`DECLINE`), with the active role highlighted in `primary` theme accent. Added individual hover tooltips (`Tank`, `Healer`, `DPS`, `Cancel / Remove Event`) detailing sign-up and cancellation actions with full trilingual parity. Dynamically hidden the remove (`X`) button for non-creators and non-officers to prevent accidental clicks while expanding title width. Added one-click `Whisper <Applicant>` option to the right-click event applicant context menu alongside `Invite`.
- **Social & Nearby Roster Watchlist Awareness & Instant Toggle (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):** Added `Watchlist:IsWatched(nameOrKey)` and `Watchlist:GetEntry(nameOrKey)` supporting lookups by GUID, lookupKey, or character name. In Social and Nearby roster lists, characters already on the Watchlist display an active disabled `[Watched]` (`[Observado]` / `[En Seguimiento]`) badge instead of `+ Watchlist`. In row right-click context menus, existing entries offer `Remove from Watchlist` while unmonitored players offer `Add to Watchlist`. Added hover tooltips to peer row buttons (`Invite`, `Whisper`).
- **Account Alts Item Level Tracking, Direct Row Action Controls & Realm Gold Scoping (`Providers/Alts.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`):** Added average equipped item level (`itemLevel`) tracking to character snapshots via `GetAverageItemLevel` and `PLAYER_AVG_ITEM_LEVEL_UPDATE`, displaying `iLvl N` across alt status subtext. Added direct row actions to the Alts tab with dedicated `[Set Surname]` buttons on all rows and red `[X]` removal buttons on non-current alts. Scoped `Store:GetTotalAltsGold(true)` to the active realm pool for accurate shard-specific balance totals.
- **Group Tab Member Actions, Self-Leave & Dynamic Header States (`Providers/Group.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):** Added safe `Group:LeaveGroup()` and wired an intuitive danger-styled `[Leave]` (`[Sair]` / `[Salir]`) button alongside a right-click context menu item (`L["Leave Group"]` / `Sair do Grupo` / `Salir del Grupo`) directly on the player's own row in the active group view. Added group leader promotion to the member right-click menu, fixed previous scan state preservation in `Group:Scan()` (`self.members`), added clean single-name upgrades in `Store:RecordCompanion()`, and updated Group header labels dynamically when switching to Recent Companions.
- **Whisper Thread GUID Class & Surname Resolution (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):** Captured sender `guid` from whisper events, upgrading `LookupClassFromCache(lookupKey, guid)` to resolve class tokens via `Compat.ClassFileFromGuid(guid)` and expand session roleplay surnames via `Compat.CacheForeverFullName`. Upgraded `+ Target` in the New 1-on-1 Chat dialog to resolve the target's full Forever name via `Compat.ResolveUnitFullName("target")`.
- **Nearby Tab Guild Context, Sightings Clear & Roleplay Emote Detection (`Providers/Nearby.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):** Enhanced passive sighting inspection to record players' guild names via `GetGuildInfo(unit)`, displaying `<Guild Name>` in `row.subText` and hover tooltips on the Nearby tab. Added `Compat.ClassFileFromGuid(guid)` in `Core/Compat.lua` with fallback class, guild, and level resolution across `RecordSighting`, and implemented clean single-name to surname upgrades. Added `Nearby:Clear()` and an interactive `[Clear]` (`[Limpar]` / `[Limpiar]`) button on `nearbyBar` with full theme updates and trilingual tooltip parity (`L["Clears all passive nearby sightings."]`). Added `PLAYER_FOCUS_CHANGED` inspection and registered roleplay emote (`CHAT_MSG_EMOTE`, `CHAT_MSG_TEXT_EMOTE`) and officer chat (`CHAT_MSG_OFFICER`) sightings.
- **Watchlist Live Targeting & Zone Capture (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Providers/Watchlist.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`):** Enabled out-of-combat `Target Player` on right-click context menus for online Watchlist entries via `Compat.GetForeverTargetVariants()`.
