Added current zone capture on `Watchlist:AddTarget()`, protected `Store:SyncWatchlistObservation()` against empty guild overwrites, and synchronized dictionary keys in `Store:GetWatchlistTable()` when `/who` results upgrade an entry with a surname.
- **Target Radar Secure Macro Parity (`UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`):** Enhanced `BuildTargetMacroText(entry)` in Target Radar to generate `/targetexact` macro lines for all candidate variations from `Compat.GetForeverTargetVariants()`, aligning in-combat secure macros with out-of-combat target acquisition.
- **Minimap Crimson Enemy Alert Ring & Alt+Click Target Radar Shortcut (`UI/Minimap.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):** Configured the Minimap icon overlay ring to glow crimson red (`0.96, 0.28, 0.28`) when enemy clan members are spotted nearby on Target Radar (yellow when unread messages are waiting, and clean white when clear). Added `Alt+Click` shortcut on the minimap button to instantly toggle the Target Radar HUD, and updated the tooltip description across English, Portuguese (`ptBR`), and Spanish (`esES`).
- **Live Target Resolution & Surname Upgrades on Watchlist (`Providers/Watchlist.lua`):** Upgraded `Watchlist:AddTarget()` to resolve the live target's full roleplay surname via `Compat.ResolveUnitFullName`, immediately capturing their class, level, guild, and setting their initial status to `online`. Enhanced `Watchlist:ApplyWhoResults()` to automatically upgrade single-name entries with their newly discovered surname upon receiving `/who` responses, expanding the session surname cache.
- **Interactive Slash Command Help Guide (`Core/Init.lua`):** Added `/sc help` and `/sc ?` handler that prints an organized, localized command cheat-sheet to chat with zero new translation keys (reusing existing verified strings).
- **Settings '?' Help Coverage & Expanded Scroll View (`UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):** Standardized help buttons for `[Open Target Radar]`, `[Reset All Window Positions]`, and `[Reset Radar Prompt]` using the `HelpBtn` helper with complete `howText` explanations across English, Portuguese (`ptBR`), and Spanish (`esES`), and expanded scroll child height (`2180px`) so bottom controls never get clipped on any display resolution or localized font metrics.
- **Universal Modal & Popup Top-Right Close Buttons (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`):** Added explicit `Theme:CreateButton(m, 22, 20, "X", "default")` close buttons (`topCloseBtn`) anchored at `TOPRIGHT, -10, -10` to all modal dialogs and system popups across SocialCentral (`EventModal`, `ClanLFGModal`, `ClanAlertModal`, `ClanInfoModal`, `DiplomacyModal`, `SocialMetaModal`, `ProfessionModal`, `CreateRoomModal`, `TempChannelModal`, `RoomInviteModal`, `NewDirectChatModal`, `SocialCentralHelpPopup`, `SocialCentralUpdatePopup`, `SocialCentralReloadPopup`), ensuring every dialog across the entire addon provides an intuitive, visible top-right `X` button with theme styling and input focus clearing alongside ESC and bottom action buttons.
- **Unified `Compat.GetForeverTargetVariants` & Multi-Target Acquisition (`Core/Compat.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):** Implemented `Compat.GetForeverTargetVariants(raw)` returning ordered target candidates (`firstName`, `firstName surname`, `firstName-surname`). Updated `SpotterHUD.HandleTargetClick`, `Spotter:FocusTarget`, and all right-click context menus (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`) to acquire targets via `TargetByName(cand, true)` through this unified helper, ensuring instant character acquisition for characters with or without WoW Forever surnames.
- **Dynamic Combat Target Tracking & Nameplate Kinematics Scaling (`UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`):** Upgraded `SpotterHUD:UpdateTargetVisuals()` to detect active player targets from `ns.Spotter.entries` even if the target is outside the current 6 visible slot rows (or hidden by active stance filters), re-locking the Compass Hero Card onto the targeted player immediately during combat lockdown without touching protected secure attributes. When targets are dropped, it falls back gracefully to the highest priority spotted target. Fixed UI scale factor multiplication (`* uScale`) in nameplate horizontal pixel projection calculations within `Spotter:GetTargetKinematics`, guaranteeing accurate bearing angles across all custom UI scales.

### Fixed
- **Mandatory 100% Opacity Across Windows, Dialogs & Popups (`UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):** Removed transparency completely across all addon windows, modals, and popups. In `Theme:ApplyBackdrop(frame, "window")`, created and enforced an explicit solid 100% opaque background quad (`_solidOpaqueBg`) at `BACKGROUND, -8` with `WHITE8X8` and vertex color alpha strictly `1.0`, backed by solid dialog backdrop colors (`0.10, 0.09, 0.08, 1.0` in Blizzard theme, `0.06, 0.07, 0.09, 1.0` in Dark theme, `0.11, 0.10, 0.09, 1.0` in Native theme). Updated all palette background, border, surface, and inset alpha definitions from `0.90`-`0.96` to `1.0`, ensuring underlying main window and game world text never bleeds through popups like Create Clan Event Call, Clan LFG, Clan Alert, Clan Info, Diplomacy, Professions, and chat modals.
- **Complete Removal of Numbers from Minimap Icon (`UI/Minimap.lua`):** Completely removed online count badge (`button.badge`) and unread direct message badge (`button.msgBadgeFrame` / `button.msgBadgeText`) from the minimap button as requested. Kept the clean border ring alert glow (yellow for unread, crimson red for spotted enemies) and set static LDB text, ensuring the minimap icon displays cleanly with zero overlapping numbers.
- **Top-Right Main Window Blizzard-Authentic Close Button (`UI/MainFrame.lua`):** Restored Blizzard's native `ButtonFrameTemplate.CloseButton` artwork in Blizzard Official theme (matching the Looking for Group window 1:1) and native `UIPanelCloseButton` in Dark/Native themes without overwriting client texture atlases. Dynamically elevated strictly above `ButtonFrameTemplate.NineSlice` (`FrameLevel 500+`) via `MaxHierarchyFrameLevel` and `OnShow` / next-tick frame-level synchronization so it renders crisp, opaque, and clickable across all themes.
- **Alt Auto-Discovery Chat Event Parameter Alignment (`Providers/Alts.lua`):** Fixed argument offset bug where `CHAT_MSG_*` event handlers passed `author` and `guid` out-of-order due to `ns.RegisterEvent` prefixes. Using `select(10, ...)` for `senderGUID` and arg 4 for `author` restores passive automatic surname discovery, caching newly spotted surnames for the logged-in player into `ns.db.alts` and session cache `Compat.CacheForeverFullName` whenever they speak in guild, party, raid, say, yell, or whisper.
- **Unified Modal Chrome & Styling (`UI/MainFrame.lua`):** Centralized `Theme:ApplyBackdrop` and button styling (`topCloseBtn`, `btnSave`, `btnCancel`) inside `EnsureModal()`, ensuring every modal dialog reliably reflects active theme palettes.
- **Live `Current Target` Compass Indicator & Slot Focus Highlighting (`UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):** Added a dynamic `[Current Target]` (`[Alvo Atual]` / `[Objetivo Actual]`) indicator on the Compass Hero Card when a spotted player is currently targeted by the player, replacing the generic `Click to Target` label. When no unit is targeted in game, clicking any Target Radar slot immediately pins and highlights it with the primary theme selection style, locking the compass arrow onto that player.
- **Guild Member Cache Isolation & Strict Stance Validation (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`):** Separated guild roster membership tracking (`guildMemberByGuid` / `guildMemberByFirst`) from the general full name cache (`foreverNameByGuid`). Added `Compat.CacheGuildMember()` and `Compat.ClearGuildMembers()` hooked into `ScanGuild()`, `SyncRosterForeverNames()`, and `PLAYER_GUILD_UPDATE`, ensuring non-guild friends or nearby players with cached surnames never produce false-positive `Same Clan` classifications.
- **Full Surname Resilient Target Selection (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):** Upgraded `Target Player` across all right-click context menus (Nearby, Social, Clan, and Group lists) to iterate through `Compat.GetForeverTargetVariants()` (space-separated full name, hyphenated name, and first name), guaranteeing that characters with WoW Forever surnames are acquired seamlessly without engine command failures.
- **Target Selection & Left-Click Instant Targeting (`UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`):** Implemented dual-layer target acquisition on Target Radar rows and Compass Hero Card: out-of-combat clicks trigger instant C-engine targeting via `TargetByName(firstName, true)` with full name fallback and instant slot selection highlighting, while in-combat clicks execute Blizzard-legal secure `/targetexact <firstName>` and `/target <firstName>` macros. Added `Target Player` (`Selecionar Alvo` / `Seleccionar Objetivo`) to the Target Radar right-click context menu, and wired chat alert links (`|Haddon:SCentral:target:...`) to immediately target the character upon clicking.
- **Mouseover Cursor Kinematics & Proximity Capture (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`):** Captured initial screen position and world heading via `GetCursorPosition()` at the exact moment of `UPDATE_MOUSEOVER_UNIT` for players sighted without friendly nameplates active (such as clanmates in major cities like Thunder Bluff), and immediately recorded yard/meter proximity tiers via `CheckInteractDistance(unit, 1/2/3)` so the compass arrow and distance badge are populated instantly without delay.
- **12-Second Off-Screen Retention & Automatic Window Closure (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`):** Increased off-screen target retention to 12 seconds (3 seconds for dead targets), allowing the compass arrow to continuously guide the player towards targets behind them or around obstacles. As soon as all targets leave range or are dismissed, the Target Radar window automatically disappears and resets its notification state for future sightings.
- **Streamlined Compass Hero Layout (`UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`):** Unified the Target Radar interface by eliminating redundant text banners beneath the Compass Hero Card, providing a clean, distraction-free view with the rotating ornate Warcraft compass arrow, real-time yard/meter distance, and class/stance badges.
- **Group Tab Member Targeting & Roster Full Name Caching (`UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `Providers/Group.lua`):** Added out-of-combat `Target Player` (`Selecionar Alvo` / `Seleccionar Objetivo`) to right-click context menus on group and recent companion member rows in the Group tab via `TargetUnit` or `TargetByName(displayName, true)`. Hooked party/raid roster scans directly into `Compat.CacheForeverFullName` so teammates' full names are cached into memory across all modules.
- **Nearby Sighting Full Surname Caching (`Providers/Nearby.lua`):** Hooked passive sightings with verified surnames into `Compat.CacheForeverFullName` so any player spotted nearby immediately expands the session-wide surname dictionary for Target Radar, Social, and chat.
- **Minimap Target Radar Awareness & Live Stance Counts (`UI/Minimap.lua`):** Added live Target Radar stance count summary (`E:X A:Y C:Z`) into the Minimap button hover tooltip when targets are spotted, appended active count badges to the right-click menu (`Target Radar (N)`), and hooked `"SPOTTER_UPDATED"` event so Minimap tooltip and badge states refresh instantaneously.
- **Unguilded Player Diplomacy & Radar Tracking (`Providers/Clan.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`):** Removed guild membership blocks from `Clan:LookupGuildStance()`, `Clan:GetDiplomacyList()`, and local `Clan:SetGuildStance()`, allowing leveling alts and unguilded characters to mark enemy/ally guilds in Diplomacy and detect them on the Target Radar via the local `_noguild` SavedVariables bucket.
- **Guild Search in Nearby Tab (`UI/MainFrame.lua`):** Added `item.guild` to `SafeTextMatches` in the Nearby tab so typing a guild name filters nearby sighted players by their guild.
- **Real-Time Combat Target Tracking & Dead Target Compass Dimming (`UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`):** Added `SpotterHUD:UpdateTargetVisuals()` that updates slot highlighting and instantly re-locks the Arcane Compass Hero Card onto the player's active target on `PLAYER_TARGET_CHANGED` even during `InCombatLockdown()` without touching protected secure attributes. When a spotted target is dead, the compass card displays `[Dead]` with dimmed arrow vertex colors (`0.55, 0.55, 0.55`), restoring full stance colors and bright auras immediately upon alive sightings.
- **Enhanced Interactive '?' Help Coverage in Settings (`UI/Settings.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):** Added dedicated interactive '?' help buttons for `[Open Target Radar]` and `[Reset All Window Positions]` in Settings (increasing project help coverage to 61 buttons) with 100% trilingual parity.
- **Top-Anchored Dynamic Window Dragging (`UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`):** Added `opts.anchor = "TOP"` support to `Theme:MakeMovable()` and `Theme:RestoreWindowPosition()` so dynamic-height list frames (such as the Target Radar HUD) save their screen offset relative to the top of `UIParent`. When spotted targets appear, leave range, or are filtered, the radar header remains rock-solid at its screen position while the list expands and contracts downwards without jumping or moving the header.
- **Immediate `Target Player` Context Action (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `Locales/Locale.lua`):** Added out-of-combat `Target Player` (`Selecionar Alvo` / `Seleccionar Objetivo`) to right-click context menus on Nearby and Social list rows via safe `TargetByName(targetName, true)`, with 100% trilingual parity.
- **Arcane Compass Arrow, Live Distance, Auto-Hide & Dynamic First-Time Dialog Prompt on Target Radar (`Media/arrow.tga`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`):** Created custom ornate Warcraft compass arrow artwork (`Media/arrow.tga`) matching SocialCentral's burnished gold/silver/arcane-cyan medallion palette, embedded in a smooth-rotating **Compass Hero Card** (`SocialCentralSpotterCompass`) inside Target Radar showing directional bearing (`Ahead`, `To your left`, `To your right`, `Behind` via lightweight `UnitPosition("player")` / `UnitPosition(unit)` world coordinates and live nameplate screen offsets) plus real-time yard/meter distance (`Distância` / `< 10m`, exact meters, or `> 28m`) both on the compass and on every target slot. Replaced cramped inline prompt bar with a dedicated floating dialog (`SocialCentralSpotterPromptDialog`) with dynamic fontstring anchoring, ESC key dismissal, and an in-settings `[Reset Radar Prompt]` button asking players once if they wish to keep Target Radar and screen notifications enabled (`[Manter Ativo]` / `[Desativar]`). Added automatic disappearance so Target Radar closes immediately as soon as all spotted targets leave range or are cleared.

### Fixed
- **Mathematical Correction for Arrow Kinematics & Heading Recovery (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`):** Fixed axis orientation in world coordinate calculations. `UnitPosition(unit)` in the World of Warcraft C-engine returns `y, x, z, instanceID` (where return 1 is North-South `y` and return 2 is East-West `x`). Swapped previous inverted assignments and aligned with WoW's native facing angle conventions (`dx = tx - px`, `dy = ty - py`, `worldAngle = math.atan2(dx, dy)`, `angle = NormalizeAngleRad(worldAngle - facing)`). Corrected off-screen target heading recovery to `NormalizeAngleRad(entry._lastWorldHeading - facing)` (eliminating rotational inversion when turning away from targets) and tuned nameplate horizontal screen offset scaling.
- **Zero-Delay Nameplate Detection & Active Polling (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`):** Added immediate proactive scanning of all visible nameplates (`ScanActiveNameplates`) on `PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD` (eliminating detection delays on login or `/reload`) and wired a 0.6-second lightweight nameplate polling loop in `_ticker`. If a player's guild name or surname arrives from the game server slightly after `NAME_PLATE_UNIT_ADDED`, they are immediately detected, classified, and displayed in the Target Radar without waiting for player selection or mouseover.
- **First-Name Full Surname Indexing & Instant Guild Affiliation (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `Providers/Nearby.lua`, `Providers/Group.lua`):** Added `foreverNameByFirst` dictionary and `Compat.IsGuildMember(firstName, guid)` in `Core/Compat.lua`. Dynamic return unpacking in `GetGuildRosterInfo` captures any player GUID format (`^Player%-` or `^0x`) regardless of client return counts, proactively caching all guild roster and friend members' full names. When `GetGuildInfo(unit)` temporarily returns empty, `Compat.IsGuildMember` immediately recognizes guildmates (such as `Ann Druyan`) as `Same Clan`, displaying their full surname and guild tag without delay. Preserved `existingName` in `Providers/Nearby.lua` and `Providers/Group.lua`.
- **Pre-Created Radar First-Time Dialog (`UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`):** Pre-created `SocialCentralSpotterPromptDialog` during addon `Init()` so all frame setup, backdrop, and movable hooks are initialized cleanly outside of combat lockdown.
- **Guild-Isolated Clan Management & Diplomacy Registry (`Providers/Clan.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`):** Added shared Clan Management (`Clan Info` modal with focus/summary, core hours/schedule, rules, recruitment contacts, and configurable Officers-only vs. All-Members edit policy) plus a full **Guild Diplomacy Registry** (`Diplomacy` subview & `+ Add Guild` modal with `+ Target Guild` quick-fill) allowing members to mark external guilds as **Ally**, **Enemy**, or **Neutral** with notes. Zero cross-clan leakage is enforced across four layers: guild-scoped SavedVariables bucket (`ScopeKey` + `guildName`), strict `"GUILD"` addon-channel transport, per-packet deterministic `ComputeGuildScopeToken()` + `IsSenderConfirmedInGuild()` validation, and immediate session wipe on `PLAYER_GUILD_UPDATE`.
- **On-Screen Clan Spotter & Secure Target Radar HUD (`Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`):** Detects players appearing on screen (`NAME_PLATE_UNIT_ADDED/REMOVED`, `UNIT_NAME_UPDATE`, `UPDATE_MOUSEOVER_UNIT`, `PLAYER_TARGET_CHANGED`, `PLAYER_FOCUS_CHANGED`), classifies them against your active guild (`Same Clan`) and Diplomacy registry (`Ally Clan` / `Enemy Clan`), attaches lightweight stance tags above readable non-forbidden nameplates, triggers debounced chat + banner + optional sound notifications, and populates a floating clickable **Target Selection Area HUD** using Blizzard-legal `SecureActionButtonTemplate` `/targetexact` macro rows with full `InCombatLockdown()` queuing and right-click quick actions.
- **LibWindow-Style Universal Window Dragging (`UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`):** Implemented `Theme:MakeMovable()`, `Theme:AttachDragHandle()`, and `Theme:ResetAllWindowPositions()` storing center-normalized `(dx, dy)` screen coordinates in `ns.db.windowPositions` (`SetDontSavePosition(true)`, `SetUserPlaced(false)`, `SetClampedToScreen(true)`, child header/chrome drag forwarding, `OnHide` mid-drag cancellation, combat-lock guard for secure frames). Every frame and dialog across SocialCentral (Main Window, Target Radar HUD, Help/Update/Reload/Theme Welcome popups, and all MainFrame/SocialPanels modals) can now be freely dragged anywhere on screen, with quick reset via `/sc resetpos`, Minimap menu, or `Settings -> Screen Clan Radar & Target Selection`.
- **Verified In-Addon 1-on-1 Direct Chat (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`):** Implemented direct 1-on-1 messaging (`DM`, `DMA` delivery receipt, `DPIN` location sharing) and active peer verification (`DPING`/`DPONG` handshake + automatic Clan/Room/Addon session detection) inside SocialCentral whenever both players actively run the addon. Added `+ Chat` quick conversation launcher, green `[SC]` verification badges across lists, peer status banner with `[Verify Addon]` button, `+ Location` pin sharing, and `Settings -> Enable Verified In-Addon 1-on-1 Direct Chat` (`addonDirectChat`). All addon messaging respects combat lockdown, `IsMessagingLocked()`, cooldowns (`DIRECT_SEND_COOLDOWN`, `PING_COOLDOWN`), and `<=240B` payload caps.
- **Pre-Addressed Official Chat Destination Fallback (`GetOfficialDestinationInfo` / `OpenOfficialChatForThread`):** When a selected conversation is a protected WoW channel (`Party`, `Raid`, `Instance`, `Guild`, `Officer`) or a peer without active SocialCentral, the addon hides its composer input and renders a single configured destination button (e.g., `Send via WoW Whisper (/w Name)`, `Send via Battle.net`, `Open Guild Chat (/g)`) that opens WoW's official chat input pre-addressed to that destination.
- **Expanded Clan Events, In-Clan Group Finder (`Clan LFG`) & Opt-in Dungeon LFG Alerts (`Providers/Clan.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`):** Added `Clan LFG` subtab & `+ Clan LFG` composer (LFG/LFM modes, Role, Dungeon/Keystone level, iLvl, `[Share Queue/Key]` auto-populate from Blizzard Keystone/LFD/LFGList, optional `/g` draft, merged with Dungeon events and `/g` sightings), opt-in `Clan Dungeon LFG Notifications` (`clanLfgAlertEnabled`, Dungeon-only toggle, comma-separated keyword filter, optional sound, clickable chat `[Open in SocialCentral Chat]` / `[Whisper]` links), expanded Event Call dialog with Role selector + Blizzard Dungeon/Raid presets + Officer **WoW Guild Calendar** publishing (`PublishGameCalendarEvent`), and unified live Guild Hub card (Guild MOTD viewer + Officer MOTD editor, active Keystone / Spec / iLvl / LFD / Premade queue status, 14-day native WoW Calendar view, Saved Lockouts viewer, full online Guild Roster with Rank & Zone, and safe out-of-combat `[Group Finder]` / `[WoW Calendar]` shortcuts).
- **Blizzard Official Default Theme & First-Run Visual Theme Picker (`UI/Theme.lua`):** Set **Blizzard Official** (`blizzard`) as the default SocialCentral theme with classic `ButtonFrameTemplate` visual parity (protruding circular Portrait medallion, burnished gold title, square crimson `X` close button, dark smoked `UI-Background-Marble` inset well, crimson CTAs, pewter metallic buttons) and **Modern Theme** (`dark`) as an optional alternative, with a first-run dual-card selector (`Theme:ShowThemeWelcomePicker`).
- **Granular Chat Sound Controls, Per-Conversation Mute & Manual Language Override (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/Minimap.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`):** Independent sound toggles for Whispers/BNet, Addon Private Rooms, Guild/Officer (silent by default), and Party/Raid/Instance (silent by default), per-thread `Mute / Unmute`, opt-in WoW channel mirroring (`chatMirrorChannels = false`, `chatChannelUnread = false`), explicit Language selector (`Auto (WoW)` / `English` / `Português (BR)` / `Español`) with `Reload UI` prompt, and upgraded Minimap hover unread badge & quick menu.

### Fixed
- **Target Radar Left-Click Targeting, Party Invite & Zero-Lag Arrow Kinematics on WoW Forever `16001` (`UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`):** Replaced heavy frame-by-frame `C_Map` queries with direct C-engine `UnitPosition("player")` and `UnitPosition(unit)` coordinates (matching DBM-Arrow and TomTom), cached `entry.unitToken` across nameplates, throttled slot distance updates to 4 FPS, and set `type1 = "target"` with `unit = entry.unitToken` on `SecureActionButtonTemplate` slots, compass hero card, and banner for 100% native C-level instant targeting on nameplates, with `/target [nodead] <Name>` macro fallback, registering `"LeftButtonUp", "RightButtonUp"` only (eliminating double-fire deselects). Guarded `ApplySecureMacroAttributes` against `InCombatLockdown()`, added an `Invite` party action in the Radar right-click context menu for friendly targets, and removed `f.lockBtn` header lock restriction to make the entire header, frame, and list area effortless drag handles with `Theme:AttachDragHandle`.
- **Blizzard Official Top-Right Close Button & ASCII `'X'` Glyphs on WoW Forever (`UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`):** Replaced `UIPanelCloseButton` on `SocialCentralFrame` (whose inherited XML normal texture renders blank in WoW Forever `16001` despite returning a non-nil texture region) with an explicit high-level crimson `Theme:CreateButton(..., 22, 22, "X", "danger")` close button anchored cleanly inside the top-right Blizzard corner slot (`baseLevel + 40`), and replaced `"×"` (`U+00D7`) with crisp ASCII `"X"` across main, modal, Watchlist, Clan Event, Theme Picker, and Target Radar close/remove buttons.
- **Main Window Dragging & Position Saving on WoW Forever (`UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`):** Replaced `SetScript("OnShow", ...)` and `SetScript("OnHide", ...)` on `SocialCentralFrame` with `HookScript(...)` so `Theme:MakeMovable`'s position restore (`RestoreWindowPosition`) and mid-drag cleanup (`EndDrag`) are never overwritten. Added an explicit clickable titlebar drag region (`frame.blizzTitleDrag`) across `ButtonFrameTemplate`'s header, added `OnMouseUp` safety-net handlers on movable frames and drag handles so releasing the mouse never leaves dragging stuck, and wired resize grip completion to `frame._scSavePosition()`.
- **Pre-Send Addon Verification Before Official Chat Fallback (`Providers/Chat.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`):** Added `Chat:VerifyBeforeOfficialSend(threadKey)` and `Chat:ShouldAutoProbe(threadKey)` so clicking the chat destination button (`btnOfficialDest`) or reopening stale conversations sends an addon `DPING` verification check first, switches straight into in-addon direct chat (focusing `replyInput` with confirmation print) if `DPONG` arrives within 1.5s, and falls back to `/w` / Battle.net official chat if there is no response (with instant click-again bypass). Fixed `ClanSpotterHUD` `"Whisper in SocialCentral"` context menu passing `targetName` and opening the `chat` tab.
- **Proactive `FirstName Surname` Roster Sync, Title Filter & Non-Degrading Surnames (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Social.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`):** Added `Compat.SyncRosterForeverNames()` that proactively caches all guild and friend roster members' GUID -> `FirstName Surname` into memory on `PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD`, `GUILD_ROSTER_UPDATE`, and `FRIENDLIST_UPDATE` so spotted players resolve their Forever surnames with zero delay. Added `TITLE_PARTICLES` filtering in `ExtractForeverNameFromLine` so Blizzard title articles (e.g., "the" in "Ann the Patient" or "of" in "Valkur of Orgrimmar") are never misparsed as surnames, added `UnitPVPName` direct C-call extraction, and ensured `ClanSpotter:InspectUnit` preserves already-resolved surnames across nameplate ticks so they are never lost or downgraded. Wired `autoProbe = true` to Spotter HUD context menus and Social list rows so peer addon presence is verified seamlessly before official chat fallbacks.
- **Full `FirstName Surname` Resolution Across Spotter/Units & Click-to-Target Notifications (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Providers/ClanSpotter.lua`, `UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`, `Providers/Nearby.lua`, `Providers/Group.lua`, `Providers/Clan.lua`, `Core/Init.lua`):** Added `Compat.ResolveUnitFullName()` using `C_TooltipInfo.GetUnit` / `C_TooltipInfo.GetHyperlink` (with hidden `GameTooltip` fallback), `GetUnitName`, Guild Roster / Friends GUID matching, and a session GUID surname cache (`Compat.CacheForeverFullName`) so units spotted on screen, mouseover, target, nearby, and group always resolve and display their full WoW Forever `FirstName Surname` (e.g., `Ann Druyan` instead of only `Ann`), upgrading space-separated Forever formatting across `Compat.ParseCharacterName()`. Upgraded `SocialCentralSpotterBanner` (`UI/ClanSpotterHUD.lua`) into a clickable `SecureActionButtonTemplate` `/targetexact` banner, updated `BuildTargetMacroText` and `isCurrentTarget` GUID matching for two-word Forever names, and made player names in Spotter chat notifications clickable hyperlinks (`|Haddon:SCentral:target:...`) that open/pin the target at the top of Target Radar.
- **False Combat Lock on Addon Verification & Forever Whisper Target Delivery (`Providers/Clan.lua`, `Providers/Chat.lua`, `Core/Compat.lua`):** Removed `C_ChatInfo.AreOutgoingAddonChatMessagesRestricted()` from `Clan:IsMessagingLocked()`, which returned false positives on WoW Forever (`16001`) out of combat and made `Verify Addon` / Direct Chat / Room messaging claim `"Messaging is temporarily locked during combat/encounter."` while not in combat. Separated real player combat (`InCombatLockdown()` / `UnitAffectingCombat("player")`) from `C_ChatInfo.InChatMessagingLockdown()` in `GuardAddonMessaging`, updated `SendRoomPacket` & `Compat.Whisper`/`Compat.Invite` to try WoW Forever space-separated `"FirstName Surname"` targets before hyphenated/first-name fallbacks, and reconciled short-name `DPONG` replies against open `"FirstName Surname"` whisper threads.
- **Blizzard Official Window Close Button on WoW Forever `16001` (`UI/MainFrame.lua`):** Replaced reliance on implicit `ButtonFrameTemplate.CloseButton` (which does not reliably appear in WoW Forever `16001`) with an explicit `UIPanelCloseButton` (`SocialCentralBlizzCloseButton`, with styled `×` fallback) parented directly to `SocialCentralFrame` at elevated `FrameLevel`, adjusted header right clearance so `Settings` never crowds the top-right `X`, and added a top-right `×` close button to shared input modals (`EnsureModal`).
- **WoW Forever Full `FirstName Surname` & `Alts` Clarity (`Core/Compat.lua`, `Core/Store.lua`, `Providers/Alts.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`):** Added `Compat.ParseForeverFullName` and `Compat.ResolvePlayerFullName` plus passive self-GUID resolution across `GUILD_ROSTER_UPDATE`, `FRIENDLIST_UPDATE`, and self chat author lines so the active character and saved Alts display their complete Forever `FirstName Surname` (e.g., `Valkur Maul`) instead of only the first name. Marked the active character row clearly with `[Current Character]` vs `[Alt]`, pinned the active character first in the list, added a manual `Set Surname` toolbar button + right-click row menu (`Set Forever Surname` / `Remove Alt`), and documented permanent WoW Forever (`Interface: 16001`) exclusivity in `AGENTS.md`.
- **Permanent Tab Strip & Responsive Input/Layout Overhaul (`UI/Theme.lua`, `UI/MainFrame.lua`, `UI/SocialPanels.lua`, `UI/Settings.lua`, `scripts/verify-compliance.py`):** Eliminated Blizzard Official tab text overflowing across adjacent tab borders by replacing unconstrained `PanelTopTabButtonTemplate` + `PanelTemplates_TabResize` with bounded, clipped (`SetClipsChildren`) classic-styled tab frames, clearing the circular portrait above `ButtonFrameTemplate.Inset`, adding `ReflowTabStrip`, `FlowButtonGroup` wrapping, fluid `Theme:CreateEditBox` / `Theme:StyleSearchBox` widgets, and dynamic width scaling across EN / PT-BR / ES.

## [0.2.2] - 2026-11-12
### Added
- **Conversations Hub & Private Chat Rooms (`Providers/Chat.lua` & `Chat` tab):** Unified thread list for `WHISPER`, `BN_WHISPER`, `PARTY`/`RAID`/`INSTANCE_CHAT`, `GUILD`/`OFFICER`, and **Addon Private Chat Rooms** (`SCentral1` `RMI`/`RMJ`/`RML`/`RM` + `C_BattleNet.SendGameData`), unread counters, configurable audio chime (`chatSound`), local character-whisper history (`chatHistoryLimit`, zero BattleTag/Real ID saved), safe chat-box prefill for native channels, and manual `Temp Channel` (`/join` + `/leave` helper).
- **Instance / Group Hub & Recent Companions (`Providers/Group.lua` & `Group` tab):** Live `PARTY`/`RAID`/`INSTANCE_CHAT` roster with Class, Level, Tank/Healer/DPS roles, Leader & Ready Check tracking, hardware-gated Ready Check & Promote controls, safe `/p` / `/ra` / `/i` status announcer, and 7-day `Recent Companions` log.
- **Clan LFG Finder, Guild Professions & Auto-Status:** Role slots (`Need T / H / D`) and `Min Lv` on Clan Event Calls, Open/Closed toggle (`EVS`), Reannounce, 1-click leader applicant invite, LFG filter chips (`Open Only`, `Need T`, `Need H`), Guild Professions directory (`PRF`, 13 professions + Craft Note), opt-in read-only `/g` LFM detector (`guildChatFinder`), and opt-in `autoStatusInstance`.
- **Social Folders, Main Linking & Social Cards (`socialMeta`):** Organize players into custom folders (`M+ Team`, `Guild Core`, etc.), link alts to a Main character, and attach persistent notes with a Folder filter in the `Social` tab.
- **Slash Shortcuts, Minimap Integration & Complete Trilingual/Help Coverage:** Added `/sc chat`, `/sc group`, `/sc clan`, Minimap unread/group tooltips & tab shortcuts, 4 new Settings controls with `?` help buttons, and 100% EN/ptBR/esES parity.

## [0.2.1] - 2026-11-12
### Added
- **Official AddOn Icon (`Media\icon.tga` & `Assets\SocialCentral-logo.png`):** Custom stylized Warcraft social medallion (`128x128` 32-bit RGBA TGA) wired into `## IconTexture`, Addon Compartment, LibDBIcon/Minimap button, and the Main Window title header.
- **Second-Decimal Versioning Rule:** Persisted mandatory `PATCH` (`0.X.(Y+1)`) bumping policy in `AGENTS.md` and distribution scripts.

## [0.2.0] - 2026-11-12
### Added
- **Clan Hub (Guild Interactions):** Shared Clan status tags (`LFG`, `M+`, `Raid`, `PvP`, `Leveling`, `Crafting`, `Busy`), Guild Event Calls creation, and Dungeon/Raid role signups (`Tank`, `Healer`, `DPS`, `Maybe`, `Decline`) synced over `SCentral1` strictly under opt-in (`clanSync`), rate limits (`>= 5s`), and combat/messaging lockdown guards.
- **Standardized `?` Help Buttons:** Added interactive `?` help buttons (`What it does` + `How to use` on hover tooltip and click popup) next to every option in Settings and every interactive feature bar in the main window.
- **One-Click Manual `/who` Check (`+ /who` & `/sc check <Name>`):** Type a character name manually and run a single hardware-event-compliant `/who` lookup cleanly without leaving Combat/Cooldown safety rules.
- **DBM-Style In-Game Version Detection & Public GitHub Updater:** Passive rate-limited peer version announcement/detection (`SCentral1` `VH`/`VR`, configurable in Settings) with localized chat notification + clickable hyperlink opening a `Ctrl+C` modal for `https://github.com/raphaelredes/SocialCentral-Releases/releases/latest`, paired with Windows updater script `scripts/update-socialcentral.ps1`.
- **Enforced Trilingual Parity:** Automated compliance check validating 100% translation parity across English (`enUS`/`enGB`), Portuguese (`ptBR`/`ptPT`), and Spanish (`esES`/`esMX`) synced with WoW game language settings.

## [0.1.0] - 2026-11-12
### Added
- Unified Watchlist (`/who` hardware-event compliant with silent UI / chat filter modes), Live Social Roster (Guild, Character Friends, Battle.net), Friendly-only Nearby Sightings, Account Alts tracker with Total Gold, LibDBIcon-grade Minimap button, and fail-closed Blizzard safety verification gate.
